{{Quote|Welcome to Baldi's Basics in Education and Learning! That's me!|Baldi|in the title screen of ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Classic Remastered''|sound=BAL_MenuIntro.wav}}
'''Baldi''', whose full name is '''Baldi Baldimore''', is the titular main antagonist of the ''[[Baldi's Basics]]'' series, hence his name, and the titular main protagonist of the ''[[Baldimore]]'' comic series. He is the head teacher of both the [[Schoolhouse]] and the [[Super Schoolhouse]], giving the player [[math|mathematical]] problems to solve in the [[You Can Think Pad]] or [[Educational activity|one of his several educational activities]] before they can collect [[notebook]]s. 

== Description ==
Baldi is a poorly-modeled CGI character in Anim8or with a light skin complexion, long skinny fingers, red lips, eyebrows and a single hair strand on his head which appears to be colored ginger/orange. He wears a long-sleeved green shirt, blue jeans, and light brown shoes. His left elbow is blue in ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]'', and gray in ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'' and ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash|Birthday Bash]]''. Although he has no visible ears, his poster mentions that he has an excellent sense of hearing.

Baldi's facial expression while chasing the player is neutral, not angry, though it is heavily compressed and distorted. This is most likely due to [[Wikipedia:Dither|dithering]].

== Personality ==
=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash'' ===
Baldi is a math-loving teacher<ref>"''Baldi sure loves numbers. I bet he'd love it if everything could be represented by a number..<br>(This message was scheduled and may not accurately reflect progress/lack thereof)''" - mystman12. April 26, 2024. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1783707323262709784 X/Twitter]</ref> seems friendly towards the player at first glance, encouraging them to continue by giving them a shiny [[Quarter]] or a [[present]]. However, upon failing a question, Baldi indirectly threatens the player and starts chasing them, showing an aggressive side to his personality. He slaps his ruler against his hand repeatedly, slowly but surely approaching the player. He gets angrier with each incorrectly solved math problem, causing him to speed up. In [[Endless Mode]], getting all three answers correct after angering Baldi will slightly appease him, causing him to slow down. In [[Story Mode]], collecting all the seven notebooks will cause him to congratulate the player, before informing them they have to get out of this place as fast as possible and laugh at their escape efforts.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' ===
In ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', Baldi seems like a fun-loving teacher. Baldi encourages the player to input their name on a [[Save system|blank]], and eagerly teaches them [[Tutorial|how to play]] the game. Baldi's primary goal is to play a game of [[Hide-and-Seek]] with the player, presumably both for fun and to teach them math. At the start of each [[floor]], Baldi counts down from 10 before taking his ruler out and searching for the player. When the player has collected all the notebooks on a level, Baldi congratulates the player on obtaining them, before informing them they need to find a way out before he catches you. After the player finishes the [[Floor 2]], Baldi waits outside the [[Pit Stop]], wanting to take the player or [[Johnny]] on [[camping]] trip, if the player has a [[Bus Pass]] to give him. He will not appear in later Pit Stops, even if they have that item. Every next floor is harder to beat than the previous. After finishing [[Floor 5]], the player will meet Baldi once again in [[Baldi's Office|his office]] where he congratulates them for beating his game.

However, he is shown to be extremely negligent in maintaining the Super Schoolhouse. As seen in certain [[event]]s, he has malfunctioning [[Balder Dash|boulder traps]] and [[Fog|fog machines]], [[Flood|flooding with a chance of whirlpools]], [[Student Shuffle|unaccompanied crowds of students]] that can push one far away from their destination, etc. He also has dangerous [[structure]]s that could severly injure someone: [[crane]]s that carry one by grabbing on to their head, small [[Vent|ventilation]] ducts that can pull one in and make them crash loudly into the duct walls due to the air, and heavy [[super door]]s that could potentially kill someone if they were to stand under it.

There have been multiple hints at that Baldi cannot properly write or spell, such as "Bald's Bus" and the abandoned spelling room in [[Baldi's Basement]]. There are other pieces of text in the game, such as posters, that also have a lot of typos and errors. However, it is unknown whether Baldi wrote these or not.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'' ===
''It's generally shown that Baldi likes to invent some inventions in his spare time. For all his known inventions, see [[Baldi/Inventions|here]].''

In ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'', Baldi is very similar to how he is portrayed in ''Plus'', although he does show some traits closer to ''Classic''. In [[Classic Style]] and [[Party Style]], Baldi acts as he does in ''Classic'', giving the player an [[item]] if the player answers correct and chasing the player in anger if they don't. Although, he still directly praises them for correct answers in [[Demo Style]], indicating he isn't as enraged as it is in ''Plus''. This is also indicated by Baldi playfully telling them to find a way out before he catch the player when getting all seven notebooks.

This is also shown by Baldi being very kind towards the player in the secret [[ending]]s including him, explaining negative numbers to the player, playing a [[Baldi's Funtime Song|special song]] for them, and allowing them to help him test out his new [[shrink machine]] in Classic Style, Party Style, and Demo Style respectively.

=== ''Baldimore'' ===
Baldi is relatively the same, though at times he can be crazy or arrogant, sometimes to the point of violence, like it was in one of the comics, ''[[Baldiman]]'', where he threatens to cut off a kid's toes. He is quite clever, being able to invent things, much like how he does in ''Plus'' or ''Classic Remastered,'' and even break the fourth wall in efficient ways. But sometimes, he can be oblivious to his surroundings, like when he accidentally ate [[Comic-exclusive character#Susan|his wife]]'s hairbrush, thinking it is a chicken leg, or when a baseball was thrown at him by [[Comic-exclusive character#TimTom|TimTom]], his possible nemesis. He is also implied to have a big appetite for food according to the deleted BaldiComic X/Twitter account's description.

According to the [[Hair Brush]] comic, Baldi may either have problems with his perception of reality.

== Artificial intelligence ==
=== Main gameplay ===
==== ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' ====
[[File:Baldi says hello.png|thumb|240x240px|Baldi greeting [[the player]] to the school while asking to play a game of hide and seek.]]
At the beginning of a floor, Baldi happily greets and welcomes the player to his Super Schoolhouse while inviting them to a game of hide-and-seek.

Baldi's greeting lines depend on the [[floor]]:<!--These are the official captions. Do not change them.-->
* [[Floor 1]] and [[Endless Mode]]: "''Oh, hi! Let's play [[Hide-and-Seek|hide and seek]]!''"
* [[Floor 2]]: "''You're good! Let's keep playing!''"
* [[Floor 3]] onwards: "''What fun! Goo-''"
<gallery position="center">
Baldi counts down.png|Baldi counting down from 10.
Baldi caught red-handed.png|Baldi's rare sprite of peeking at the player while counting.
Baldi disappears in Explorer Mode.gif|Baldi disappearing in the Explorer Play Style.
</gallery>
After the player moves at least two [[tile]]s away from the starting elevator area, Baldi will cover his eyes and begin to count down from 10. During the countdown, there is a slight chance that Baldi will uncover his right eye and peek at the player. This occurrence does not affect the gameplay. Once the countdown is over, Baldi will smile for a while before grabbing his ruler and chasing the player. Strangely, he will also get angry. However, if the [[Play Style]] is set to "[[Explorer]]", Baldi will simply disappear after finishing his countdown, allowing the player to roam the school freely.

[[File:Baldi's scary slapping!.png|thumb|240x240px|Baldi chasing the player while he slaps his ruler.]]
Baldi is the main threat in both the Classic Schoolhouse and [[Super Schoolhouse]]. He is the only character capable of causing a [[game over]]. Unlike other characters, Baldi moves in a unique way. He will move a short distance each time he slaps his ruler. This allows him to cover ground quickly and catch up to the player if they are not careful. Baldi also has an excellent sense of hearing; if the player makes a sound, he will instantly head for the source of the sound. However, if it has lower priority than the sound he is currently investigating, or if the player is in line of sight, he will not go to that sound. He also completely wipes his sound "storage", and heads where he last saw the player. As quoted in his poster in the [[Principal's office]], he specifically knows who makes a sound. This means that it is important to be quiet when trying to avoid Baldi, by waiting for another character to open a door so the player can enter a room without making a noise by themselves, using [[WD-NoSquee]], or other methods. The [[Baldicator]] will appear at the bottom right of the screen each time Baldi hears a sound. This head indicates whether Baldi is coming to the location where the sound was made. But technically, Baldi does not necessarily know where the player is, only relying on sound and sight, so if the player does not open doors or does not get in Baldi's sight, he will just wander around until he finds the player.

If Baldi fails to see or hear the player for two [[hallway]] turns, he will begin wandering around the Schoolhouse. Baldi can see the player through any set of yellow swinging doors, but cannot if they are behind a window; however, he can see them if the window has been shattered.

Baldi's interval between slaps will decrease for every notebook the player collects, as well as every incorrectly solved math problem. If Baldi's anger value exceeds <math>N - 1</math>, where <math>N</math> is the number of notebooks on the current floor, the value will slowly decrease until it no longer exceeds <math>N - 1</math>. This is called "temporary anger" in the files of ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash''.

[[File:Baldi's jumpscare.png|thumb|240x240px|Baldi's jumpscare for when he catches the player.]]
If caught, the camera will be automatically turned to face Baldi while a random loud buzzing noise plays. After this occurs, the player will be sent back to the starting elevator, with one less [[power tube]]. If the player is caught with only one [[power tube]] remaining, they will be sent back to the title screen.

{{SpoilerHide|
If the player [[Ending|completes]] all the floors in Hide-and-Seek, they will meet Baldi in [[Baldi's Office|his office]] where he will sing them a song while dancing. Once the song ends, random portions of its lyrics will play at rapid intervals, overlapping each other. The game will then 'pseudo-corrupt' into a distorted and randomly colored {{C|[[BadSum]]}} screen.
}}

====''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash|Birthday Bash]]''====
{{See also|Story Mode}}
[[File:ClassicMobile-GooglePlay1.png|thumb|Baldi at the beginning of ''Baldi's Basics Classic, on mobile.'']]Before obtaining the first notebook in one of the beginning classrooms, the player needs to solve three math problems using the You Can Think Pad. During this time, Baldi gives instructions and reads out the questions. If all three questions are answered correctly, Baldi will give the player a Quarter, but will give a [[present]] in ''Birthday Bash''. To get the second notebook, the player will have to answer three more math problems, but after solving the second problem, the third question is corrupted and blocked by other random equations. When Baldi starts reading out the question, the numbers he says are censored with static noises.

[[File:Angry Baldi in the YCTP.jpeg|thumb|Baldi getting angry when the player answers a question incorrectly for the first time in ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash''.]]When the player gets their first problem wrong in the You Can Think Pad, Baldi's face will become distorted, making him appear to be frowning intensely, and his eyebrows will furrow. He either says "'''{{Font|I HEAR EVERY DOOR YOU OPEN}}'''" or "'''{{Font|I GET ANGRIER FOR EVERY PROBLEM YOU GET WRONG}}'''". If the player gets all 3 questions wrong, it will display "'''{{Font|I HEAR MATH THAT BAD}}'''" instead. When that happens, Baldi gets alerted of where the notebook was, and goes directly toward it. After the player fails a question, Baldi disappears on the bottom left-hand corner of the You Can Think Pad for the rest of the notebooks. Once the player exits the You Can Think Pad after getting their first problem wrong, Baldi begins to chase the player through the Schoolhouse, signaling his movement and location by slapping a ruler on his hand.

[[File:Baldi's jumpscare Classic.png|thumb|Baldi's jumpscare for when he catches the player in ''Baldi's Basics Classic''.]]
Baldi puts 3 math problems in every notebook the player must answer correctly. If the player gets an answer wrong, Baldi's slap interval becomes shorter and shorter, thus making him move faster and faster. Every notebook (except the first one) will always end with an unsolvable problem, making Baldi's slap intervals shorter per notebook collected. It is worthy to note that Baldi (and all of the other characters) will stop moving while the player is using the You Can Think Pad.<ref>"''Yeah, Baldi doe-Baldi stops moving when you're in the You Can Think Pad, so... it's uh, not somethin-speed isn't something you have to worry too much about.''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|38:36|Livestream archive 38:36-38:45}}</ref> Unlike in ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' and ''Classic Remastered'', HUDs will disappear when Baldi catches the player.

Once the player has four notebooks, Baldi will be moving at the same speed as the player's walking speed, so the only way to outrun him is by running. After the player collects six notebooks, Baldi will start moving at the same speed as the player's running speed. However, once the player collects the 7th notebook, Baldi moves so fast that he can catch them even if they are sprinting. The only way to get away from him is by using a BSODA or getting pushed by [[1st Prize]] or Gotta Sweep, but with the possibility of ramming into Baldi.

It is worth noting that Baldi's behavior in ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash'' is practically the same as ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' and ''Classic Remastered'', though recoded and altered slightly in the latter games, so no need to cover it here.

====''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered]]''====
Baldi has similar behavior to ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', but in [[Demo Style]], he has some minor changes:

Baldi, who is still greeting the player, puts seven Math Machines across the Schoolhouse's classrooms. Each of the Math Machines has only one question, and three questions with the [[Hard Mode]] [[Fun Setting]] enabled, where the player will have to put in the right number balloon into it, which is slowly floating in a classroom. No matter if the player got the question right or not, a notebook would pop out. If the player answers it correctly, Baldi will become passive for 5 seconds and congratulate the player, then return to his angry state again. When answering a problem wrong with the Hard Mode Fun Setting, it will automatically skip every other question and give the player the notebook; however, it will also vastly increase Baldi's anger (or temporary anger if it goes higher than 6). Just like the other two styles, the second Math Machine contains an unsolvable problem: as such, the numbers, operations and question marks glitch. Baldi will of course become angry. Everything else stays the same.

Also, unlike in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'', Baldi's speed after collecting all 7 notebooks is slower than the player's running speed.

{{SpoilerHide|He also appears in all secret endings if the NULL style has been beaten. However, he will not harm the player and is friendly.}}

=== Field trips ===
==== ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' ====
[[File:Welcome to the campsite.png|thumb|Baldi welcoming the player to the campsite in ''Baldi's Basics Plus''.]]
Baldi will appear in all field trips and will tell the player how the field trip minigames work. If the player throws bad fuel into the [[campfire]] or misses good one in [[Campfire Frenzy]], or gets a strike in [[Pick-Quick-Nic]] (except for the player not choosing the correct food in time), Baldi will be disappointed and say "''Ohhhh...''". Prior to [[V0.6 (Plus)|V0.6]], Baldi would be disappointed when a star is lost and he would guide the player vocally, and prior to [[V0.3 (Plus)|V0.3]], he did not guide the player at all in the [[camping]] or farm field trip and would chase them with his ruler if they failed the objective.
* In the camping field trip, Baldi appears wearing a camping suit, hat and a backpack.
** In the current version, the player can either help Baldi fuel the fire in [[Campfire Frenzy]] or they can eat with him in [[Pick-Quick-Nic]]. If the player puts the wrong fuel in the fire or if they pick the food that he was thinking of, Baldi will get disappointed and bend his body down.
** Prior to V0.6, the player's task was to place the campfires in the right positions of where groups of firewood will land, based on the math questions and answers. If the player placed at least one campfire in the wrong position, Baldi will get disappointed and bend his body down.
** Prior to V0.3, the player's task was to collect [[log]]s and use them to refuel the campfire. If the player fails to keep the fire going until the [[timer]] runs out, an angry Baldi will spawn in the burnt-out logs, and begin to chase them. He will move extremely quickly, with his anger set to either 7 or 8 and will be unable to escape under practically every circumstance, with nothing being able to slow him down as the forest lacks any useful items. The player loses all of their items upon losing the field trip.
* In the [[farm]] field trip, prior to V0.4, Baldi was seen wearing a striped sweater, a blue apron in a ranch outfit, a farmer's hat on his head, and wheat-like grass in his mouth.
** From V0.3.2 to V0.3.8, Baldi was only seen when guiding the player. However, he would still make the disappointing sound when the player inserted the incorrect animal to the barn, or when the timer ran out when there was at least one required animal.
** Prior to V0.3, Baldi would only spawn to chase the player if they failed to reach the flagpole after a long time had surpassed.

=== [[Challenge]]s ===
In each challenge, Baldi will not greet the player. Instead, he will try to catch them once the elevator closes.
* In the [[Speedy Challenge]], Baldi, along with the player, will move extremely fast.
* In the [[Stealthy Challenge]], Baldi gets locked in the Principal's office, so if the player gets into detention, they will almost instantly get caught by him. Baldi's base speed is also much faster than in the main mode. Trying to use a [[Dirty Chalk Eraser]] in the Principal's office will barely work, as Baldi wanders around the Principal's office in fast speed, meaning that he will catch them, unless the player gets far away from him and use Dirty Chalk Erasers quickly while moving to the corner.
* In the [[Grapple Challenge]], Baldi's speed has been roughly doubled (except when none of the notebooks are collected) to counter the speed of the [[Grappling Hook]].

=== [[Tutorial]] ===
[[File:Balditorial.png|thumb|Baldi, in the final section of the Tutorial.]]
While Baldi is mostly inactive throughout the tutorial, only teaching the player some game mechanics by speaking, at the final section, Baldi will count down for 10 and start seeking the player, similarly to how he does it in the main gameplay.

However, rather than using his ruler, he will start moving as a standard character, without his normal behavior. He will still go after noises that are caused by objects using the noise value and stop to praise the player when a math problem is answered correctly on one of Baldi's Math Machines.

Upon catching the player, Baldi will stop completely, letting the player go.

=== Other appearances ===
* ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' marks Baldi's first appearance (excluding non-''Baldi's Basics'' media), with a role similar to his appearance in ''Baldi's Basics Plus''. Getting caught by Baldi will directly send the player back to the [[Screen|main menu]].
{{SpoilerHide|[[File:BaldiOffice Classic.png|thumb|Stretched-out Baldi in the alternate ending of ''Baldi's Basics Classic''.]] In the alternate ending where the player meets [[Null]], Baldi will be seen stretched-out and distorted inside his office.}}
* [[File:BirthdayBash2.png|thumb|Baldi in ''Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash''.]][[File:SURPRISE.jpg|thumb|Baldi appearing with the original cast for a birthday surprise.]]In ''Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash'', Baldi remains the same as in ''Classic'', but wears a party hat (which jumps a bit each time he slaps his ruler in his hostile state) for the celebration of ''Baldi's Basics Classic''<nowiki>'</nowiki>s first anniversary. As soon as the player reaches the last exit door in the [[cafeteria]], Baldi and the rest of [[:Category:Schoolhouse characters|characters]] will appear to shout a surprise for the player. Baldi then encourages the player to blow out the [[Cake|birthday cake]]'s candle, which grants the player access to the [[glitched school]].
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'', Baldi has the same appearances in other games, except that his voice lines are re-recorded, and he looks more pixelated in the You Can Think Pad.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics - Field Trip demo: Camping]]'', Baldi will first appear standing next to [[Baldi's Bus|"his" bus]]. Later at the forest, before he "leaves" to set up the bear traps, Baldi tells the player to gather logs for the fire. Getting caught by Baldi in this demo results in a game over.
* [[File:LetsPlayHiAndSeek.png|thumb|Baldi from the start of ''Full Game Early Demo''.]]In ''[[Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo]]'', once the player has found the 8 notebooks and triggered 3 of the exit elevators, Baldi will gradually go faster and faster until he goes so fast that he is impossible to run away from, which means that the player has to hurry to reach the final elevator.
** When the broken ruler event occurs, he can still cause a game over despite his ruler being broken.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics - Challenges Demo]]'', Baldi appears in all three challenges: Speedy, Stealthy, and Grapple Challenge.
{{clear}}

== Tips & Tricks ==
=== Advantages ===
* Several characters can be used as the player's advantage against Baldi:
** [[Beans]] - Baldi's movement can be slowed down if he is hit by Beans' bubble gum, which gives the player more time to get away from him.
** [[Dr. Reflex]] - Baldi cannot catch the player if he has been squished by Dr. Reflex's hammer, even if the player is squished as well.
** [[Gotta Sweep]] - can sweep Baldi away, allowing the player to get away from him more easily.
** [[The Test]] - Slows down Baldi when the player looks at him, so they can escape from him more easily.
* There are several items to use against Baldi for the player's survival:
** [[BSODA]] - If used, it will push Baldi (and anyone else) away. It is very useful in long hallways, as it will buy the player a lot of time to run away.
** [[Dangerous Teleporter]] - If the player uses the Dangerous Teleporter, they will teleport to a random area to get away from the close-approaching Baldi. It works even during the final part of each level, but it may also lead the player to a more dangerous situation like teleporting right next to Baldi.
** [[Portal Poster]] - Convenient for getting to another room or hallway quickly. However, Baldi can see and follow the player through the portal.
** [[An Apple for Baldi]] - Baldi will happily take the apple, and after finishing his dialogue, start eating it at an inhuman speed for around 10 seconds. The player must run as soon as possible and find either any Notebooks or other useful items.
** [[Grappling Hook]] - The Grappling Hook's pull has enough force to keep the player's distance away from Baldi, and the speed can vary depending on the length of the hook's line.
** [[Alarm Clock]] - After it rings, Baldi will head to the location where it is placed, unless he sees the player. Baldi's Math Machine and Mrs. Pomp may also override the Alarm Clock's sound.
** [[Baldi's Least Favorite Tape]] - Creates buzz-like statics via the [[tape player]] that deters Baldi and any other character (who are not chasing the player) away for about 30 seconds.
** [[Dirty Chalk Eraser]] - Covers the player in dust clouds so that Baldi will not see them.
** [['Nana Peel]] - Baldi won't be able to move to the player when slipping. Useful when a [[floor]] has many hallways.
** [[Invisibility Elixir]] - Baldi will not be able to see the player, but he can still indirectly catch them.
* Inputting a Quarter in the [[pay phone]] will make the phone emit a buzz-like static noise, which acts exactly like Baldi's Least Favorite Tape.
* As part of one of the [[event]]s, [[Broken ruler|Baldi's ruler breaks]], which means the player cannot hear him coming for a short period of time, but he cannot catch the player while his ruler is broken.
=== Disadvantages ===
There are a few characters who are capable of attracting Baldi:
* [[Arts and Crafters]] - When he teleports the player, Baldi will not only be teleported too, but also head to the location where the player is teleported at. Unless Baldi has seen the player, and he does not see them after being teleported, which causes him to head back to where he last saw them. This is extremely unlikely, however, as it requires the player to be extremely lucky with the seed/map they are playing on or use a Dirty Chalk Eraser the exact frame the player get teleported by Arts and Crafters.
* [[Playtime]] - When the player messes up a jump, Baldi will head to the location where Playtime and the player are playing, unless a higher noise value is going off.
* [[Principal of the Thing]] - When he teleports the player to detention, Baldi will head to the center of the Principal's office if nothing that causes a higher noise value than detention was made, like the [[Alarm Clock]], or the [[tape player]]/[[pay phone]] is disrupting Baldi's hearing. It is also worth noting that Baldi will prioritize sight over this (and any other sounds/teleportation), so he will go to exactly where the player was when being caught by the Principal before he heads to the Principal's office.
* [[1st Prize]] - When he collides with a surface while he is accelerating towards the player at fast speed, the ''Bang!'' sound will play and it will attract Baldi to the location where this sound is played, with a high noise value, obstructing any other noise that was quieter than the noises the player has made.
* [[Chalkles]] - When the player spends over 15 seconds in a [[classroom]] where he spawned, Chalkles will lock the player in, and Baldi will head to the tile with Chalkles' chalkboard on it. It is worth noting that as of [[V0.4 (Plus)|V0.4]] of ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', Chalkles will only slowly go back to being invisible after entering a room, not go away instantly. It is also worth noting that the player can use the [[Principal's Keys]] to unlock a door locked by Chalkles.
* [[Mrs. Pomp]] - When the player goes to exit a room after the timer runs out, Mrs. Pomp will drag the player to her class in a rage, and Baldi will head to the center of the classroom. This attraction also happens when the player enters her classroom either they get dragged around by her or enter her classroom by themselves. This will override every sound, except for the sound for when Baldi sees the player. The player can use the same Principal's Keys trick with Chalkles with Mrs. Pomp, too.

== Slap interval ==
[[File:Baldi's slap interval graph.png|thumb|Graphs to feature Baldi's slap intervals. The variable ''s'' varies depending on the game or floor number.]]
Baldi has a certain "slap interval" when he chases the player. The slap intervals differ between the [[:Category:Games|games]] and the floors in ''Baldi's Basics Plus''.

Baldi's longest slap interval is one smack every 3 seconds, and the intervals get shorter for every notebook the player collects and every wrong answer from Baldi's Math Machine/You Can Think Pad. In ''Classic''/''Birthday Bash'', if Baldi is going faster than his max speed, his slap interval slowly starts to become longer. Prior to [[V1.3 (Classic)|V1.3]] in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'', Baldi would not slow down after getting additional answers wrong. Since ''Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo'', notebooks collected before Baldi chases the player do not affect Baldi's slap interval. However, since V0.3 of ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', Baldi will have the shortest slap interval after the maximum number of notebooks are collected.

'''''Note:''' All interval values in these tables are written in seconds.''
=== Main ===

== Development ==
=== Origins ===
* mystman12's first drawings of Baldi were from a set of comics he made named ''[[Baldimore]]''.<ref>"''It went like this: My sister made a comic, so I wanted to make a comic! So I slapped this mess together.''" - mystman12. May 15, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/996471628895793153 X/Twitter]</ref>
** Baldi has a [[Comic-exclusive character#Andrew|son]]<ref>"''Basically, Baldi walks up to his son (At this point in time, him having a son may or may not be canon.) who is watching...''" - mystman12. August 10, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1028005823207886849 X/Twitter]</ref> and a [[Comic-exclusive character#Susan|wife]]<ref>"''Baldi's wife (Again, I'm not entirely sure what still is and isn't canon at the moment!) wants him to change back, but Baldi is just happy to have more hair!''" - mystman12. August 11, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1028448344786513921 X/Twitter]</ref> in mystman12's old comics, but mystman12 has said that he does not know which facts about Baldi are still canon.
** He called this comic as ''Baldimore'' because he was bald, his name was Baldi, he always wanted more food, and he lived in [[Comic-exclusive location#Baltimore|Baltimore]]. The name of this comic was based on the ideas in his childhood.<ref>"''The comic was called Baldimore because Baldi was bald, his name was Baldi, he always wanted more food (I want MORE... IMORE... or something.), he lived in Baltimore, and because I had horrible ideas as a kid.''" - mystman12. May 15, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/996472189271597056 X/Twitter]</ref>
** Baldi's character is loosely inspired by a comic called "Beastly Beastly" made by his sister Cady, with the likes of a monster.{{Citation}}
* Baldi's last name Baldimore was originally considered not canon but mystman12 decided to canonize it anyway after making his Mii for him.<ref>"''To clarify, I meant that this Mii's last name is Baldimore because I didn't know what else to put... but maybe I should make it canon anyways because why not. :P''" - mystman12. June 8, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1005133873267773442 X/Twitter]</ref>
* In the ''Bad Ball'' microgame made by mystman12, Baldi wears a blue shirt instead of a green one. He noticed this after recording the video.<ref>"''Yep! It's strange, I feel like green has always been the color I've used for his clothes, but when I went to record this I noticed he was wearing blue. I'm not sure when I decided green should be his signature color, but I guess it was after I made this.''" - mystman12. May 21, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/998734736976678913 X/Twitter]</ref>
* [[File:mystman12 Miiverse archive.jpg|200px|thumb|mystman12's Miiverse archive]]mystman12 at first said that Baldi's first internet appearance was in his deleted "BaldiComic" X/Twitter. However, this was disproven as he was going to his Miiverse archive and found out that Baldi's first internet appearance was actually in his ''Game & Wario'' Miiverse sketch post.<ref>"''Y'all remember when I said Baldi's first appearance on the internet was that short lived BaldiComic Twitter account I made? Well I was wrong. I just dug up my Miiverse archive and this Game & Wario Miiverse Sketch post was actually his first appearance. Oh no.''" - mystman12. March 30, 2021. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1377077191150813191 X/Twitter]</ref>
* Baldi and Dr. Reflex are the only comic book characters who were added to ''Baldi’s Basics'' while [[TimTom]] was canceled.

=== Model design ===
* In Baldi's waving animation, his right arm appears to bend very unnaturally, most likely meaning that Baldi is double-jointed.  
** This animation was actually a mistake, but it was used in the game anyways due to its more jarring appearance.<ref>"''I went with CG for the reasons mentioned above, but also because it gave me the opportunity to do crazy stuff when animating him, like his wonky hand wave (That was actually a mistake, but I felt it was creepier so I left it! :P)''" - [https://www.gamasutra.com/view/news/324042/How_Baldis_Basics_taps_into_the_real_horror_of_90s_edutainment.php How ''Baldi's Basics'' taps into the real horror of '90s edutainment by Joel Couture] | Gamasutra</ref>
* Baldi's model was originally created during development on ''Classic''. His oldest one that mystman12 has is dated March 21, 2018.<ref>Q: "''When was Baldi's model originally created?''" / A: "''I originally created it when making the original game. The oldest version of his model that I have is dated March 21, 2018!''" [[:File:Gmail questions 20220922.png|Gmail (question)]], [[:File:Gmail response 20220922.png|Gmail (response)]]</ref>
* In ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', Baldi's render looks a lot cleaner than from the previous games, this could be because there were a few light sources added to the Anim8or scene with the render pose, pointed roughly towards Baldi.
* As said in [[Episode 3]] of the Basically Games Podcast, Baldi's elbow being blue is not a rendering error and is intentional, though the context of it being blue has not been revealed yet as it is implicated with the lore of the game.
** It could be a reference to how Baldi was originally going to have a blue elbow, seen in the BADSUM image.
* Baldi's elbow was originally gray due to accidentally being uncolored, it's the default color when a material is not applied to an object or when creating a new material in Anim8or.
** The HEX code is #E0E0E0, and the RGB code is 224 224 224.
* Baldi's camping outfit still uses the classic model with a gray elbow, while the farm outfit uses the current blue elbow one.
* Baldi may or may not has numerous model revisions.
** In the 100,000 download thumbs-up gif, his head and body are both weight painted to his head bone, and legs are straighter. In comparison, his general rig has his head and body weight-painted to their according bones.
** In ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', likely to account for the numerous field trip accessories, it could be possible that all of Baldi's field trip sprites are all copies of his existing model.
*** In Baldi's tutorial model and the "[[Great Job!]]" sticker, his hair appears to be scaled up, and he has another set of eyebrows layered on top of his other ones.
** Some of these could be disproven through Anim8or's layering and morph target system, which can enable and disable these model changes at will.

=== Audio design ===
* The audio for Baldi slapping his ruler was made by mystman12 slapping his hand with a plastic ruler, despite Baldi using a wooden one in the game.<ref>(1:07:50) "''The Baldi ruler sound I legitimately just took a ruler and slapped it on my hand from the mic. It was a plastic ruler, so it's not completely accurate, but it's good enough.''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|1:07:50|Livestream archive}}</ref>
* Baldi's voice was inspired by the character [[Wikipedia:Waluigi|Waluigi]] from the ''[[Wikipedia:Super Mario|Super Mario]]'' franchise.{{Citation}}
* mystman12 uses a Blue Yeti microphone to record Baldi's voicelines, and used it ever since he started developing ''Baldi's Basics Plus''. 
** However, when mystman12 first revealed this, he also thought his Blue Yeti microphone was broken and that he needed to use his Rode microphone instead, since it didn't record any voicelines properly, so he stated that on his X/Twitter as well.<ref>"''I think my Blue Yeti finally died. Served me well, but I've got better options now. I did plan to keep using it for Baldi to ensure all his voice lines sounded consistent (Since it was the only mic I had when I started working on Plus), but now I guess I'll have to use my Rode.''" - mystman12. December 4, 2024. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1864477393429111146 X/Twitter]</ref>
*** The Blue Yeti microphone, however, wasn't the case for this issue, as it turns out that the USB converter caused it and the microphone still worked properly.<ref>"''False alarm it just randomly stopped playing well with my USB A to C converter. USB mics are fun!''" - mystman12. December 6, 2024. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1865123693346639893 X/Twitter]</ref>
*** Upcoming Baldi's voicelines could've sounded different, if this issue remained unsolved.<ref>"''If Baldi's future voicelines sound slightly different  from what's in the game now, this is why!''" - mystman12. December 4, 2024. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1864477560433738157 X/Twitter]</ref>
** The new audios appear to interfere on the microphone.
* Baldi's audio when congratulating the player for collecting all notebooks reuses his post-NULL style audio from ''Classic Remastered'', due to Baldi saying "Find a way out before I catch you!" but edited to account for how many notebooks on a floor is collected.
** Similar to Baldi's audio when congratulating the player in the ''Full Game Early Demo'', the audio is spliced with soundbytes for Baldi saying "four", "seven", and "nine", on the first, second/fourth, and third/fifth floors respectively, heard when using the You Can Think Pad in ''Classic Remastered''. This is likely done to make the audio easier to reuse without needing to record separate voicelines for each floor.
** Despite Baldi already mentioning seven notebooks in ''Classic Remastered'', his voiceline is still spliced to maintain consistency with the other recordings.
* The "''WOW!''" And "''Let's go camping!''" are the oldest audio that Baldi had said in ''Baldi's Basics Plus''.
** This is because these lines were first recorded in ''Baldi's Basics - Field Trip demo: Camping'' and weren't re-recorded since.
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', Baldi's screech sound was made when mystman12 imported a non-audio file into Audacity as "Raw Data", but he does not remember what settings he used to get the sound.<!--"It is likely an edited ''[[Wikipedia:Donkey Kong Country|Donkey Kong Country]]'' image, with a cropped 99 in the bottom right corner." <-- mystman12 has not yet confirmed what file he used for the screech. --><ref>"''Hmm, if I recall correctly I think I loaded a random non-audio file into Audacity via the "Import > Raw data" tool. I don't remember what kind of file or what kind of settings I used though. Generally though, importing a non-audio file into Audacity is a good way to get horrible sounds like that.''"- mystman12's [[:File:Baldi's screech development.png|X/Twitter DM]]</ref>
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'', Baldi's jumpscare sound is different depending on which platform the player plays this game on.
** On Windows, it is the screeching noise from the You Can Think Pad, but then played each frame, hence why it sounds different.
** On macOS, most likely due to architecture, the sound is different. It seems to play each frame, but then at a higher rate.
** On mobile platforms, the sound does not repeat at all, but instead plays once.
* mystman12 has revealed that at some point during development of ''Baldi's Basics Classic'', Baldi had an unused dialogue file with a "parrot noise" at the end of the line in a reference to [[Wikipedia:List of Jimmy Neutron characters#Miss Winfred Fowl|Ms. Fowl]] from the show ''[[Wikipedia:The Adventures of Jimmy Neutron: Boy Genius|The Adventures of Jimmy Neutron: Boy Genius]]''; however, it seems that it was not left in the files of any version.<ref>"''You know... Like Ms. Fowl from Jimmy Neutron, I actually have an unused audio file that I have in my with all my Baldi voice recordings called Ms. Fowl ...''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|31:50|Livestream archive 31:50-32:00}}</ref>
* A Lost Bits video of ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' by TetraBitGaming revealed a voice clip that would have been used for the spelling subject, actually shown by mystman12 himself.<ref>"''The only other subject I did any work on was spelling. I actually started recording Baldi's intro for that subject, but that's it.''" - mystman12. {{YouTube|CSM2vX2uFGU|2:58|''Baldi's Basics Classic'' Lost Bits 2:58-3:06}}</ref>
* [[File:Baldi's unused Speech fields.png|200px|thumb|The unused "Speech" fields.]]In the Unity Project file for ''Baldi's Basics Classic'', there exists 3, albeit empty, Audioclip spots on Baldi's script. It suggests that it was planned for Baldi to talk while chasing, but in the final game, this was scrapped.
** This idea has since been reused for Null in NULL style of ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered''. Since he makes no sound while moving, it's one of the only ways to know where he is (aside from the doors that open when he passes by).
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' ''Remastered'', when Baldi says "''Answer the three questions - correctly - and you might get something special!''", the "correctly" part is distorted. This was due to when the same line was recorded in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'', the mic glitched out, distorting the "correctly". The distortion in the re-recording of the line is a playful nod to the unintended glitching of the mic, and also serves as an intentional emphasis on the importance of answering the questions correctly.<ref>"''That's intentional. For emphasis!''" - [https://steamcommunity.com/app/1712830/eventcomments/3485249057158228080/#c3485249057158393073 ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'' Steam Community Hub]</ref>
* In ''Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash'', "''a shiny quarter!''" and "''Then, when you find something, you can use it on, right click on the object with the quarter selected. That's how you use items y'know!''" quotes were removed because Baldi gives the player a present instead of a Quarter - which is not guaranteed to ''have'' a quarter inside.
* In ''Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo'', the audio of Baldi congratulating the player for collecting all the notebooks is reused from ''Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', with the exception of the "''eight''" quote (used for the You Can Think Pad) replacing "''seven''". It is also more echoed and less loud.

=== Other information ===
* Baldi's sprite used in the secret endings of ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'' is reused from ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' (Prior to [[V0.9 (Plus)|V0.9]]) when he stops to praise the player for answering an activity correctly - though the sprite is also used in normal gameplay, only in Demo Style.
* In the name entry screen, the green color of Baldi's shirt looks a little weaker due to the lighting in Anim8or.
* Tutor Baldi (the Baldi that greets the player) does not have a hitbox. This can be seen if the player sprays a [[BSODA]] at him.
** Baldi as a farmer and camper do not have hitboxes either.
* If Baldi somehow stopped while chasing the player, after Baldi is able to move, he will suddenly move toward them without slapping his ruler. This happens because Baldi's speed is independent of his slapping interval.<ref>Q: "''In Baldi's Basics Plus, when Baldi isn't slapping, he is slowly building up speed for his next slap. This "energy" is released when he slaps.''" / A: "''I programmed Baldi this way so that his slap rate could be independent of his speed. In the future I might mess with stuff like making him slap in time to music for example, and I wanted to make sure that sort of thing didn't result in any unintended changes in speed.'' - [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=h6Gdikb1Njs&lc=UgyNVef10C1omfUi6VF4AaABAg.A4Zt6YevQJqA4dzFaPt__a The MOST DIFFICULT Mod Baldi's Basics Has To OFFER.. (O'Hare Over Baldi)]</ref>
* There might be a transition for changing nice Baldi into angry Baldi after countdown is over, as revealed in one of mystman12's posts.<ref>"''Baldi's transition from nice to angry will also be handled a lot more naturally later as well, it won't just be proximity based like it is currently.''" - [https://steamcommunity.com/app/1275890/discussions/0/2441461920662378593/#c2441461920662757019 ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' Steam Community Hub]</ref> However, the post was most likely referring to a change that was eventually done in V0.3, where Baldi would start a countdown rather than instantly chasing the player once the player walked a far enough distance away.
* In ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', if all {{C|AnimationClip}} files are removed from the game assets, Baldi will have an idling sprite with his normal outfit in the outdoor area.
* When analyzing the code for Baldi in the tutorial, when he tags the player, it turns out he enters his "praise" NPC state for 999,999 seconds, meaning that Baldi can technically resume chasing the player, if they have the patience to wait that out (999999 seconds amounts to a little over '''''11.5 days''''').
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', if the player gets out of the school's {{C|NavMesh}}, either with hacks or standing on the three tiles connected to an [[exit]], Baldi (and other characters, if they're chasing the player.) will try and fail to reach the player until they get back in the school. as the {{C|NavMesh}} itself was built to only reach the inner bounds of the school layout, excluding exits. This isn't the case in Classic Remastered/Plus, as if the player somehow finds a way to get out of bounds and Baldi can still see the player, he will burst through the wall to try and get them.
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', Baldi's waving animation is smoother than other games and demos due to it having all [[99]] frames, where the animation's total amount of frames were halved in ''Plus'' and ''Classic Remastered''.
* On the mobile version of ''Baldi's Basics Classic'', when the player answers all three questions correctly in the first notebook, Baldi will say "''You did great! Come here and get your prize, a shiny quarter!''", but he does not give instructions on how to use items as mobile devices do not have a mouse and a keyboard.
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'', unlike in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', Baldi can see the player even if they are above the Schoolhouse.
* In ''Classic Remastered'', if noclip hacks are used to get out of the schoolhouse and fly higher, then Baldi will pass through the walls and will move beyond the school boundary.
* In ''Classic Remastered'', pressing {{Key|P}} after Baldi welcomes the player will cause Baldi to repeat his introduction dialogue again, along with his welcoming animation.<ref>"''If you press the "P" key in Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered before angering Baldi, he will replay his waving animation.''" - mystman12. January 24, 2023. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1617849386180169730 X/Twitter]</ref>
** This feature was intended to test something, most likely the trailer, but it was not removed from the game.<ref>Q: "''Bruh I thought that was test stuff you left in game by mistake''" / A: "''It was lol.''" - mystman12. January 24, 2023. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1617908343116554241 X/Twitter]</ref>
* In ''Classic Remastered'', by using hacks to spawn Baldi in NULL/Glitch Style, once the boss fight begins, Baldi will always follow the player around the school like Null and the red {{C|Baldloon}}.
** He will also gain the same speed boost that Null/the red {{C|Baldloon}} get when the player uses the [[Principal Whistle]], and he will also be attracted to the player's position.
** If Baldi catches the player in NULL style, he will erase the player's save files.
* In ''Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo'', when Baldi slaps for the first time after spawning, he jumps a little. This no longer happens in future game releases.
* In Party Style, giving him An Apple makes his hat disappear, same with the field trips before they were renewed in ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' V0.3. This is because mystman12 did not make a sprite of Baldi eating An Apple with his party hat on, as there are no apples normally available in Party Style.
** The same also happens if the player starts the broken ruler event by either using some sort of hack, or by enabling [[Chaos Mode]], as it duplicates all characters when a Notebook is collected, and events also happen in other styles (and can stack).

Baldi is not a threat in this game, as he asks the player to find his ruler for him. When doing so, it unlocks a trophy in-game, where he can say a few others lines of his own. mystman12 also did voice acting for this character.<ref>"''I manage to complete every difficulty in this game mode while also finding the secret easter egg: Baldi! Baldi from Baldi's Basics is in the game and he is fully voice acted by Mystman12 himself! The developer of Baldi's Basics in Education and Learning!''" - {{YouTube|Btn713BKAN0||BALDI WAS IN THE BACKROOMS! {{!}} Boofie's Bunker - Easy/Normal/Hard/Extreme Backrooms & All Unlockables}}</ref>

** A few lines Baldi can say [[Fourth wall break|break the fourth wall]], such as the non-canonical nature of his cameo ("''Remember kids, this isn't canon!''"), the graphical fidelity of the game ("''These graphics are far too advanced for me!''"), so on.
* mystman12 created some Baldi fireworks designs in ''[[Wikipedia:Animal Crossing: New Horizons|Animal Crossing: New Horizons]]''.<ref>"''Don't mind me just traumatizing my islanders with some of the scariest fireworks designs I could think of! #AnimalCrossing #NintendoSwitch''" - mystman12. [https://x.com/Xploshi/status/1282785285391167496 X/Twitter]</ref>
* mystman12 voiced Baldi and possibly others in the ''[https://fridaynightfunkin.wiki.gg/wiki/Friday_Night_Funkin%27 Friday Night Funkin<nowiki>'</nowiki>]'' mod, ''[https://fridaynightfunking.fandom.com/wiki/Baldi%27s_Basics_In_Funkin%27 Baldi's Basics In Funkin<nowiki>'</nowiki>]''.<ref name=":0">"''HE SAID YES TO VOICE ACTING''" - Fidy50, director of Baldi's Basics In Funkin'. == Trivia ==
* Assuming the school is set in an American environment, Baldi is 5 feet 7 inches accounting the brick wall texture.
* Teachers doing any sort of action that involves harming students is well notably illegal nowadays in reality. However, this was common and accepted by law between the 19th and 20th century, where teachers would beat students with objects, such as canes and rulers if they did something wrong or broke the rules. This was until it got banned in most countries around the 1980s. However, this is still a thing in some African and Asian regions.
* Baldi's name, coincidentally, is an Indonesian/Malay word for a pail, more specifically, a plastic bucket.
** On the same note, the pronounciation of his name in Portuguese also aligns with "balde", the word in the language for "bucket".
* Despite [[Principal of the Thing]] being the official principal, Baldi refers to the school as "his" Schoolhouse. It is possible that he owns the schoolhouse with the Principal of the Thing managing it.
* Baldi's birthday is on June 3.<ref>"''June 3!''" - mystman12. August 22, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1032372311167385601 X/Twitter]</ref>
* Baldi is about 29 years old.<ref name = "age">"''He is probably about like 29...''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|29:49|Livestream archive 29:49-29:52}}</ref>
* The overall image of Baldi giving a thumbs up when the game was downloaded over 100,000 times is a [[Inspiration and reference|reference]] to ''Sonic's Schoolhouse''<nowiki>'</nowiki>s image screen when beating the level with 10/10 correct answers.
* Spoken by mystman12 during his livestream, some lines have references to other media:
** The line where Baldi says "''FUS RO DAH!''" is a reference to one of the dragon shouts from the game ''[[Wikipedia:The Elder Scrolls V: Skyrim|The Elder Scrolls V: Skyrim]]''.
** Another one of Baldi's lines, "''Hadouken!''", is a direct reference to the 1987 video game: ''[[Wikipedia:Street Fighter|Street Fighter]]'' in which the character Ryu will shout the same phrase when using one of his special moves.
** Baldi's "''Open Salami!''" line is one of the Mario's voices from the game ''[[Wikipedia:Super Mario Sunshine|Super Mario Sunshine]]''.<!---Move all four trivia parts to mystman12's Baldi's Basics livestream video page once it gets made.--->
* Gotta Sweep and Baldi are mystman12's favorite characters.<ref>"''I think Baldi would have to be my favorite, since that’s a character I made up years ago, but I think besides that I would have to go with Gotta Sweep.''" - mystman12. August 3, 2018. [http://gamingtrend.com/feature/interviews/you-call-hamburgers-steamed-hams-baldis-basics-in-education-and-learning-creator-on-development-burgers INTERVIEWS You call hamburgers steamed hams — Baldi’s Basics in Education and Learning creator on development, burgers by Elisha Deogracias]</ref>
* Despite Baldi having excessive hunger and eating a lot, he has a thin body.<ref>"''He has an insatiable appetite. No one really knows how he stays so thin.''" - [[:File:Gmail response 20211007.png|Gmail]]</ref> It could possibly mean that Baldi has difficulty gaining weight or just has a fast digestion system.
* Baldi is extremely terrible at spelling. There are lots of examples, such as [[book]]s in [[Baldi's Basement|his basement]] and the spelling classroom's letters order.
** This was possibly made because it makes sense why Baldi only teaches math.
* Vanilla is Baldi's favorite ice cream flavor. He especially likes the ice cream from the plastic cups with wooden spoons, commonly bought at stores.<ref>"''Vanilla, but specifically the vanilla ice cream from those little plastic cups with the wooden spoon-like things.''" - [[:File:Gmail response 20211007.png|Gmail]]</ref>
* mystman12 says that Baldi would open a pizza place,<ref>"''Baldi would open a pizza place.''" - mystman12. October 31, 2021. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1454664534531133444 X/Twitter]</ref> but all the pizza boxes are empty since Baldi has eaten all the pizza.<ref>"''But they have nothing but empty pizza boxes because he ate all the pizza.''" - mystman12. October 31, 2021. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1454664659152379904 X/Twitter]</ref>
* A variant of Baldi with a camping suit was planned to have a plush and an action figure. But it, along with the [[Arts and Crafters]] beanie plush have yet to be released.
* mystman12 made a weird Baldi model with an upside-down orange head.<ref>Q: "''Do you have any super weird renders of Baldi that you've made in the past that no one has ever seen?''" / A: "''I sure do, like this one:''" - [[:File:Gmail questions 20220922.png|Gmail (question)]], [[:File:Gmail response 20220922.png|Gmail (response)]]</ref>
* According to the interview on Game Jolt, Baldi in the ''Baldimore'' comic series and Baldi in the ''Baldi's Basics'' series are different, because the former is an inventor, and the latter is a teacher.<ref>(09:40) Q: "''Is the Baldi from that comic in your mind the same Baldi of the teacher, or is it a different Baldi from a Baldi multiverse?''" / A: "''It's probably somewhere in between - I don't actually think there will be a Baldi multiverse - but in the same way, it's not, probably not like the same iteration of Baldi, yeah. Baldi in the comic was not a teacher. He was an inventor, so Baldi in Baldi's Basics is definitely different.''" - [https://gamejolt.com/p/new-video-interview-with-the-creator-of-baldi-s-basics-classic-rem-ggdvhzy9 Interview with the creator of Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered, BasicallyGames!]</ref>
** However, he also has invented/built some stuff in the ''Baldi's Basics'' series, such as the [[Portal Poster]], [[shrink machine]], and vents.
* Alongside math, Baldi also likes science, although that involves a lot of math.<ref>Q: "''does baldi like any other subjects? or is it just math?''" / A: "''Science is another favorite of his! But of course, that also involves a lot of math.''" - [[:File:Gmail questions 20221114.png|Gmail (question)]], [[:File:Gmail response 20221114.png|Gmail (response)]]</ref>
* Baldi has done something possibly heinous in 1992, but he will not admit to it until he is caught.<ref>Q: "''When will Baldi admit to what he did in 1992?''" / A: "''When he's caught...''" - [[:File:Gmail questions 20221110.png|Gmail (question)]], [[:File:Gmail response 20221110.png|Gmail (response)]]</ref>
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', many people thought that Baldi said "''You '''failed''' all seven notebooks!''". However, mystman12 himself has confirmed it was "found" and not "failed", and he was completely unaware of the debate prior to being questioned about it.<ref>"''It's "you FOUND". I wasn't even aware there was a debate until now. :P''" - mystman12. May 28, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1001223229686599681 X/Twitter]</ref>
* In ''Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo'' and ''Challenges Demo'', Baldi's opening quote seems to be poking fun at free-to-play, a common trend in recent mobile games.
* The spelling error in Baldi's description in the Principal's office prior to V0.3.5 of ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' was first fixed in the Baldi [[Toy#Vinyl figures|Youtooz figure]]'s box set.

[[Category:Male]]\n\n======== Inventory len 9782 ========\n{{For|a similar HUD|Item counter}}
{{Quote|To pick up an item, just look at it and press the "ACTION" button.<br>Then it will be added to your inventory.|Baldi|Tutorial|sound=BAL_Tutorial_Inventory.wav}}
The '''inventory''' (or '''item slots'''{{Tt|*|Texture file name}}) is an [[item]] storage system in the ''[[Baldi's Basics]]'' series, shown on the top-right corner of the screen.

== Mechanics ==
=== ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]'' ===
<div style="text-align: right; ">[[File:ItemSlot Dynamic 0.png]][[File:ItemSlot Dynamic 1.png]][[File:ItemSlot Dynamic 1.png]][[File:ItemSlot Dynamic 1.png]][[File:ItemSlot Dynamic 2.png]]</div>
[[File:9InventorySlots teaser.png|200px|thumb|An example of 9 inventory slots.]]
Normally, the inventory can hold up to 5 items at once. Items can be picked up with the "Interact" action (Left-click or {{Key|E}} by default) and used with the "Use Item" action (Right-click or {{Key|Q}} by default). By applying the [[backpack]] [[sticker]], the player will gain 1 extra inventory slot, for a total of 9 slots if using 4 Backpack Stickers. If the "[[Game Setting#Challenges|Shrinking Inventory]]" challenge is enabled, using an item will reduce the number of available inventory slots by 1, but lasting for only a single [[Floor|level]].

The player can scroll the mouse wheel or press the number keys ({{Key|1}}, {{Key|2}}, {{Key|3}}, {{Key|4}}, and {{Key|5}} by default; {{Key|6}}, {{Key|7}}, {{Key|8}}, and {{Key|9}} are also included if there are extra slots made by Backpack stickers) to select different items. The item the player has selected will have a red background in its square. If the player has an inventory slot selected with nothing in it, then the item slot will just say "{{Font|[[Nothing]]}}".

To swap an item, the player has to left click an item with another item selected with a full inventory. The player will pick up the new item and the old one will be left in its place. If the player approaches [[It's a Bully]] while having items, he will take a random item from their inventory.

When the player picks up an item, it will move left to right in the inventory. However, when they use an item, it will move right to left.

=== ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]''/''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash]]'' ===
[[File:ItemSlots.png|200px|right]]
The inventory has a different design and can only hold 3 items at a time. Once the inventory is full, picking up an item will result in the previous item getting permanently removed.
{{clear}}

=== ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered]]'' ===
[[File:ItemSlot Dynamic.png|right]]
The inventory functions the same as the one from ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', but there are only 3 item slots and there's no animation when items are picked up or used. In [[Authentic Mode]], the inventory uses the same texture as the one in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash''.
{{clear}}

== Development ==
* [[mystman12]] considered adding a separated inventory to keep [[Quarter]]s in a future update, as stated during a livestream.<ref>"''I'm considering giving quarters in like a separate item slot in the full game so you stack quarters instead of having them to take off from your item slot. I'm-I'm thinking about getting you like five slots in the full game and then get this new separate quarter slot.''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|1:12:13|Livestream archive 1:12:13-1:12:29}}</ref> It was later scrapped, due to [[You Thought Points]] making it redundant.<ref>"''I don't plan on giving quarters a separate item slot anymore. You Thought Points kind of made the idea of quarters as a currency redundant.''" - [[:File:Gmail response 20220909-1.webp|Gmail]]</ref>
* Even though the "'''{{Font|Nothing}}'''" icon ({{Color||#FFFFFF|[[File:PlaceHolder.png|link=]]}}) was used in ''[[Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo|Full Game Early Demo]]'', it was not implemented in the later [[:Category:Demos|demos]] nor ''Baldi's Basics Plus''.
* In all games except for ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', [[Baldi]] teaches the player how to use the inventory after completing and collecting the first [[notebook]].<ref>"''Just click on it with the interact button to pick it up. Then, when you find something you can use it on, Use<!--Following the official caption--> the item button on the object with the quarter selected. That's how you use items y'know!''" - [[:File:BAL_GetPrize3.wav|{{C|BAL_GetPrize3.wav}}]], [[:File:BAL_GetPrize4.wav|{{C|BAL_GetPrize4.wav}}]], [[:File:BAL_GetPrize5.wav|{{C|BAL_GetPrize5.wav}}]], and [[:File:BAL_GetPrize6.wav|{{C|BAL_GetPrize6.wav}}]] files from ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered''<nowiki/>'s asset files</ref>
** In ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', he does so in the [[Tutorial]].
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'', ''Birthday Bash'', and Authentic Mode of ''Classic Remastered'', the inventory resembles a cut off piece of the [[You Can Think Pad]].
* [[File:Classic HUD outside.png|200px|right]]In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'', when using hacks by teleporting out of the map of [[Baldi's Office]], the player can see the inventory, along with the stamina bar and item counter.
* In the "Baldi's Basics 3 Year Anniversary Presentation!" video, the inventory had 5 slots during development of ''Classic Remastered''.<ref>Baldi's Basics 3 Year Anniversary Presentation! - {{YouTube|GUNA7YiOXWc|9:03}}</ref>, but was reduced to 3 slots in-game to match ''Classic''/''Birthday Bash''.
{{clear}}

[[Category:Player aspects]]\n\n======== Stamina len 9337 ========\n{{StructuredQuote
| text = No running in the halls!
| speaker = [[Principal of the Thing]]
| sound = PRI_NoRunning.wav
}}
[[File:Staminometer with stamina.png|thumb|The Staminometer that is used to feature the stamina.]]
'''Stamina''' is a meter [[:Category:HUDs|HUD]] in the ''[[Baldi's Basics]]'' series. It allows [[the player]] to run anywhere so they can get away from [[Baldi]] or other [[Character#Schoolhouse/Super Schoolhouse characters|characters]] and collect [[notebook]]s faster and more efficiently.

== Mechanics ==
=== ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]/[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered]]'' ===
[[File:Stamina.gif|thumb|The Staminometer's needle moving.]]
The amount of stamina left is displayed by the Staminometer, located at the bottom left of the screen. It has a red to green gradient background, text at the top that says "'''{{Font|STAMINOMETER}}'''", and a blue needle that displays the amount of stamina the player has. It is located at the bottom left of the screen as a heads-up display.

When the player runs by holding the "Run" key ({{Key|Left Shift}} by default), the Staminometer's needle will move to the left. If the Principal of the Thing sees the player run for more than a half second (except in the playground and [[Principal's office]]), he will scold them for breaking one of the [[school rule]]s, so caution should be taken while running.

There are five ways to recharge stamina:
* Stopping and waiting for several seconds.
* Eating an [[Energy Flavored Zesty Bar]], which will ''set'' the player's stamina to 200%.
* Completing an activity (collecting a notebook prior to [[V0.12 (Plus)|V0.12]])
* Walking around or standing still in the [[playground]]. (Running here does not recharge nor drop the stamina.)
* Drinking from a [[water fountain]].

[[File:Staminometer empty.png|thumb|When stamina is empty.]][[File:Overcharged stamina.png|thumb|The Staminometer without the needle.]]If the Staminometer's needle is located at the left and covering half of the needle, the player will not be able to run, making them vulnerable to Baldi. Also, when the player eats an Energy Flavored Zesty Bar, the needle cannot be seen. It will slowly move to the left while they are using stamina until it returns to the normal full position, then the drain speed returns to normal.
{{clear}}
=== ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]''/''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash]]'' ===
[[File:Stamina Classic.png|thumb|The stamina bar.]]
[[File:Out-of-Stamina.png|thumb|The meter when stamina has completely run out.]]
The stamina meter is located on the top center of the screen. It is a green bar with a red background, and when it runs out, a warning ({{Font|{{Color|#FF0000|YOU NEED REST!}}}}) will display at the bottom of the meter.

{{SpoilerHide|
[[File:Authentic.png|thumb|The Staminometer in Authentic Mode.]]
However, in [[Authentic Mode]], there is a lever on top of the Staminometer to toggle running.

The player has infinite stamina while in [[Baldi's Office]], the [[glitched school]], and [[Baldi's Basement]], so even if they run, their stamina will not deplete.}}
{{clear}}

== Development ==
* The player recharges stamina while they are playing Playtime's minigame, if they are standing still{{Tt|*|Jumping does not count as moving}}, which would seemingly and logically consume energy. This might be due to the fact that the player is standing still when they play jump rope, and [[mystman12]] did not program the jumping to decrease stamina.
** It might also be due to the fact that because the player is standing still and is vulnerable to Baldi, the stamina recharges to allow the player to run away from Baldi if he is close by once the player had finished Playtime's jump rope minigame. This keeps the game fair.
* The infinite stamina feature was first mentioned in one of the comments in [[Beta-tester#JayskiBean|JayskiBean]]'s {{YouTube|5iYmNVHajIw|1:00:18|I MADE MYSTMAN12 (creator of Baldi) PLAY ALEX BASICS IN BIOLOGY AND ZOOLOGY - All Endings & Secrets}} video.<ref>"Q: "''True, but Birthday Bash didn't give you infinite stamina ;)''" / A: "''But it might soon!''" - mystman12. [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5iYmNVHajIw&lc=UgxbJb8tsNiy1IClQVx4AaABAg.9eNx0916HIA9eOvMzJrs6v YouTube]</ref>
* In ''Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash'', while the player is on the elevator-like platform, the stamina will not be refilled until they blow out the fire on the [[cake]]'s candle. Pressing the "Run" key will reduce the stamina in that case.
** This does not occur in the [[Party Style]] from ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered''.
* [[File:Classic HUD outside.png|thumb|200px|The stamina meter seen outside when using hacks.]]In [[Baldi's Office]] (only in ''Baldi's Basics Classic''), the stamina GUI moves outside the [[hallway]] where the player sees the splash screen, along with the [[inventory]] and the [[Item counter|notebook counter]]. This can only be seen when using noclip hacks.
{{clear}}

{{Quote|You need to collect two notebooks before you can use these doors.|Swinging doors, in ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash''/''Classic Remastered''|sound=BAL_Doors.wav}}
'''Swinging doors''' are a type of [[:Category:Doors|door]] in the ''[[Baldi's Basics]]'' series. Like standard [[door]]s, opening these doors will attract [[Baldi]] and angry [[Dr. Reflex]] by the sound, but there is no sound when closing, unlike standard doors.

Some swinging doors have special requirements to get them to open. Normal and one-way swinging doors can be locked by a [[Swinging Door Lock]], preventing any entity from opening it, including the [[player]] and all [[NPC]]s, for 30 seconds in ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]'' and ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered|Classic Remastered]]'' (or 15 in ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'' and ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash|Birthday Bash]]'').

== Types ==
There are five types of swinging doors, '''normal swinging doors''' (also known as '''yellow doors''' in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash''), '''coin doors''', '''one-way doors''', '''auto door''' and '''flap doors'''.

=== Normal ===
<gallery position="center">
SwingDoorGame.png|The normal swinging door.
LockedSwingDoorGame.png|A locked swinging door.
</gallery>
The most common variant is littered throughout the [[hallway]]s. It has windows which the player can see through to view incoming [[character]]s. Every character (excluding [[Chalkles]]) can open this door. This door does not need to be interacted with to be opened but instead requires physical contact. The Swinging Door Lock can only be used on this type of swinging door.

Swinging doors may appear next to each other, notably with the [[cafeteria]], [[library]] and [[playground]].

=== Coin ===
[[File:CoinDoorGame.png|200px|right]]
The '''coin door''' first appears as an ordinary swinging door that has a realistic photo texture of a coin slot in a deep reddish-purple color, and it has text that reads "'''25¢'''" and "'''PUSH TO REJECT'''".

The coin door appears in some hallways and requires the player to insert a [[Quarter]] into the slot of the coin door. If the player does not insert a Quarter into the coin slot, nobody can pass through the door, essentially acting as a permanent Swinging Door Lock.

After inserting a Quarter into the coin slot, the coin door will change into an ordinary swinging door permanently, becoming accessible.

[[File:The Principal scolds the player through the coin door.png|200px|thumb|Principal of the Thing scolding the player (seen in the caption) from the other side of the coin door.]]
Like with a locked normal swinging door, NPCs can see the player through the coin door.

These swinging doors can be generated on [[Floor 2]] onwards and all challenges, except for the [[Stealthy Challenge]].
{{clear}}

=== One-way ===
<gallery position="center">
RightWayDoor.png|The one-way door on the right side.
WrongWayDoor.png|Ditto, but on the wrong side.
</gallery>
Characters can open and go through one-way swinging doors if the side has green arrow icons. On the other side, there is a door with a red line icon, which cannot be opened once it has been closed. Even if the wrong side of the door is opened, NPCs will not go through the door, but instead, they will go in a different direction. However, if the door is already open, the player can go through it regardless of side.

These swinging doors can be generated on Floor 2 onwards and in [[Endless Mode]].

=== Auto ===
[[File:AutoDoorInJohnnyWall.png|200px|right]]
The auto door is a special door used for [[Johnny's Store]]. It is a pair of metal doors with a sensor above them. One of the metal doors has a sign on it with red text. It says "'''{{Font|{{Color|#FF0000|yep, open!}}}}'''".

Unlike other swinging doors, this door cannot be locked by the Swinging Door Lock, due to their appearance being different.

=== Flap ===
[[File:Flap door.png|200px|right]]
Flap doors are used on the entrances or exits of the conveyor belt part of the [[crane room]]. Like the auto door, these doors cannot be locked by the Swinging Door Lock.

These doors can only be found in the [[factory]] [[type]].
{{clear}}

== Text ==
The texts that are displayed in the respective door, or in any place of the door

=== Special door ===
* Auto door (Unlocked) | {{Font|Comic Sans MS|{{Color|#FF0000|#FFFFFF|yep, open!}}}}

=== Cut ===
* Auto door (Locked) | {{Font|Comic Sans MS|{{Color|#FF0000|#FFFFFF|nope, closed!}}}}

== [[Location]]s ==
=== Main gameplay ===
While these doors are mostly scattered through the halls, they also lead to some [[:Category:Special locations|special locations]], such as the [[cafeteria]], [[library]], [[outdoor area]], and [[playground]].

=== [[Challenge]]s ===
The only types available in each current challenge are normal and coin doors, while the Stealthy Challenge has only normal swinging doors.

== Other appearances ==
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'', ''Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash'' and ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'', only the normal swinging doors are featured. At the start of the game, the player will be unable to go through the first three swinging doors, with one in front of them and one to the left and right, until they have collected two [[notebook]]s. These doors also lead through [[exit]]s that are found after the player collects 7 notebooks in [[Story Mode]]. There are 8 swinging doors in total.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics - Field Trip demo: Camping]]'', there are 2 normal swinging doors: one that leads to the outdoor area and another behind the [[Super Schoolhouse]] where {{C|[[PlaceFace]]}} is hiding.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo]]'', there are 5 normal swinging doors in total. Furthermore, the coin doors are introduced, but there is only one.

== Development ==
=== Texture design ===
* Swinging doors locked by the Swinging Door Lock has a slightly different border around the edges.
* The coin door's slots look like quarter slots in arcade machines.

=== Other information ===
* Using BSODA on an NPC in the path of a one-way swinging door will open the door, regardless of which side the NPC is blasted.
* When any character is located between a swinging door or auto door, it will keep open.
* The Stealthy Challenge has the least swinging doors in all pre-made maps as it has only two of them.
* If a Swinging Door Lock is used on one-way swinging doors, their texture will reset to the regular one.
* If using hacks to place swinging doors to the Principal's office, Principal of the Thing will lock these doors during detention, and their lock timing is followed by the timer in the [[gauge]].
* If 2 swinging doors are generated side-to-side across the same wall (which is somewhat common for large special locations such as the [[library]]) the player as well as NPCs will be able to go through the wall between the doors, doing this will cause both doors to open.
* The auto door uses the same sounds as an [[elevator]].
* In [[Demo Style]] of ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'', it is possible to bypass the first three swinging doors before collecting two notebooks. To do this, the player needs to answer the [[Math Machine]] questions but not collect the notebooks. This will make Baldi angry despite not having any notebooks, and if the player waits long enough for the flood event to occur, they can be pulled into a whirlpool and get teleported beyond the swinging doors.
* In ''Classic Remastered'', despite the "You need to collect two notebooks before you can use these doors." line seemingly coming from Baldi, the line's captions appear in the direction of the swinging doors.
** On top of this, Baldi does not have a talking animation when the line plays.
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', until the second notebook is collected, the swinging door at the dark hallway with the [[Baldi Mailbox]] comic has a Swinging Door Lock on one side of it.
* Swinging doors will make sound even if the player passes by them while they are open. This only happens in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash''.
* [[File:Swinging Door outside of the school.png|thumb|200px|right|The hovering swinging door in the ''Full Game Early Demo'', seen when hacking.]]In ''Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo'', when teleporting outside of the school with hacks, a swinging door can be seen above the school building.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics in Education and Learning: Development Build]]'', the opening sound for the swinging doors is the one for the standard doors instead of its own opening sound.
{{clear}}
