{{Quote|I HEAR EVERY DOOR YOU OPEN|[[Baldi]], in the [[You Can Think Pad]] after the player gets a problem wrong.}}
'''Doors''' are one of the major [[structure]]s of the ''[[Baldi's Basics]]'' series, allowing access to different [[location]]s, like classrooms and faculty rooms.

== Overview ==
The player can open the doors by interacting with or touching the {{Cursor}} button while facing them.

When a door is opened, it will automatically close itself after 3 seconds. However, the door can stay opened if the player interacts with it constantly.

Baldi can also hear any door the player opens. In this case, if the player opens any door, Baldi can immediately listen out for which door they opened and ultimately know that the player opened it, therefore Baldi will track down the location of the door.

During the [[flood]] [[event]], every door in the [[Super Schoolhouse]] will stay open, giving the player a chance to enter any room without being heard by Baldi and angry [[Dr. Reflex]], unless they see the player.

A special door type, called a [[swinging door]], has a few different designs that function as obstacles.

== Types ==
There are several different types of standard doors. The area made by [[WD-NoSquee]] can make those doors silent, preventing them from being heard by Baldi. The [[Principal's Keys]] can be used on doors locked by [[Chalkles]], [[Mrs. Pomp]] or any door locked during detention. The texture of the door depends on the room it leads to.

=== Common doors ===
Only three types of '''common doors''' are found, and mostly connect to [[:Category:Common locations|common rooms]] such as [[classroom]]s, [[faculty room]]s and the [[Principal's office]].

==== Class doors ====
'''Class doors''', or '''blue doors'''<ref>"''You can only save and quit by exiting through the blue door in the Pitstop.''" - [https://steamcommunity.com/app/1275890/discussions/0/6895657216183953210/#c6895657216184012294 ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' Steam Community Hub]</ref>, are very common and considered the standard, and usually connect to classrooms where the player can find [[notebook]]s. All characters (except for Chalkles) can open blue doors. Blue doors also connect to the [[Principal's office]] in the [[Grapple Challenge]] in ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' and other games. All class doors have the recurring number [[99]], the number found and heard several times in the ''Baldi's Basics'' series.

==== Faculty doors ====
'''Faculty doors''' have a wooden appearance with the words "'''{{Font|School Faculty Only}}'''" written on them. They lead to faculty rooms where the player can find more useful items such as an [[Energy Flavored Zesty Bar]] or a [[Portal Poster]]. 

If the player enters, exits, or stays inside a faculty room while in [[Principal of the Thing]]'s sight, they will get scolded by him for breaking the [[school rule]] of entering a faculty room, unless the [[Faculty Nametag]] item is in action. However, simply opening the faculty door while the Principal is there does not count as a [[school rule]] violation.

==== Principal door ====
The '''Principal door''' is the door used for the [[Principal's office]]. It is a yellow door with the word "'''{{Font|Principal}}'''" on it. As with the supplies and placeholder doors, the Principal door opens on the right side. This door may be opened and shut at any time, although, during detention, the door may only be unlocked until the timer expires.

=== Unique doors ===
'''Unique doors''' are only found connected to [[:Category:Special locations|special]] or [[:Category:Hidden locations|hidden rooms]].

==== Supplies door ====
The '''supplies door''', or '''closet door'''{{Tt|*|Baldi's Basics Plus V0.3.4 changelog}}, is the door exclusively used on [[Gotta Sweep's closet]]. Like Principal and placeholder doors, the supplies door opens on the right side.

==== Dr. Reflex clinic door ====
The '''Dr. Reflex clinic door''', also known as the '''clinic door''', is a blue door used for [[Dr. Reflex's clinic]], exclusive to ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]''. However, instead of having 99, it features a red letter "'''{{Font|{{Color|#FF0000|H}}}}'''" on it, standing for Hospital.

==== Lab door ====
'''Lab doors''' are green doors used for [[teleporter room]]s, exclusive to ''Baldi's Basics Plus''. These doors have text that reads: "{{Font|Baldi's Lab}}".

==== Mystery door ====
The '''mystery door''' is a green door printed with multi-colored 3D question marks. It will appear as a part of the [[mystery room]] event.

==== Safe room door ====
The '''safe room door,''' is a blue door exclusive to ''Baldi's Basics Plus''. Instead of a 99, it has an image of Baldi's frowning face and a prohibition sign over it.

==== Baldi's Office door ====
The '''Baldi's Office door''' is exclusive for [[Baldi's Office]] in ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'', ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash|Birthday Bash]]'', and ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered|Classic Remastered]]''. The door has text that reads: "{{Font|Baldi's Office}}".

When opened in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', the opened section of the door for Baldi's Office is not transparent, and instead, distorted. Therefore, it looks like a stretched out image of the brick wall. The player can still pass through the door because the door collider is still on.

In ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'', the door appears normal when it is opened.

==== Placeholder door ====
'''Placeholder doors''' are blue doors with red "'''{{Font|{{Color|#FF0000|DOOR}}}}'''" text written vertically. These doors were used for [[debug room]]s, prior to [[V0.4 (Plus)|V0.4]]. Unlike the common doors, the door knob is attached to the left, so they open towards the right side.

== Text ==
The texts that are displayed in the respective door, or in any place of the door.

=== Common doors ===
* Class door | {{Font|{{Color|#000000|#1C12AB|99}}}}
* Faculty door | {{Font|{{Color|#000000|#E6A700|School Faculty Only}}}}
* Principal door | {{Font|{{Color|#000000|#DCED0B|Principal}}}}

=== Unique doors ===
* Supplies door | {{Font|{{Color|#000000|#FFA600|Supplies}}}}
* Dr. Reflex clinic door | {{Font|{{Color|#FF0000|#1C12AB|H}}}}
* Lab door | {{Font|{{Color|#000000|#00CF02|Baldi's Lab}}}}
* Mystery door | {{Font|{{Color|#000000|#00CF02|Mystery Room!}}}}
* Baldi's Office door | {{Font|{{Color|#000000|#00CF02|Baldi's Office}}}}
* Placeholder door | {{Font|{{Color|#FF0000|#0000FF|DOOR}}}}

== Development ==
* All doors are not models but just 2D textures with collision: there are two textures for each door (open and closed), with the exclusion of '''Swinging doors''', which have the locked texture, the dark open, closed, and locked swinging door texture, the coin door texture, the flap door texture, and the right and wrong way door texture, which totals 8 textures compared to the regular 2 for standard doors.
* The faculty doors are created by using a pre-existing pattern in GIMP.<ref>"''The window and faculty door textures were created using a preexisting pattern in GIMP.''" - Baldi's Basics in Education and Learning by [[mystman12]] for Meta Game Jam | [https://itch.io/jam/metagamejam/rate/239841 itch.io]</ref>
* The doors have the same image size as the ''[[Inspiration and reference#Sonic's Schoolhouse|Sonic's Schoolhouse]]'' door images.
* Prior to ''[[Baldi's Basics Kickstarter Exclusive Demo|Kickstarter Exclusive Demo]]'', each door's back texture is the same as its front's.
* The supplies, placeholder, and Principal's office doors are the doors to open towards the right side instead of the left.
* mystman12's office has a classroom door rather than its own door, due to mystman12 not making a texture for it.
* The open Baldi's Lab door texture it's shared with the open Baldi's Office door texture.
* Sometimes, the door has different textures on each side. This is intended to represent the type of room that is on the other side.<ref>"''Since doors can now lead to multiple types of rooms, I've made it so that the label represents the type of room that is on the other side.''" - [https://steamcommunity.com/app/1275890/discussions/0/2289464808481784973/?ctp=9#c2441461920662769254 ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' Steam Community Hub]</ref>
** The similar thing can happen in ''Classic Remastered''. However, only faculty rooms and Gotta Sweep's closet will have the classroom door on the back of it, where it connects to a hallway.
* [[File:2 doors next to each other.jpg|200px|right]]Rarely, 2 doors can be found next to each other. This can happen in any hallway area consisting of two long areas bisected by a wall, and with the room connecting to two locations.
* There are 15 doors altogether in the classic [[Schoolhouse]].
** However, if the door to Baldi's Office is counted, there will be 16 in total.
* There are 8 doors in the [[glitched school]].
* There are 4 doors in [[Baldi's Basement]], which are all faculty doors.
* There are 2 doors in [[mystman12's office]], one is a classroom door, and the other is a supplies door.
* There are 14 doors in ''Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo''.
* The door mask is 1 pixel lower than the actual door texture causing one pixel to be cut off the top.

'''Notebooks''' are the main [[:Category:Objective items|objective]] in the ''[[Baldi's Basics]]'' series that supposedly belong to [[Mentioned character#The player's friend|the player's friend]] in ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'' and ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered|Classic Remastered]]''.

== Description ==
The notebooks are spiral notebooks found in [[classroom]]s hovering on [[Model#Furniture|big desks]]. They come in various colors, which are, in the alphabetical order, black, blue, cyan, green, red, salmon, and yellow.

== Overview ==
=== ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]'' ===
==== Hide-and-Seek ====
[[File:NotebooInaDesck.png|thumb|A notebook seen in-game.]]
Once the player left-clicks on the notebook, it will be immediately added to the [[Item counter|notebook counter]]. Also, [[the player]]'s [[stamina]] does not refill, but Baldi will still go faster. The amount of notebooks required is changed based on the [[floor]]:
* [[Floor 1]]: 4 notebooks
* [[Floor 2]] and [[Floor 4|4]]: 7 notebooks
* [[Floor 3]] and [[Floor 5|5]]: 9 notebooks
Starting with Floor 2, the [[Math Machine]] can be found in the classrooms, and on Floor 3, the [[Balloon Buster]] and [[Match Machine]] activities can be found in classrooms. To get a notebook, the player must answer the problem correctly or incorrectly by using one of the [[balloon]]s floating around the room.

After collecting all the required notebooks, the [[elevator]]s will open up. The player must reach all of the elevators to complete the floor.

Notebooks award the player 10 [[You Thought Points]] when collected. Notebooks from the Math Machine, the Balloon Buster and the Match Machine do not award YTPs, however.
==== [[Endless Mode]] ====
Similar to [[Hide-and-Seek]], but there is no limit to the number of notebooks the player can collect, and Baldi does not speed up per notebook. After a certain amount of time, the notebooks will respawn in random rooms, and a chime plays to notify the player of their presence. If the classroom has Baldi's Math Machine, the player has to solve the [[math]] problem again in order to get a notebook. The player's final score will show up on the screen after getting caught by Baldi and it will appear on the leaderboard if the player scores enough points.

==== [[Challenge]]s ====
The number of notebooks in the challenges are different:
* [[Speedy Challenge]]: 25 notebooks
* [[Stealthy Challenge]] and [[Grapple Challenge]]: 7 notebooks

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' ===
==== Story Mode ====
[[File:ClassicMobile-iPhone5.png|thumb|right|A notebook seen in ''Baldi's Basics Classic''.]]The notebooks have different colors and are bigger. Left clicking or touching the {{Cursor}} button near the first notebook will open the [[You Can Think Pad]], which prompts the first 3 normal math problems that can be solved. However, the second notebook and onward will show an impossible and corrupted question that the player cannot solve. After the player gets all three problems wrong, Baldi will be attracted to the location where the notebook is.

When the player collects all seven notebooks (as long as they get 6 questions wrong), Baldi will become a bit faster than the player's running speed. At this time, [[Arts and Crafters]] will also become a new threat to the player. If looked at for more than a second, he will charge at the player and bring them straight to Baldi which puts the player at risk of a [[game over]].

However when collecting a notebook, it will refill/restore stamina to full, the new objective is to find all four [[exit]]s in any order to finish the game.

==== Endless Mode ====
The gameplay is almost the same as in ''Baldi's Basics Plus''. If the player finds a notebook in this mode, the third math problem in the 3rd notebook onwards can be solved, but Baldi will still accelerate while the player is trying to find them. When the player gets caught by Baldi, the number of notebooks they collected is their high score for Endless Mode if the player collected more than they did last time. The high score is displayed near the Endless Mode icon.

=== ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash]]'' ===
The original seven notebooks are in the same places as ''Baldi's Basics Classic''. 
{{SpoilerHide|
Two additional salmon notebooks are located in the [[glitched school]].
}}

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'' ===
The notebooks are found in the same spots in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', but the colors are randomized. In Classic Style and Party Style, Baldi is also attracted near the notebook when the player gets any problem incorrectly for the first time. 
{{SpoilerHide|
In [[NULL (style)|NULL/Glitch Style]], collecting a notebook will attract [[Null]] or the red {{C|[[Baldloon]]}} to the location where the player collects it.
}}

=== ''[[Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo]]'' ===
In this demo, there are 8 notebooks that the player needs to collect. All of the notebooks are colored green.

== Development ==
=== General ===
* The notebook respawning audio in Endless Mode is a slowed-down version of a crashing cymbal. In Music Maker, the sound effect is {{C|119 Reversed Cymbal}}, and it is edited with {{C|3F}}.
** It is also played in the [[Kickstarter]] video when [[mystman12]]'s face is closing up and he makes a frowning face. It is twice as long as the in-game audio.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Plus''/''Classic Remastered'' ===
* Notebooks are a lot smaller than in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash''.
* In the game files, there are 7 different notebook colors, but the black one is currently unused in the game.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' ===
* The color of the notebooks will change even if the player uses the same seed.
* In [[V0.14.4 (Plus)|V0.14.4]], the notebooks have an animator component attached to them. Via modding it is possible to re-enable this component, which plays an animation of a blue notebook spinning in place.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash'' ===
* [[File:Notebook under the school.png|200px|thumb|A notebook stored under the map along with It's a Bully using cheats.]]Every time a notebook is collected, it is stored beneath the map. This can be seen when hacking the game or when glitching outside of the Schoolhouse.<!-- The image was taken in an outdated version of Classic (prior to V1.4), so it should be updated. -->
** Notebooks can still be interacted with below the map, meaning that more than 7 notebooks can be collected in Story Mode.
* It is possible to hack the game to make the 3rd problem for the 2nd to 7th notebooks solvable.
** Only some of the things in the school are loaded until the player gets a question wrong.
* The salmon notebook on the Story Mode icon looks gray due to dithering.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Classic Remastered'' ===
* In the "'''{{Font|HOW TO PLAY}}'''" [[screen]] from the game menu screen, the word "notebooks" is misspelled as "noteboos".
** This typo can also be found when mystman12 graphed Baldi's ruler slapping interval.<ref>"''I always assumed it was on a curve, since the rate at which he increases his ruler slapping *is* on a curve, but I wasn't exactly sure what that looked like, what anger values corresponded to what average speed, etc. So I did some math and... DUN DUN DUN!''" - mystman12. July 14, 2020. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1283147132447358977 X/Twitter]</ref>

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' ===
* Sometimes, the notebook counter is set to 5/7 when loading the school scene in Unity.
* Each notebook was originally planned to include [[Cut subject|different subjects]] rather than just math, but due to the time limit, as well as the history and spelling subjects being considered too difficult for those who do not understand American history and/or the English language, the idea was cut.<ref>(1:04:11) "''I ran out of time to add more subjects initially and ultimately I've sort of decided against adding anything like history, anything like a logic base, just because I don't want to make it harder for people who don't know American history very well or don't know English, stuff like that, so I'm trying to keep it.''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|1:04:11|Livestream archive}}</ref> Localization for each country is the reason why those subjects are unlikely to be implemented.<ref>"''I don't plan on adding subjects like English or History because those would need a lot of work to be localized for different locations and languages.''" - mystman12. August 22, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1032378101286592512 X/Twitter]</ref> According to mystman12 during his stream, more subjects are still intended to be added for ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', although they will be logic-based instead of trivia-based.<ref>(1:04:28) "''In the full game, I'm gonna try to keep it like logic base stuff.''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|1:04:28|Livestream archive}}</ref>
** When hacking, the notebooks' supposedly used subjects are revealed as follows:
*** '''Geography''': Yellow notebook.
*** '''English''': Green notebook.
*** '''Spelling''': Blue notebook.
*** '''History''': Red notebook.
*** '''Math''': Salmon notebook.
*** '''Geology''': Cyan notebook.
*** '''Science''': Black notebook.

The '''You Can Think Pad''' is a device that appears throughout the ''[[Baldi's Basics]]'' franchise.

It is also the [[educational activity]] in the series, except for ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]''.

== Description ==
In ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]'', the You Can Think Pad is a green map tablet with an antenna, a red ellipsoid, and a black screen. This device shows the map, pause screen, and other mechanics.

In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'', ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash|Birthday Bash]]'' and ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered|Classic Remastered]]'', it appears as a green tablet on a black background with a purple ellipsoid on the top left corner of it. It has an antenna on the top right corner. However, in ''Classic Remastered'', when Baldi is present, there is a white background instead. In the top center, there is navy-colored text saying "'''{{Font|{{Color|#000080|You Can Think Pad}}}}'''."<!--Note: The reader can simply select the text to make it easier for them to see.--> On the bottom left corner, [[Baldi]] appears on a small screen on the bottom left, but once a problem is answered incorrectly, he makes an angry face and he does not show up when collecting the next [[notebook]]s. The "[[Everybody's Favorite Subject]]" track that plays during the first two sequences until a wrong answer is given is played in a two-second loop. There is also a green number pad on the right side with small purple buttons with numbers '''{{Font|0}}'''-'''{{Font|9}}''', the '''{{Font|C}}''' button to clear the answer entirely, the negative sign ('''{{Font|-}}'''), and a big circle button with "'''{{Font|OK!}}'''" text.

In ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'', the You Can Think Pad is more pixelated, the apex<!--also known as the top--> of the antenna is more red, and the "OK" button is smaller. This was done to fit within the native 480x360 resolution.
{{clear}}

== Functions ==
=== ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' ===
<gallery position="center">
Pause Menu Plus.png|The pause menu in the You Can Think Pad.
Advanced map.png|The advanced map in the You Can Think Pad.
YCTP back.png|The list of obtained stickers in the You Can Think Pad in Hide-and-Seek.
YCTP back Endless.png|Ditto, but in Endless Mode.
</gallery>The front of the You Can Think Pad displays mechanical features such as the advanced [[map]] and the [[pause menu]]. Both concepts were first introduced in [[V1.2 (Kickstarter Exclusive Demo)|V1.2]] of ''[[Baldi's Basics Kickstarter Exclusive Demo|Kickstarter Exclusive Demo]]''.

The back of the You Can Think Pad displays the list of [[Sticker|stickers]] upon turning it around. However, the player cannot change stickers here. The only way to change stickers is by using the [[Packet-O-Matic]].

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash''/''Classic Remastered'' ===
{{StructuredQuote
|text = Answer the three questions - correctly - and you might get something special!
|speaker = Baldi
|sound = BAL_Math_Intro3.wav
}}
[[File:ClassicRemastered4.png|thumb|The player using the You Can Think Pad to solve a math problem in ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered''.]]
The You Can Think Pad is portrayed as a tablet computer used to teach [[the player]] basic math, including but not limited to one-digit addition and subtraction.

When the player uses the You Can Think Pad, it has a question and an answer box, both of which are colored white. The answer box has text that says "'''{{Font|{{Color|#909090|ENTER ANSWER}}}}'''" in a light gray color, but it is not featured in ''Classic Remastered''. The player has to type an answer consisting of numbers only, and sometimes a hyphen (for negative numbers) into the empty box. If they solve the problem correctly by typing in the right answer (or clicking/touching the buttons on the number pad), they get a green check indicator on one of the three boxes on the left. If they answer incorrectly, they get a red cross indicator instead.

[[File:Impossible Question.png|thumb|The impossible question.]]
Upon getting all of the questions right in the first notebook, the You Can Think Pad will display "'''{{Font|WOW! YOU EXIST!}}'''". The last question of the first notebook is normal and can be solved, but the final question for the second notebook and every further notebook consists of jumbled numbers, making it impossible to answer correctly. However, in [[Endless Mode]], or if the [[Hard Mode]] [[Fun Setting]] is enabled, only the second notebook has the impossible question.[[File:YCTP Hard Mode.png|thumb]]When the player gets their first question wrong, the music will stop, and Baldi's smile will slowly turn into a frown, before displaying one of two messages on the You Can Think Pad. Both of these messages appear to be threats to scare the player, yet they also give hints to Baldi's behavior. The {{C|[[hang]]}} track will play in ''Classic''/''Birthday Bash''. If there is at least one question answered correct, it will either say "{{Font|I HEAR EVERY DOOR YOU OPEN}}" or "{{Font|I GET ANGRIER FOR EVERY PROBLEM YOU GET WRONG}}". After returning back to the building, Baldi starts chasing the player, and the real game begins.

Every time the player answers all questions in the You Can Think Pad, the game exits out of it after five seconds for first and second notebook. In ''Classic Remastered'', it takes two seconds to go back to the building when notebooks are collected after Baldi gets angry.

Failing all three questions will cause a unique message to show up, saying, "'''{{Font|I HEAR MATH THAT BAD}}'''". The message only appears in Story Mode, oddly enough. It will also make Baldi furious, moving abnormally fast for a few moments, before gradually slowing down to his normal speed (outside of Hard Mode).

The player can find a [[Secret code|secret]] by entering a specific number in the answer pad.

== Other appearances ==
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics - Field Trip demo: Camping]]'' and ''[[Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo]]'', the You Can Think Pad is unimplemented.
{{SpoilerHide|
* In ''Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash'' and Party Style of ''Classic Remastered'', the player can still use the You Can Think Pad from two extra notebooks in the [[glitched school]].
* [[File:Pad ClassicRemastered Glitch.png|thumb|The glitched You Can Think Pad in NULL/Glitch Style.]]In ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'', when the player gets a notebook in the [[NULL (style)|NULL/Glitch Style]], the You Can Think Pad glitches out, distorting the graphics inside of the 6 screens, and there are no math questions. Instead, it just shows the message. Also, the bottom left screen shows Null or the red {{C|Baldloon}} depending on the name of style.
** The checkmarks and X's represent each of the styles and their secret endings instead, if the player has not found a certain secret ending, a X will be displayed, top to bottom:
**# Classic Style, reached by getting all answers wrong and exiting the Schoolhouse.
**# Party Style, reached by using the Dangerous Teleporter after the Schoolhouse is darkened and heading to the Principal's office.
**# Demo Style, reached by using the Portal Poster on the corner of the room with the Safety Scissors poster. Party Style's normal ending gives a hint as to where the player needs to put it.
}}

== Trivia ==
=== General ===
* The You Can Think Pad might be a reference to [[Wikipedia:ThinkPad|Lenovo's ThinkPad]] business laptop and the [[Wikipedia:Casio ClassPad 300|Casio's ClassPad 300]] graphing calculator.
* The green check indicators are also used for a few [[option]]s in the options menu.
* The model of the You Can Think Pad was featured as one of the first [[Kickstarter#Pre-order store|digital goodies]].<ref>"''The classic You Can Think Pad model''" - [https://www.kickstarter.com/projects/193364415/baldis-basics-in-education-and-learning-full-game/posts/2910090 First Digital Goodies are now available!]</ref>

=== ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' ===
* The top left part and antenna's colors of the You Can Think Pad were swapped from their appearances in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash''.
* The You Can Think Pad's teaching feature was moved to the [[Math Machine]], as its purpose was changed in this game.<ref>"''The YCTP has been re-purposed as the device used to view the map and pause menu and stuff. All the math problems and other activities that must be completed to unlock notebooks no longer use a separate screen.''" - [https://steamcommunity.com/app/1275890/discussions/0/4738295219690522530/#c2274827383909738719 ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' Steam Community Hub]</ref>  
** The reason for doing this is pausing the game to solve math problems would break the flow and ruin the fun in the game.<ref>"''I've scrapped the idea of pausing the game to solve math problems, it just broke the flow too much and wasn't any fun. There will be more variety to the activities down the line (So not just math machines) but I don't plan on doing anything that requires stopping everything else.''" - [https://steamcommunity.com/app/1275890/discussions/0/2441461920662378593/#c2441461920662757019 ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' Steam Community Hub]</ref>
* Although [[You Thought Points]] originated from the You Can Think Pad,<ref>"''First off, the You Can Think Pads will get a new feature, the ability to award You Thought Points (YTPs)! Depending on how quickly you solve the problems they present, you'll earn more YTPs. ... There will be other ways to earn YTPs as well, such as in field trips, or even through some characters!''" - [https://www.kickstarter.com/projects/193364415/baldis-basics-in-education-and-learning-full-game/posts/2252327 Kickstarter Update #3 - One week in! More new features and creating characters!]</ref> the player cannot earn You Thought Points in this device because its function was changed when this point system was implemented.
* In the {{C|json}} file since [[V0.3.3 (Plus)|V0.3.3]], there is an unused quote that reads "'''{{Font|I can't remember what this one is supposed to say...}}'''". Also, the "'''{{Font|Keep up the good work or see me after class...}}'''" and "'''{{Font|I HEAR MATH THAT BAD}}'''" quotes are not included.
* Since [[V0.3.5 (Plus)|V0.3.5]], the You Can Think Pad no longer zooms in when using it.
* Prior to V0.8, the You Can Think Pad's advanced map layout was the same as the one in the pause menu.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash''/''Classic Remastered'' ===
* NPCs stop moving while the player uses the You Can Think Pad.<ref>"''Yeah... Baldi- Baldi stops moving when you're in the You Can Think Pad, so ...''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|38:36|Livestream archive 38:36-38:45}}</ref> However, they still move if the Hard Mode Fun Setting is enabled.
* mystman12 has confirmed that there currently is no true answer to the impossible question, although there might be one someday.<ref>"''Uh... just to... oh no.  ...clarify, there... there is still no... no true answer to the impossible question. But there might be. Someday.''" - mystman12, {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|28:04|Livestream archive 28:04-28:25}}</ref>

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash'' ===
* The secret codes can still be filled in even when the player has answered all three questions in each You Can Think Pad.
* By adjusting to the 4:3 aspect ratio, the You Can Think Pad can be seen in full view.
* The impossible questions were made by overlaying three layers of randomized equations on top of each other.<ref>"''It's actually just three layers of randomized numbers on top of each other.''" - mystman12. June 29, 2018. [https://twitter.com/mystman12/status/1012762145338085376 Twitter]</ref>

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' ===
* [[File:PrivacyPolicyUpdate.gif|thumb|right|A joke teaser under production of V1.3.]]While V1.3 was under production, a joke teaser shows the You Can Think Pad with the text "'''{{Font|We've updated our privacy policy.}}'''".
* The player can only put numbers in the answer, not letters and symbols (except for the hyphen used for negative numbers).
* Prior to [[V1.3 (Classic)|V1.3]], at the beginning of the first (and second, in most cases) notebooks, Baldi said the quotes about the subject and how to use the You Can Think Pad. However, since this update, this only applies to the first notebook the player collects. On the second notebook, these quotes are not used.
* Prior to [[V1.4 (Classic)|V1.4]], the You Can Think Pad had a few differences:
** It was possible to type decimal points/dots in the answer.
** The You Can Think Pad lacked the number pad from its right side.
** It had a trademark icon (<sup>TM</sup>) at the end of its name: "'''{{Font|{{Color|Purple|#FFFFFF|You Can Think Pad<sup>TM</sup>}}}}'''".
*** The title's font color was purple rather than navy.
** The texts and marks were not pixelated.
* In [[V1.4.1d (Classic)|V1.4.1d]], the "'''{{Font|OK!}}'''" button could be pressed, even though the answer box is empty.
** Since [[V1.4.2 (Classic)|V1.4.2]], on mobile versions, it can now only be pressed when the answer box is filled. This was done to prevent the player from accidentally inputting a wrong answer while spam clicking the {{Cursor}} button to collect notebooks.<ref>"''"OK" button can no longer be pressed while the answer box is empty. This is to prevent a wrong answer being accidentally input when mashing the interact button to pick up a notebook.''" - mystman12. March 20, 2019. [https://basically-games.itch.io/baldis-basics/devlog/72633/android-142-update itch.io]</ref>

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'' ===
* [[File:Correctly wrong.png|thumb]]Even though answering all three problems correctly in the third or further notebooks when Hard Mode Fun Setting is enabled, the You Can Think Pad still says a message when answering one or two problems incorrectly without this Fun Setting. This is because Baldi still gets angrier in that case.<ref>"''Since Baldi doesn't cool down in hard mode if you get all three problems correct (Unlike in endless mode where he does) I didn't want to change the text and possibly imply he is cooling down. Getting any of the three problems wrong will make him extra angry though so be sure to get them right!''" - [https://steamcommunity.com/app/1712830/discussions/0/3485249057158268761/?ctp=7#c3464983493947112953 ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'' Steam Community Hub]</ref>
* There is a 1 in 513 (0.1949...%) chance that Baldi will appear and open his mouth slowly while a 1000&nbsp;Hz tone plays on the YCTP. This is the lip-sync test, originally used to test Baldi's lips syncing to audio. It was thrown in as an Easter egg.
{{SpoilerHide|
* One of the quotes in Glitch Style, "'''{{Font|C0M3 B@CK WhEN UR @ L!T7LE MMMMM-}}'''" ("'''{{Font|COME BACK WHEN YOU'RE A LITTLE MMMMM-}}'''") is a reference to Morshu from ''[[Inspiration and reference#Link: The Faces of Evil|Link: The Faces of Evil]]''.
}}

== Glitches ==
=== ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' ===
* [[File:Plain pause menu.png|thumb]]Since V0.3.5, if the player keeps the game paused for a long time, the floating Baldi heads will go away.
{{clear}}
=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' ===
* [[File:Incorrect quote.png|thumb|The "'''{{Font|WOW! YOU EXIST!}}'''" text showing despite a problem being answered incorrectly.]][[File:Baldi YCTP Classic V1.0.png|thumb|right|The text overlapping the impossible question.]] In the [[V1.0 (Classic)|first version]], the "'''{{Font|WOW! YOU EXIST!}}'''" text will always display after completing the third problem regardless of any being incorrectly answered. This also carries over to the impossible question, where upon completion, the text overlaps the problem.
* In V1.4.1d, the first Android version, the game would sometimes pause after leaving the You Can Think Pad.
* Since [[V1.3 (Classic)|V1.3]], if the player answers incorrectly on the first or second questions in the first notebook but gets the third question right, the speed of Baldi's slap will glitch and go super-fast.
{{clear}}  
=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'' ===
* [[File:Whiteout jumpscare.png|thumb]]When the player gets caught by Baldi while using You Can Think Pad with Hard Mode, the screen becomes completely white.
* Prior to [[V1.1 (Classic Remastered)|V1.1]], the answer would be marked wrong even if it was entered correctly, if the mouse was over one of the buttons on the number pad, where pressing the {{Key|Enter}} key acted as pressing it. This glitch was fixed by disabling the mouse cursor while submitting an answer.

{{Quote|To help him out, you have to go back in the school and find all his notebooks for him.|"'''HOW TO PLAY'''" story screen|to the player, ''Baldi's Basics Classic''}}
'''The player''' is the unidentifiable main protagonist in the ''[[Baldi's Basics]]'' series. Due to the fact that the player is represented as the person themselves with no revealed identity (gender, name, species, etc.), the [[character]]s constantly do [[fourth wall break]]s when speaking to them. The player takes the role of a student of the [[Schoolhouse]] or [[Super Schoolhouse]] to retrieve all [[notebook]]s while avoiding [[Baldi]].

The player actively keeps track of how many notebooks they have at any given time with diligence as it is their main mission, indicated by the [[Item counter|notebook counter]] located on the top left corner. Their [[inventory]] on the top right keeps track of the [[item]]s, depending on the game, they currently have in their possession. There is a [[stamina]] meter located at the bottom left corner. In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'' and ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash]]'', the inventory can only store three items, and the stamina meter is located at the top center between the two others [[:Category:HUDs|HUDs]].

== Description ==
The game is played from a first-person perspective, so the player's external appearance is never shown. They see from slightly below Baldi's eye level, indicating that they are shorter than him by default as if they are young.

Ingame, they resemble a tall grey cylinder, however this is never shown due to the game being first-person.

[[Principal of the Thing]] gives hints that they are a child, as when sending them to [[Principal's office|detention]], he says that they should know better and shouldn't make him send them to detention again, but still insists on telling [[Mentioned character#The player's parents|their parents]] about what they did, even for a small infraction such as running in the [[Hallway|halls]].

[[1st Prize]] claims to be programmed to desire their image and will chase them relentlessly in a prolonged effort to hug or push them.

Curiously, the player's movement lacks view bobbing, potentially identifying that they slide around similar to most other characters.

The player is only heard making a direct sound four times. They are heard drinking from [[water fountain]]s, biting into an [[Energy Flavored Zesty Bar]] or gulping the [[Invisibility Elixir]] in ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]'', and in ''Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash'' and Party Style of ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered]]'', they are also heard blowing the [[Cake|birthday cake]]'s candle out.

The player can also make some of the items' sounds upon using them, such as the [[BSODA]]/[[Diet BSODA]], the [[Swinging Door Lock]], etc.

== Personality ==
The player is excellent at quick mental subjects such as addition and subtraction in [[math]] problems. However, they fail to solve more complex problems either because those problems are genuinely impossible, or they simply cannot understand how to solve them.

The player's morality can be shaped through their actions—for example, while it is not necessary to cut [[Playtime]]'s jump rope (which is considered a rude action but temporarily thwarts Playtime being an obstacle), the player could engage in such an action without hesitation. Given that the [[Safety Scissors]] has no other friendly uses, it could be implied that the player would always grab and use them for their purpose of cutting Playtime's jump rope, or some other similarly malicious action such as cutting 1st Prize's wiring. However, performing such an action grants the player extra time to evade Baldi, resulting in a greater chance of survival, albeit at the cost of someone else. Therefore, it can be assumed that everything questionable that the player does or may do is in the spirit of evading Baldi and not being a bully.

Although perhaps the player is only doing it for the items or [[You Thought Points]] they can obtain, the player goes on [[field trip]]s with Baldi and helps other [[student]]s find their [[Lost Item]]s, so it seems that they like to at least being social and help others.

They seem to lack many of the human characteristics that would typically make such a scenario as ''Baldi's Basics'' impossible, such as fear or shock that would typically faze any other person too much to engage in Baldi's scenario efficiently enough to survive.

It can be assumed that the player is not very assertive or strong, as the player is unable to circumvent hazards that other characters bring, such as being caught by Baldi, being stuck jumping rope with Playtime, failing to walk past [[It's a Bully]] without any items, being caught breaking the [[school rule]]s by Principal of the Thing, being pushed with ease by [[Gotta Sweep]] or 1st Prize, getting caught in [[Beans]]' gum, slipping on a [['Nana Peel]], being told to get to [[Mrs. Pomp]]'s [[classroom]] without being late and then getting dragged by her or being forced to do [[Dr. Reflex]]'s reflex test and then getting squished by his hammer. Furthermore, their generosity and lack of assertiveness is shown by the fact that they are willing to stay in [[Stealthy Challenge|school after hours]].

{{SpoilerHide|
The player also seems to be a very curious and determined person, because the player managed to discover all the secrets in ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'', despite Null's warnings not to continue playing.

Despite the lack of assertiveness and strength mentioned above, the player managed to beat [[Null]] in the [[NULL (style)|NULL]] style by throwing objects and running very fast, this probably happened because the player broke the game by making it do things that were not supposed to happen, just like Null also broke the game when he closed the last exit.

The player also appears to be a prankster, because according to [[NINENINE secret]], on April 17, 2019, they prank called a distant relative of a character named “[[Mentioned character|Lord Bumbo]].”
}}

== Role ==
=== ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]'' ===
As the player enters the school, they are greeted by Baldi standing in front of the [[elevator]]. The player has to find classrooms which contain a notebook each. When Baldi finishes his countdown, he will start chasing the player. After the player has succeeded to collect all the notebooks, all elevators are open. The player has to escape by going through all elevators depending on the [[floor]] they are on while trying to avoid Baldi in order to complete the game.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash''/''Classic Remastered'' ===
When collecting a notebook, the [[You Can Think Pad]] will appear for the player to answer math questions. After the player answers all of the questions correctly in the first notebook, Baldi will give them a [[Quarter]] ([[Present]] in ''Birthday Bash'' and Party Style of ''Classic Remastered''). But as soon as the player views the third question of the second notebook, it shows a distorted math question, as [[mystman12]] designed the game in such a way that the player would always fail the third question from the second to seventh notebook. The first notebook does not have that question. Instead of elevators, the player has to go to four [[exit]]s. Each exit disappears, showing a map indicating where the player is until the fourth exit, which they have to go through in order to escape the school. However, in ''Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash'' and Party Style of ''Classic Remastered'', instead of getting out of the Schoolhouse, all characters greet them as a surprise party instead.

In [[Demo Style]] of ''Classic Remastered'', the player can get a notebook from the [[Math Machine]] after answering a math problem. In this style, only the second problem is impossible to solve as it is always glitched out.

In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Classic Remastered'', [[Mentioned character#The player's friend|the player's friend]] requests them to head back to the Schoolhouse to navigate the notebooks for him.

{{SpoilerHide|
Possibly after following Null's message to "do worse though" in Classic Style, the player finds Null in Baldi's Office, and despite what Null said, the player decides to continue playing the game. After finding Null in Party Style and Demo Style and beating the game with all the Fun Settings activated, the player faces Null in the [[NULL (style)|NULL]] style. The player manages to get all the notebooks, but when the player was about to escape, Null closes the last exit, something that was not supposed to happen. In spite of this, the player decides to throw objects to Null to be able to face him, something that the player achieves, and Null ends up disappearing in [[mystman12's office]].
}}

== Development ==
* mystman12 jokingly referred the player as "everyone's favorite character", naming them "Capsule Collider" after the model's name.<ref>"''That's right, it's everyone's favorite character, Capsule Collider!''" - mystman12. April 3, 2019. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1113597868668014592 X/Twitter]</ref>
* There will be no other playable characters.<ref>"''No, there won't be any selectable characters or anything.''" - [https://www.kickstarter.com/projects/193364415/baldis-basics-in-education-and-learning-full-game/comments?comment=Q29tbWVudC0yMzQ1NTgzMA%3D%3D&reply=Q29tbWVudC0yMzQ3NTA4Mg%3D%3D Kickstarter comment]</ref>
* The cylinder model is the only model for the player as of October 8, 2022.<ref>Q: "''Does the player have Any sort of Character design? or is it just a cylinder?''" / A: "''Currently just a cylinder haha.''" - [[:File:Gmail questions 20221008.png|Gmail (question)]], [[:File:Gmail response 20221008.png|Gmail (response)]]</ref>
* The player's icon on the [[map]] is shown as a white triangle, which was first seen in the ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' development footage from mystman12.<ref>"''Working on the full map screen for #BaldisBasics! #ScreenshotSaturday''" - mystman12. February 22, 2020. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1231304355124563969 X/Twitter]</ref>
** There is another arrow on the bottom left at 0:20 in the video. mystman12 confirmed that it was a leftover from [[Multiplayer|multiplayer mode]].<ref>"''The second arrow is a remnant of multiplayer though! I've been programming the game with multiplayer in mind, so that when/if I do decide to add it it won't be a huge pain. That said it will still be a big challenge and I don't have plans to add it any time soon.''" - mystman12's [https://www.kickstarter.com/projects/193364415/baldis-basics-in-education-and-learning-full-game/comments?comment=Q29tbWVudC0yNzU4NTAzNw%3D%3D&reply=Q29tbWVudC0yNzY1MDMyMw%3D%3D Kickstarter comment]</ref>
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics in Education and Learning: Development Build]]'', the player's movement seems to be more ice-like because it is not using some sort of rounded speed as the normal game does. The reason for the player's strange movements and varying speed seems to be due to the game being played with a controller.

== [[Audio]] ==
=== ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' ===

[[vi:The player]]
[[Category:Characters]]\n\n======== Exit len 13264 ========\n{{DISPLAYTITLE:Exits}}
{{Baldi's Location Template
|image = Exit ClassicRemastered.png
|inhabits = [[The player]]
|structures = 
* [[Swinging door]]
* [[Baldi's Bus]]{{Tt|*|Classic Remastered}}
|aka = Entrance{{Tt|*|mystman12's Twitter/X image of the Schoolhouse map legend}}
|added = ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'' {{S|([[V1.0 (Classic)|V1.0]])}}
}}
'''Exits''' are the four areas that are [[the player]]'s only means of escaping the [[Schoolhouse]]. They are featured in ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'', ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash]]'', and ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered]]''.

== Description ==
Each exit has two [[Sprite#Room decorations|potted plants]], two [[Texture#Window textures|windows]], and hall lights attached to the [[Texture#Ceiling textures|ceiling]].

In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'' there is a hanging sign with red "'''{{Font|Impact|{{Color|#FE0100|EXIT}}}}'''" text. In ''Classic Remastered'', the "{{EXIT}}" text is handwritten and it will [[Lighting|glow]] when the Schoolhouse gets darkened.

== Overview ==
=== Main gameplay ===
[[File:RedHallway.jpg|thumb|right|The hallway after reaching the first exit.]]There are four exits in the Schoolhouse. One at the starting point, two in the other [[hallway]]s, and one in the [[cafeteria]]. The exits appear visible. They will be blocked when the player answers a [[math]] problem wrong.

Once the player collects all 7 [[notebook]]s, the wall will reappear, after the exits reappear itself. Heading to one of the fake exit walls appears and blocks it off, a quiet ambience will start playing, and the entire school becomes [[Red light|red]] and gets dark shadings. Heading to another fake exit will replaced by the wall and makes a loud static noise until the player gets caught by [[Baldi]] or wins. 

The player must get three of four exits to trigger a real exit, no matter where it is. When the player reaches the exits except for the last one, they will change into wall-like [[map]]s, showing a layout of the Schoolhouse.

[[File:Global-exit.png|thumb]]
The player must go to all four exits in any order to win the game. When the player escapes via the fourth exit, a certain [[ending]] will appear, depending on the number of [[math]] questions the player answered incorrectly.

There is an exit that does not work. This exit is next to a large [[Sprite#Room materials|globe]] in a [[faculty room]] with a [[Swinging Door Lock]], but there is only a sign above the globe and no doors. Reaching this exit does not make the player progress in the game. This was explained by [[mystman12]] on why there was an exit sign there in the episode 2 of the podcasts. <ref>''"So, the globe with the exit sign is a good example of what happens when you gotta finish a game soon, and you gotta use what you have, and you gotta try and make something interesting out of it. So that was literally just me- I don't know if I have ever mentioned this before, but when I was first finishing Baldi's Basics Classic in those final hours, I wanna say like... eight hours before the deadline, the school was completely barren, the level layout was complete, but I didn't have any furniture placed pretty much everywhere, and so within those last hours, I was scrambling to just... put stuff down to make the school look somewhat "lived in"...''-mystman12, [https://www.patreon.com/posts/basically-games-120473987 Basically Games Podcast: Episode 2 1:06:15-1:07:38]</ref>

=== Other appearances ===
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]'' and [[:Category:Demos|demos]], the exits are replaced by [[elevator]]s.
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'', the player cannot go through the [[swinging door]], the red light does not affect [[character]]s, [[item]]s, nor sprite objects, and it does not consist of dark shadings. Also, the exit signs have a different design. Heading to the second fake exit makes an increasingly loud bass-boosted noise.
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'', the exits work similarly in ''Classic''/''Birthday Bash'', but the ambient noises after reaching the exit are different. When playing any style with the [[Explorer|Free Run]] [[Fun Setting]], all exits are not hidden all the time. Even if the player collects 7 notebooks, exits will not be closed when they try to come in contact with them, but instead they can enter the outdoor area by opening a swinging door. Some NPCs like Baldi or Playtime will stop moving if the player is on 3 [[tile]]s in front of the swinging door.
* If [[Arts and Crafters]] has caught you and you are on the 3 [[Tile|tiles]] of any exit, he will still teleport you. (This does not apply to Classic,Birthday Bash or Plus)
{{SpoilerHide|* [[File:Exit-tilt.png|thumb]]In ''Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash'', the exit from the cafeteria will become accessible when triggering the other three exits in hallways. This also applies to Party Style in ''Classic Remastered''. In a faculty room from the [[glitched school]], there is a minimized and tilted exit connected with the [[outdoor area]] along with a wall above that blocks it. Other mechanisms are same as in ''Baldi's Basics Classic''.}}

== Trivia ==
=== General ===
* [[File:Floating sign.png|thumb|The floating exit sign next to the cafeteria exit.]]All of the exits in the game feature two plants, two windows, two walls, one floor, one fake swinging door, one exit sign, and the same type of ceiling. Due to the fact all exits have an exit sign, it means a floating exit sign can be spotted in the cafeteria, as it is not connected to a ceiling.
* In [[Endless Mode]], the exits can only be seen before the player answers a problem wrong. They do not reappear even after collecting 7 notebooks.
* Since ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' [[V1.2.2 (Classic)|V1.2.2]], the maps for the fake exits are outdated, as the hallway with the [[pay phone]] is notably longer than it currently is.
** This inconsistency has carried over to ''Classic Remastered''.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' ===
* Since [[V0.6 (Plus)|V0.6]], an exit sign is added in [[The Pit Stop]].
* Since [[V0.8 (Plus)|V0.8]], elevators use the same decorations as exits.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash'' ===
* The sound the first exit location makes when disappearing is called "{{C|MEC_SLINGSHOT}}". According to [[mystman12]]'s Twitter/X,<ref>"''Look at a redesigned, much more reliable bus lock in #JoesUltimateBusRide! Oh yeah I'm still working on Joe's Ultimate Bus Ride btw. :P Actually, earlier this year I deleted nearly all my code and have started from scratch. The old code was a mess, since it spanned many...''" - mystman12. December 18, 2017. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/942922888339972096 Twitter/X]</ref><ref>"''Those would all be cool features for sure! Fun fact though, I never actually considered putting a slingshot in until LostBits video mentioned it. The file named "MEC_SLINGSHOT" was actually a sound used for the slingshots in the pinball game I've been working on.''" - mystman12. June 14, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1007455815974965249 Twitter/X]</ref> this sound was intended to be used in a [[Joe's Ultimate Bus Ride|pinball game]] he was working on.
* The ambience that plays after reaching the first exit originates from a [[:File:Quiet noise found media.wav|''Donkey Kong Country 2'' soundbyte]].
* By enabling all [[:Category:Schoolhouse characters|characters]] in the beginning of the game by hacking, it is revealed that they cannot move to the exit areas except for It's a Bully. This is due to the exit areas not having a walkable {{C|[[NavMesh]]}} for those characters.
* [[File:Exit-block.png|thumb|The blocked exit.]]Exits have swinging doors but they function differently and are purely for aesthetic reasons besides detecting collision once the player has triggered 3 exits.[[File:Exit-unblocked.png|thumb]]
* Through hacking in both [[mode]]s, the exits are found underneath the map, as well as the large walls that block them.
** Through hacking, if touching these exits below the map, they will go down 1 more tile, basically opening the blocked exit. The exit does not go up 1 more tile even though it is opened.
{{clear}}
=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' ===
* [[File:Original drawing map of Baldi's Basics Classic.jpg|thumb|There's only 1 exit in the original drawing.]]In the original drawing, there is only one exit marked in red.<ref>"''One of the first things I did when working on the original version of #BaldisBasics was draw up the layout of the school. Here's the original drawing! While it's mostly the same, it has a few notable differences, like a playground area that never made it into the game.''" - mystman12. March 20, 2019. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1108518104324337671 Twitter/X]</ref> But in the game there's 4 exits.
* [[File:Classic V1.0 no exit map.png|thumb]]<!--High quality image needed.-->Prior to [[V1.0 (Classic)|V1.0]] to [[V1.2.1 (Classic)|V1.2.1]], there were no maps for the fake exits.
{{clear}}
=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'' ===
* The exits being blocked function somewhat differently than they did in ''Classic''/''Birthday Bash'', as walls are added in front of the exits instead of the exit being shifted downwards. Another change includes the exits having less range to be triggered, which means the player cannot utilize [[1st Prize]] to trigger them, and must be walked up to manually.
{{SpoilerHide|
* In [[NULL (style)|NULL/Glitch Style]], the player cannot get in the last exit as it is blocked by invisible walls. Eventually, [[Null]]/the red {{C|[[Baldloon]]}} closes this exit first.
* [[File:Null-exitbrokenwindow.png|thumb|The broken window of an exit.]]The windows of the exits can be broken through hacks, if the player can somehow move Null or the red {{C|Baldloon}} through the exits.
* [[File:Outof-elevate.png|thumb]]The east exit has an elevator gate above it, which can be seen by using debug mode and no-clipping out of the map. It can also be seen very clearly when the red {{C|Baldloon}}s start erasing the glitched school. This gate does not do anything when this exit is triggered, so its purpose is currently unknown.
}}
{{clear}}

[[zh:出口]]\n\n======== Game_over len 14623 ========\n{{Other uses|Game}}
{{StructuredQuote
|text = Game over!
|speaker = Baldi
|sound = BAL_GameOver.wav
}}
A '''game over''' happens in the ''[[Baldi's Basics]]'' series when [[Baldi]] catches [[the player]] in the [[Schoolhouse]] or when the player runs out of [[power tube]]s in the [[Super Schoolhouse]].

== Description ==
=== ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]'' ===
==== [[Hide-and-Seek]] ====
When the player gets caught by Baldi, the game will take them back to the [[elevator]], making them try the [[floor]] they are currently in again. Along with that, they will lose a power tube. If no tubes are left, the game brings the player back to the main menu [[screen]].

==== [[Field trip]]s ====
The player will fail the [[minigame]] when the [[campfire]] completely goes out in [[Campfire Frenzy]] or when the player gets three strikes in [[Pick-Quick-Nic]].

==== [[Endless Mode]] ====
The player only has one power tube, but instead of leading to the main menu screen, the game will display a screen for the results.

==== [[Challenge]]s ====
The player only has one power tube and they will be sent to the main menu screen when caught by Baldi.

==== [[Tutorial]] ====
Baldi cannot cause a game over, making it absent in this mode.

=== ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'' ===
[[File:DeathScreenSheet.png|thumb|The Death screen objects]]
There are a total of 6 images for the game over screen, unlike in ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', which has no specific game over screen. A random image will display when Baldi catches the player before sending them back to the menu screen; a blue fidget spinner, a glasses case, pink scissors, a telescope, and a music box.

{{SpoilerHide|There is a 1 in 99 (1.010101...%) chance that a small, orange rectangle with the number "[[99]]" half-cropped away to look like a pair of eyeballs will appear after Baldi catches the player. After a couple of seconds, a jumpscare will occur with the half cropped away 99 box now in a reddish hue appearing close to the screen along with loud distorted noises. The game crashes five seconds after the jumpscare.

All of the objects seen in the game over screens are not dithered, unlike most of the others in the rest of the game, except for the cropped "99" box.
<gallery position="center">
Rare jumpscare.png
</gallery>}}

Also, a screeching noise would play while the player gets caught. However, this audio was replaced by other random loud audios since ''Full Game Early Demo'' for unknown reasons.

=== ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash]]'' ===
The game over screen is the same as ''Baldi's Basics Classic''.

{{SpoilerHide|However, unlike Baldi's Basics Classic, the cropped "99" box will not appear in this game. However, other game over screens will rarely have a red-colored jumpscare before causing the game to crash like when the cropped "99" box appears in Baldi's Basics Classic. The jumpscare also has a different audio.
<gallery position="center">
Red 2.png
Red 3.png
</gallery>}}

{{clear}}
=== ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered]]'' ===
[[File:Classic Remastered rare game over.png|thumb]]
Unlike in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', the images for game over screen will not appear after the player is caught by Baldi, and the player will simply get sent back to the menu. In place of this however, there is a rare chance that the game will load a fake error screen with a startling noise saying:
{{clear}}
<center><poem>
{{Font|Comic Sans MS|{{Color|#FF0000|'''Oh no!'''

'''A critical error has <br>{{Tt|occured|occurred}}!'''

'''The game will now close.'''
}}}}
</poem></center>

After a few seconds, the game closes automatically.
{{SpoilerHide|
However, after [[Null]] in the [[NULL (style)|NULL]] style is defeated, when this error screen appears, instead of closing the game, it will stay up for a few seconds until suddenly, the game begins to corrupt. A loud noise will play while Baldi says "''Welcome to Baldi's Basics in Education and Learning! That's me!''" in a random speed. The error text is glitched out. The crash background will distort with random colors and/or zoom out to the point it looks like static.

After some time, the background will turn dark and start to flicker, the text will disappear, and random snippets of midi instruments and audio clips will play in random speeds and pitches, and sometimes stutter. Then, a photograph of Null by a [[lake]] is seen afterwards, accompanied by a loud noise and tone. After 15 seconds, the photo will then change with Null's face and arms appearing in front of the screen screaming as he disintegrates. After that, the photo will slowly disappear piece by piece, and the game will close. It is noticeable that after the image completely disappears, a heavily darkened {{C|[[PlaceFace]]}} is hiding in the background, making it difficult to see. After that, the game finally crashes. This all only happens once.
<gallery position="center">
Distorted error.png
Fake Error Grainy.png
Nulldi.png
Nullscream.png
</gallery>

In this game, Null/the red {{C|[[Baldloon]]}} in NULL/Glitch Style can cause a game over, but they will do a "jumpscare sequence" and close the game, forcing the player to start the game from opening a program again if they want another shot at this style. The sequence will also happen when the player moves their cursor over the 'Yes' option in the quit menu after collecting at least one notebook or, alternatively, if the player hovers the mouse over the question mark that appears after enabling [[debug mode]] on the [[pause menu]]. However, these extra jumpscare conditions are exclusive to the NULL style and thus will not occur in Glitch Style.
}}

=== ''[[Baldi's Basics - Field Trip demo: Camping]]'' ===
There is no power tube system. Instead, the game will bring the player to the [[Kickstarter]] information screen.

=== ''[[Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo]]'' / ''[[Baldi's Basics - Challenges Demo]]'' ===
There is also no power tube system. The player will immediately go back to the title screen when caught.
{{clear}}

== Trivia ==
=== General ===
* [[File:Baldi's jumpscare Classic.png|thumb|Baldi's jumpscare.]]It's unconfirmed whether Baldi kills or simply hurts the player during the game over sequence, leaving the fate of the player unknown.
** In the {{YouTube|CS3deEpP4SM||Kickstarter promotion video}}, it is simply stated that "''If he touches you, it's game over!''".
** It can be intuited through the [[broken ruler]] event that Baldi does something to the player with the ruler when he catches them, possibly hit them, because without the ruler, Baldi cannot catch the player.
** According to the cut Tutorial tape recorder, ''"If he reaches you that's the end.. of.. everything".''
** However, in ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]'', Baldi makes it very clear he has no evil intentions and simply lets the player go upon catching them.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash'' ===
* The music box is actually a [https://www.musical-movement.net/products/YUNSHENG-HAND-CRANKED-MUSICAL-MOVEMENT-2515492.html real life product] of the brand Yunsheng.
* The objects in the game over screen have better quality than anything else in the game. The "99" game over screen in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' does not count towards this.
* [[File:Where's 4 1?.png|thumb]]In the Unity project file, there appears to be a missing {{C|4_1}} game over screen as it jumps directly from {{C|3_1}} to {{C|5_1}}. A {{C|4_1}} screen was scrapped, confirmed by the files of the [[March 30th Build 1 (Classic)|March 30th Build]].

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' ===
* [[File:99origin.png|thumb]]The "99" game over screen originally came from a ''[[Inspiration and reference#Donkey Kong Country|Donkey Kong Country]]'' corruption. There is the similar image as this game over screen at the bottom right.<ref>"''Thank you for changing my life.''" - mystman12. September 9, 2023. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1700665529961234906 Twitter/X]</ref>
* The "99" game over sound effect actually plays twice before the game crashes, as the audio is actually looped; though it gets cut off almost immediately.
* The pink scissors game over screen is similar to [[Safety Scissors]].

=== ''Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash'' ===
* The jumpscare sound is actually ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' V1.3.2's {{C|.exe}} file imported into Audacity as raw data and encoded with VOX ADPCM at a sample rate of 44100 Hz.
** This jumpscare was made by accident.<ref>"''I didn't mean to do that. it was actually an accident... The best kind of accident! :P''" - mystman12. June 11, 2019. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1138460790258880517 Twitter/X]</ref>

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'' ===
* Even though the ''Classic'' game over screens do not appear in this game, the objects that are featured in them appear on the [[conspiracy board]] in [[Baldi's Basement]].
* The loop used for the error screen's music is a modified version of the Korg Wavestation preset "NutPanBeat" played in F#2 key in octave 2 at [[99]] BPM.

== Glitches ==
=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'' ===
* The sound in the fake error screen is not affected by audio settings.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' ===

* There is a bug that where jumpscare sound effect can overlap in some old versions.

{{Quote|Truly an incredible sock puppet, I've never seen one like it!|Former description|office poster}}
'''Arts and Crafters''' is the large sock puppet of the [[Schoolhouse]] and [[Super Schoolhouse]].

== Physical description ==
Arts and Crafters is a white and gray long-sleeved sock puppet sporting googly eyes (the right eye being bigger than the left as well as reflecting the room if looking closely), a black mouth, and a red tongue made out of cardboard. The black mouth is only slightly visible when his mouth is closed, and his tongue can only be seen when he opens his mouth. If observed closely, there is a slightly off-color part on the top of his head, possibly covering a brand name. Arts and Crafters is being worn by a right hand.

If looking carefully at Arts and Crafters when attacking at the player, the hand inside of him appears more vividly, grasping to make his mouth open.

== Personality ==
Arts and Crafters seems to be very shy at first, preferring to stay by himself and avoiding other people, seen by how he flees from the [[player]]'s sight when spotted in hallways. However, after the player collects all the [[notebook]]s on the [[floor]], he ultimately becomes more aggressive, starting to run after the player out of jealousy for having more notebooks than himself when the player sees him. He does not emit any audio containing words but only makes a noise resembling a static sound.

Arts and Crafters can be inferred to be an artist or a scrapbook writer, as he has a lot of notebooks, and his jealousy could stem from the thought that because the player has so many notebooks, they are more prolific than him in his field of work.

== Artificial intelligence ==
=== Main gameplay ===
When the player begins the game, Arts and Crafters will spawn in one of the rooms and avoid being seen by the player. During gameplay, he'll teleport around to random [[hallway]]s, watching the player for a few seconds before fleeing away from them and disappearing again. In this state, Arts and Crafters is completely harmless and does not affect gameplay in any noticeable way. The player will more commonly find him at the end of a hallway, but he can also be found in a room in rare occasions.

<gallery position="center">
Crafters charging at the player.png|Arts and Crafters charging towards the player in ''Baldi's Basics Plus''.
Crafters teleporting progress.png|Arts and Crafters rotating around the player before teleporting them.
</gallery>
However, once the player collects all the notebooks in [[Hide-and-Seek]] or the certain amount (4, 7, or 9, depending on the level size) or more of notebooks in [[Endless Mode]], Arts and Crafters will become hostile towards the player. If the player makes eye contact with Arts and Crafters for more than one second, he will charge at the player at an increasingly high speed, emitting a loud static-like roaring noise. If the player comes in contact with Arts and Crafters in this phase, he will begin quickly orbiting around them, appearing to duplicate into five, and getting faster. After 10 seconds of "attacking", he teleports both [[Baldi]] and the player in front of a random elevator, Baldi appearing 5 [[tile]]s behind the player. Upon teleporting, Baldi will be attracted to the player's teleporting spot, no matter what noises played before. After teleporting the player, Arts and Crafters will disappear, remaining invisible for the rest of the player's time on the floor.

Arts and Crafters can move in some cases:
* Heading off to the [[Principal's office]] for the [[party]] [[event]].
* Being pushed by [[Gotta Sweep]] or [[Cloudy Copter]].
* Being sucked up by a [[Flood|whirlpool]].
* While the [[tape player]] or the [[pay phone]] makes a noise.

=== [[Field trip]]s ===
[[File:Arts and Crafters in camping.png|200px|right]]Arts and Crafters appeared in the [[camping]] field trip until [[V0.3 (Plus)|V0.3]] of ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]''. Arts and Crafters would only start attacking the player if they wandered behind the [[Sprite#Camping 2|"'''{{Font|Baskerville Old Face|{{Color|#FF0000|NO}}}}'''" signs]] for more than 10 seconds. He will endlessly loop around aimlessly in an attempt to catch the player until he can teleport them to the [[campfire]].
{{clear}}

=== Other appearances === 
* [[File:UHOH.jpg|200px|thumb|Arts and Crafters charging towards the player in ''Classic''/''Birthday Bash''.]]In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'', Arts and Crafters has the same behavior as in ''Baldi's Basics Plus''. He spawns in the [[cafeteria]] and he will only attack if the player collected all 7 notebooks. This occurs in both [[Story Mode]] and Endless Mode. When he reaches the player, Arts and Crafters will instantly teleport the player and Baldi to the south [[exit]] where the game begins, it can be helpful because the player can use this teleport to their advantage by quickly reaching the final exit after the other three have been activated. Even without running, it is possible to just barely avoid being caught by Baldi after teleporting there, so it comes in handy if the player has run out of [[stamina]] and is about to be caught. Also, if the player still has a Swinging Door Lock, they could use it to prevent Baldi from going through the swinging door in the T-shaped hallway.
* [[File:SURPRISE.jpg|200px|thumb|Arts and Crafters appearing with the original cast for a birthday surprise.]]In ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash]]'', Arts and Crafters has the same appearance in ''Classic'', but he is one of the few characters not to wear any birthday accessories. Furthermore, he was not able to shout out a word to surprise the player with the original cast after reaching the last exit in the cafeteria. Instead, he just opens his mouth.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered]]'', Arts and Crafters will teleport the player and Baldi to a random exit that has not been reached. While the teleporting mechanism is similar to ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', other mechanisms are same as in ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash''.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics - Field Trip demo: Camping]]'', Arts and Crafters can take the player's [[log]]s away after teleporting and can even teleport angry Baldi back to the same place with the player after spawning if the campfire is out.
{{clear}}

== Tips & Tricks ==
=== Advantages ===

* If you splash a ''[[BSODA]]'' at ''[[Arts and Crafters]]'' before he tags you, you can escape.

* Arts and Crafters can be really helpful, especially if there's one elevator left to be accessed in the floor, as the player can just avoid looking at him until all the other elevators are closed, and then let Arts and Crafters teleport them to the last one.
* Due to how fast he can be, Arts and Crafters occasionally misses the entrance of hallways. Also, he normally doesn't enter rooms by himself, making avoiding him easier.

=== Neutral ===
* Using a [[Faculty Nametag]] will prevent Arts and Crafters to attack the player, and if Arts and Crafters was already triggered, he will ignore you if you have the [[Faculty Nametag|Nametag]] equiped.
* Arts and Crafter will disappear if he interacts with the player while he is oriented opposite or gets squished.
* Arts and Crafters keeps accelerating, making him really fast when hiding in a blue locker or a dark spot to not get caught.
* Arts and Crafters cannot teleport the player if they are being sucked in a [[whirlpool]], teleporting while the [[Dangerous Teleporter]] is in effect, traveling in a [[vent]] or being moved by a [[crane]]. However, he can still teleport and attract Baldi.

=== Disadvantages ===
* Arts and Crafters' teleport might put the player in danger, especially if they don't have any items to avoid Baldi.
