== Development ==
=== Origins ===
* Arts and Crafters is a wordplay of "[[Wikipedia:Handicraft|arts and crafts]]".
*[[File:waht have i done.jpeg|200px|thumb|Presumably the original image that inspired Arts and Crafters' design, revealed through a ''Out of Context Files on my Computer'' tweet.<ref name="Sussie in FNaF2 beta">"''waht have i done.bmp''" - Out of Context Files on my Computer. March 20, 2026. [https://x.com/mypcnocontext/status/2035067096791470363 X/Twitter]</ref>]] Arts and Crafters' design was inspired by [https://theamazingworldofgumball.fandom.com/wiki/Sussie Sussie] from ''[[Wikipedia:The Amazing World of Gumball|The Amazing World of Gumball]]'' when mystman12 tried to edit a picture of Sussie in the dark from far away.<ref>"''The idea for Arts and Crafters came from... like... Have you ever seen the character Sussie from The Amazing World of Gumball? The idea for Arts and Crafters is for some reason years back, I like photoshopped a picture of Sussie like standing out like far the distance like this dark... dark like porch at night, you know like, because I thought it looked really creepy. And so that was the inspiration for like Arts and Crafters would have like a creepy like thing like far away a photo-realistic object like far away off the back end of like a hallway or something like that.''" - {{YouTube|ftr2aImDo5g|1:40|Mystman12 (Baldi’s Basics) Q&A - CultureFreak 1:40-2:17}}</ref><ref name="Sussie in FNaF2 beta" />
* Arts and Crafters' design appears to not match any real life sock puppet characters at all.
* His behavior is based on photo-based characters staring at people from a distance.<ref>"''the creepy factor of seeing some photo-realistic character like that just staring at you from a distance stuck with me. That's pretty much entirely what defined Arts and Crafters' behavior in-game.''" - mystman12. June 24, 2023. [https://www.instagram.com/p/Ct1ppnXAxZY/ Instagram]</ref>
=== Audio ===
* Arts and Crafters was originally intended to speak actual words instead of just screeching, as [[mystman12]] has confirmed that the "''Let's play!''" line was originally for him before he realized that it would fit [[Playtime]] more and that's how her voice "came to be".<ref name="lets play">"''Actually, when I recorded Playtime's line, "let's play," I was actually doing it as a test for Arts and Crafters, I went "let's play!" (clown-like voice) and then... I remembered I actually had a plan for Arts and Crafters which was a screeching noise he has, so I was like, 'Oh, I must just use it for Arts and Crafters,' then I realized this is perfect for Playtime, so that's how her voice came to be.''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|18:28|Livestream archive 18:28-18:56}}</ref>
* Arts and Crafters' loud corrupted audio originates from a ROM corruption of the [[Wikipedia:Super Nintendo Entertainment System|Super Nintendo]] game ''[[Wikipedia:Donkey Kong Country 2|Donkey Kong Country 2]]''.<ref name="audio origin" />

=== Mechanics ===
* Arts and Crafters is the only character who gets in the player's way only once in each floor and game.
* Arts and Crafters is one of the fastest characters when he moves at maximum speed, being faster than [[1st Prize]] and Gotta Sweep. Arts and Crafters is so fast that he often misses the entrance to a hallway.
** Despite Arts and Crafters' speed, it is possible to lose him.

* Arts and Crafters can become invisible.

[[File:Invisible gummed.png|200px|thumb|Invisible Arts and Crafters hit by Beans' bubble gum.]]

* Beans can hit Arts and Crafters, even while invisible.

* When the player looks at Arts and Crafters for a while, then collecting the last notebook, he will immediately try to attack the player after they see him. This does not happen in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash''.
{{clear}}
* [[File:3D Schoolhouse.png|150px|thumb|A full map in a 3D model program showing the invisible Arts and Crafters triggers.]]In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', Arts and Crafters can be controlled by eight triggers, but these triggers can only be seen in the 3D model map. However, the map does not show the other two triggers.<ref>"''So those rooms aren't actually rooms at all, they're actually just pieces of the triggers used to control Arts and Crafters. There are actually eight triggers total and when you enter one Arts and Crafters will head to a predetermined location based on the trigger you entered.''" - {{YouTube|CSM2vX2uFGU|1:42|<nowiki>Baldi's Basics Classic LOST BITS (ft. The Developer!) | Unused Content [TetraBitGaming]</nowiki> 1:42-1:57}}</ref>
** If the player enters one of the triggers, Arts and Crafters will go to a "{{C|goTarget}}", as internally called in the triggers' script, if he is not angry. If the player exits this trigger, he will go to a "{{C|fleeTarget}}" and have his sprite be forced visible by the game for 3 seconds, if he is not angry.
* By using hacks to make the characters' names visible, it is revealed that Arts and Crafters becomes invisible rather than truly vanishing. He will turn invisible once the player gets close enough.
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', if the player moves fast enough to enter a room before Arts and Crafters catches them, they will get stuck in there, because getting out will have Arts and Crafters catching them, and Arts and Crafters constantly zips back and forth in front of the [[door]], unless the room has more than one door.
{{clear}}

=== Other information ===
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', Arts and Crafters' sprite is entirely un-dithered, having the highest resolution sprite in the game. It also has a yellow sunset-like lighting instead of the gray daytime-like one. This was changed since ''[[Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo|Full Game Early Demo]]''.
* On the poster of Arts and Crafters, his picture is slightly smaller than it appears in ''Baldi's Basics Plus''.
* The current poster reveals Arts and Crafters' gender, unlike his older poster that simply referred to Arts and Crafters as an "it". This was likely referring to the sock puppet, not Arts and Crafters himself.
** However, Arts and Crafters is referred to as an "it" in the ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' V0.3.3 development log as well.

== Trivia ==
* [[File:Arts.png|200px|thumb|The sock puppet used to create Arts and Crafters in the Kickstarter video.]]In the ''[[Baldi's Basics Kickstarter Madness]]'' video, mystman12 can be seen hitting Arts and Crafters with his ruler.<ref>"''Alright, guys, it's serious time!''" - {{YouTube|CS3deEpP4SM|3:42|''Baldi's Basics Kickstarter Madness'' 3:42-3:55}}</ref>
* Arts and Crafters is intended to have a [[Toy#Prototypes|beanie plush]], but it has yet to be released in the new standard set.
{{clear}}

The '''Principal of the Thing''' is, hence his name, the principal of the [[Schoolhouse]] or the [[Super Schoolhouse]] in the ''[[Baldi's Basics]]'' series. He is one of the most prominent and most bothersome [[character]]s that [[the player]] can encounter.

== Description ==
'''Principal of the Thing''' appears as a dithered, semi-realistic image of a tall, thin man with his right hand raised and pointing upward. His eyebrows are lifted, and his mouth is open, as if shouting or giving a command. He wears a yellow bow tie, a dark gray sweater with lighter horizontal stripes, pale blue jeans, and white socks that appear slightly brownish-red due to dithering, with no shoes. Parts of his left arm and legs appear glitched or partially erased. In-game, his skin tone has a tannish hue, suggesting a darker complexion; however, official merchandise — such as [[Merchandise|his plushie]] — depicts him with a lighter tone, implying that the darker appearance may result from sprite dithering or in-game lighting.

Prior to [[V0.14_(Plus)|V0.14]] of [[Baldi's Basics Plus]], his bow tie was not very easy to notice, since [[mystman12|Micah McGonigal]] used very few colors to dither the Principal's image.<ref>"''Actually, he's always had a bow-tie, you just can't see it in the game because of how few colors his sprite uses. :P''" - [[:File:Principal wears a bow-tie.jpg|Twitter/X DM]]</ref> The Principal's bow tie was very similar to the color of his skin, and was only then made further clear through his merchandise items focusing on such. This was eventually fixed in V0.14, though his sprite in [[Baldi's Basics Classic|Classic]] remains the same. Additionally, when Principal is not chasing the player, he lowers his hand. This makes it clearer to know when he is actually chasing them.

== Personality ==
Principal of the Thing has a calm personality but a bossy attitude. He speaks in a monotone voice and never raises it even when he is angry. Like a hall monitor, he is in charge of the Schoolhouse or Super Schoolhouse and will put students in detention upon getting caught breaking any of the [[school rule]]s "in the [[Hallway|halls]]", even if they are in any [[location]] except for the [[playground]] and [[Principal's office|his office]]. He is known to be very strict, as he sends the player to detention without warning for a small infraction such as running in the halls. He can also go as far as to even use corporal punishment, as evidenced by [[Beans]]' line, where it is stated that the Principal would be "smacking his head and causing a ring" in his ears for breaking too many rules. It was also said that the Principal may "throw the book" at Beans as revenge for spitting gum at the Principal. This could be a pun, however, as "throwing the book" is a phrase meaning "to punish (someone) as severely as possible."

However, his attitude is accompanied by a sense of humor that is considered well-hidden to others, as he made a joke about "served justice" being tasty on his own office poster, said surprises were against the rules for no other reason than comedy, and even made a joke about "breaking the 4th wall" when the player went through a very literal one. This could also justify why the detention times are so unfathomably short, ranging from only 15 to [[99]] seconds depending on how many times someone was caught breaking the rules. His sense of humor seems to focus on jokes that take a while to understand, and simply saying random things.

== Artificial intelligence ==
=== General ===
[[File:Principal in the hall.png|thumb|Principal of the Thing, while idle.]]
Principal of the Thing will spawn in his office, and begins doing what a standard hall monitor would do in a school environment. He will wander around the school at the player's running speed, checking rooms and occasionally happily whistling. He will pass by the player without conflict unless the player is caught breaking any of the school rules. Before Principal of the Thing enters a faculty room, he will knock on the door three times. By using the [[Principal Whistle]], he will come to the player at a very high speed, saying "''Don't worry, I'm coming!''". Principal of the Thing tends to only go to places he has not visited, rather than just wandering in completely random directions. This does not apply to ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash''.<ref>"''Principal of the Thing used to wander around just like everyone else, but now he does so differently. Rather than wandering completely randomly, Principal of the Thing now prefers going to places he hasn't been in a while. This means if you see him pass through a hall, it's likely you won't see him come that way again for a while!''" - [https://www.kickstarter.com/projects/193364415/baldis-basics-in-education-and-learning-full-game/posts/2539061 Kickstarter UPDATE #20: Status Update #8 - Everyone is here!]</ref>

[[File:Principal is scolding the player.png|thumb|Principal of the Thing scolding the player for running in the halls, which is against the school rules.]]
If Principal of the Thing sees the player breaking the rules for [[#Guilt system|a specific amount of time]], he will chase the player and put his hand up in Plus until he catches them. He will not whistle or knock on faculty room doors while chasing the player.

Once he has caught up to the player, he will give them detention for a certain amount of [[Timer|time]], which features on the [[gauge]] and timers on doors in the Principal's office. After the player gets sent to detention, they will be teleported at the center of the Principal's office and the Principal will be teleported one [[tile]] in front of where they are looking at. Then, he will stand still for 3 seconds, and he will exit his office. During detention, the player is unable to open any door to the Principal's office for 15 seconds for the first time, and the timer will be increased by 5 seconds each time they are caught.<!--Detention time increment for original games is mentioned in 'Other appearances' section--> Any detention after 60 seconds will last for 99 seconds. Being put into detention attracts Baldi and angry Dr. Reflex to the center of the Principal's office and resets Baldi's sound queue. This will cause Baldi to go towards the Principal's office even if other noises played before detention.

<gallery position="center">
Principal's detention.png|Principal of the Thing in his office after sending the player to detention.
Principal's detention after.png|Ditto, but after he announces the time.
</gallery>

Principal of the Thing can send It's a Bully to detention for bullying in the halls if the Bully asks for an item from the player. Similar to the player's case, Principal of the Thing will teleport to his office after It's a Bully is sent to detention.

If the Principal tries to send the player or It's a Bully to detention while he is either oriented opposite or squished, only the Principal will be teleported to his office. If this happens to the player, Baldi and angry Dr. Reflex will be attracted to the player's location, as well as the player still receiving the detention time, where the Principal will scold the player for escaping detention.

[[Lights Out|After the school closes]], Principal of the Thing will turn off the lights in any room he enters, except for any classrooms that have incomplete [[activities]]. If the Principal sees the player during this time, he will call the player out for being in school after hours and chase them.

=== [[Challenge]]s ===
* In the [[Stealthy Challenge]], there are 6 Principal clones. If any of them notice the player, the Principal clone will call the player out for being in school after hours and chase them for detention, where the player can no longer avoid them, as they always know where the player is once they are spotted.
* Principal of the Thing is absent in the [[Speedy Challenge]] and the [[Grapple Challenge]], though his office does appear in the Grapple Challenge.
{{clear}}

=== Other appearances ===
In all games and demos except for ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', Principal of the Thing cannot open locked standard doors, but instead he just passes through them.
{{clear}}
* [[File:PrincipalScoldPlayerInDetentionMobile.PNG|thumb|Principal of the Thing in his office after sending the player to detention in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' on mobile.]]In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'', Principal of the Thing wanders randomly around the entire map. The timer will be increased by 15 seconds each time the player gets detention, except when the player gets detention for the fifth time and onwards. The detention time list is shorter, ranging from 15, 30, 45, 60, to 99 seconds. He will always know the player's location whenever he chases the player, so even if he is out of the player's sight, he can find them and send them to detention. The detention timer is featured on the screen like in ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', prior to [[V0.8 (Plus)|V0.8]].
{{clear}}
* [[File:SURPRISE.jpg|thumb|Principal of the Thing with the original cast for a birthday surprise.]]In ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash]]'', Principal of the Thing has the same appearance and mechanics from ''Classic'', except that he also wears a party hat to celebrate ''Baldi's Basics Classic''<nowiki>'</nowiki>s first anniversary. When appearing with the original cast for a surprise when the player reaches the last [[exit]] in the [[cafeteria]], he will say "''No surprises in the halls.''" for comical purposes. The detention text's size is same as one in ''Baldi's Basics Classic''.
{{clear}}
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered]]'', Principal of the Thing's mechanic is the same as in ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', but with the [[Hard Mode]] [[Fun Setting]], the detention timer increment is increased by 15 seconds, just like in ''Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'' and his chasing mechanic becomes similar to both games. With the [[Lights Out]] Fun Setting, he is the only NPC who has light, and it is visible even from a long distance. The detention timer text is smaller than the one in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash''.
{{clear}}
* [[File:Principal in the Field Trip demo.png|thumb|right|Principal of the Thing in the ''Field Trip demo''.]]In ''[[Baldi's Basics - Field Trip demo: Camping]]'', Principal of the Thing plays a very minor role and is one of the four characters who appeared and returned from ''Baldi's Basics Classic''. He can only be found past a few [[Texture#Demo end|dark walls]] in a hallway, where he will say "''No [[Fourth wall break|breaking the fourth wall]] in the halls!''". However, Principal of the Thing will not send the player to detention and will simply stand there idly. There is no Principal's office in the school's [[map]] for ''Field Trip demo'', as it has only two small hallways and an outdoor area.
{{clear}}

== Guilt system ==
Principal of the Thing has a certain "guilt system" to detect the player breaking the school rules. The guilt time in seconds varies depending on the school rule that was broken.

Each school rule has a guilt time in seconds that will start when the rule is broken. During guilt time, if Principal of the Thing sees the player or [[It's a Bully]], the time he sees the rules being broken will increase. If this timer reaches over 0.5 seconds (or instantly for [[It's a Bully]]), he will call them out for what they have done, chase them, raise his hand (since V0.14) and send them to detention. You cannot gain guilt in the [[Playground]] or the [[Principal's office]].

{| class="article-table" border="1" cellpadding="1" cellspacing="1"
!Rule broken 
!Guilt time
|-
|"No running"
|0.1 seconds
|-
|"No entering faculty"
|1 second after leaving<ref group="Guilt">Indefinite whilst being in a faculty only room</ref>
|-
|"No drinking"
|1 second
|-
|"No escaping detention"
|Until detention timer is up
|-
|Looking in other people's lockers
|1 second
|-
|Being in school after-hours
|Indefinite
|-
|Bullying 
|10 seconds
|}<references group="Guilt" />

== Development ==
=== Origins ===
* His name is a play on words of the phrase: "principle of the thing".
* Principal of the Thing's whistle is [[Inspiration and reference|inspired]] by a scene in the Cartoon Network animation series ''[[Wikipedia:The Amazing World of Gumball|The Amazing World of Gumball]]''. In the episode "[https://theamazingworldofgumball.fandom.com/wiki/The_Banana The Banana]", the character [https://theamazingworldofgumball.fandom.com/wiki/Banana_Joe Banana Joe] is seen {{YouTube|dUgOmT1Pcbc||whistling}} a similar tune.
* According to mystman12's live stream, he was unsure where Principal of the Thing's shirt came from. He went on to mention it was simply a shirt he just happened to have in his possession.<ref>"''I have no idea where the shirt for the Principal came from. Just a shirt I had...''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|1:25:55|Livestream archive 1:25:55-1:26:04}}</ref>
* In the "I'm animating" video, mystman12 is wearing clothes similar to Principal of the Thing's.<ref>"''Animation is easy, guys. Stop acting like it's so hard!''" - mystman12. December 6, 2015. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/673685823825584129 X/Twitter]</ref>
* Principal of the Thing, up until July 23rd, 2026, was the only character who has never been seen undithered in any way, including either a prototype or a small portion of him.
** On July 23rd, 2026, the Out Of Context Files on my Computer posted an image featuring an HD render of Principal's face.<ref>[https://x.com/mypcnocontext/status/2080279108357886293 The image]</ref>
* There is an odd, idiomatic tongue-in-cheek joke for the Principal's office description: While using the "justice is served" phrase (an idiom used to give proper punishment or fair treatment) when catching the students violating any rules, Principal then states that the served justice tastes good for him like a legitimate food similar to the "dinner is served" idiom.

=== Mechanics ===
==== ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' ====
* Principal of the Thing is one of the fastest characters when the [[Principal Whistle]] is used.
* Despite Principal of the Thing's quote "''No being in school after-hours in the halls!''" not being mentioned in the school rules poster, he scolds the player for breaking this rule.
* If the player is in a faculty room before teleporting, and Principal of the Thing is in the player's sight after teleporting, he will scold them for entering the faculty room. The same thing will happen while hiding in a blue locker before doing so. In that case, the Principal will scold the player for looking in other people's lockers.
* Principal of the Thing will be able to send characters other than the player and It's a Bully to detention in the final version of the game.<ref>"''Yeah, I haven't added the ability for PotT to send other characters to detention yet. That will be in the finished game.''" [[:File:Baldi enters the Principal's office while the tape is in use.png|itch.io comment]]</ref>
* According to mystman12, Principal of the Thing is the most important character in ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' (besides [[Baldi]]).<ref>Q: "''whos the most important character (besides baldi) in your opinion (makes the game better, funnier, harder???)''" / A: "''Principal of the Thing. He's the only other character in the game that's guaranteed to show up every time and he forces players to always be aware of the school rules!''" - [[:File:Gmail questions 20221114.png|Gmail (question)]], [[:File:Gmail response 20221114.png|Gmail (response)]]</ref>

==== ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash'' ====
* All of Principal of the Thing's voice lines have a high pitched noise in them.
* By using hacks, if the player in the starting T-shaped hallway uses the Yellow Door Lock on all three [[swinging door]]s to get themselves trapped while the Principal is running after them for detention, Principal's speed limit will glitch; moving extremely slow and steady but also rarely moving in normal speed for brief amounts of time, and using the BSODA on him will not push him away normally. This will last until any Yellow Door Lock expires.

==== ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' ====
* There are [[Cut audio|unused audio files]] for Principal of the Thing that say, "''No stabbing people with pencils in the halls!''" and "''No looking in other people's lockers in the halls!''".     
** mystman12 revealed in his gaming stream that the "''No stabbing people with pencils in the halls!''" quote was going to be used for when a cut character named "[[Cut character#Pencil Boy|Pencil Boy]]" stabs the player.<ref>"''The "stabbing people with pencils in the halls" thing was actually going to do a character... I would think I was gonna call him just like "Pencil Boy" or something. ... And basically what I was gonna be was this little kid who would basically come to you and start stabbing with pencils, and if the Principal saw, then he would get rid of him.''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|48:31|Livestream archive 48:31-48:56}}</ref>
** The "''No looking in other people's lockers in the halls!''" quote was used for an unused concept where the player was intended to be able to open [[locker]]s and find items. mystman12 has revealed that the reason he did not implement this concept is that he thought the idea would be too tedious to pull off. However, the audio file is used when he catches the player hiding in a blue locker since ''[[Baldi's Basics Kickstarter Exclusive Demo|Kickstarter Exclusive Demo]]'', and [[V0.6 (Plus)|V0.6]] of ''Plus'' would implement green storage lockers that the player can use to store items in, allowing the line to be spoken in its original context at last.

==== ''Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash''/''Classic Remastered'' ====
* Strangely, the sprite of Principal of the Thing wearing a party hat has a lot of space above him.
* Principal of the Thing's party hat is a 3D model, although his sprite is based on a real-life person. mystman12 decided to use the pre-made 3D hat for the Principal's party hat and he did not have a real life one when making a party variant for the Principal's sprite.<ref>Q: "''why is principal of the things party hat in party mode/birthday bash cgi and not a real life picture like him?''" / A: "''I can't remember exactly why, I think I just decided to use the 3D hat since I already had it and didn't have a real one to take a photo of.''" - [[:File:Gmail questions 20221114.png|Gmail (question)]], [[:File:Gmail response 20221114.png|Gmail (response)]]</ref>

==== ''Baldi's Basics - Field Trip demo: Camping'' ====
* Principal of the Thing's "''No breaking the fourth wall in the halls!''" line from the ''Field Trip demo'' is a pun for going through the final wall, which reads "'''{{Font|Comic Sans MS|You'll regret this...}}'''", which was the fourth wall that the player could go through.
* Principal of the Thing is much taller than in ''Baldi's Basics Plus''.

=== Other information ===
* All other characters besides [[Beans]] and [[Arts and Crafters]] have a reason to not get sent to detention for running in the halls:
** [[1st Prize]] uses wheels to travel.
** [[Chalkles]] and [[Cloudy Copter]] seem to be flying.
*** Arts and Crafters seems to also be flying, but it's unknown if he's really doing it or not.
** Baldi, [[Gotta Sweep]], [[Mrs. Pomp]], and Dr. Reflex are faculty, which makes them unable to get detention by any means.
*** Baldi does not run, as he seemingly teleports as he slaps his ruler.
*** Mrs. Pomp also does not run as she hops.
** Playtime is jumping rope in the halls instead of running, seemingly bypassing the rules. However when her jump rope is cut, which prevents her from jumping, she implicitly speedwalks.
** It's a Bully does not move in the school as he is stationary, but he is of course put into detention solely for living up to his name and blocking the hallways.
** [[The Test]] seems to teleport rather than run.
*** Despite this, Principal of the Thing is clearly running around the school, since he is always faster than the player's running speed. The only justification behind faculty members running without punishment is the [[Faculty Nametag]], which presumably allows staff to freely break their own rules.
** Beans does not have a reason as he is meant to be sent to detention in-game. This, however, is a feature that isn't implemented yet.<ref>Q: "''baldi's basics logic is just beans spitting a gum at you or at any character (even principal) and not get detention like player or bully does''" / A: "''That was also going to be an intended feature, but never got implemented''" - Padre Snowmizzle. May 21, 2022. [https://discordapp.com/channels/448443037484449804/448443038008606722/977471835744636988 Discord]</ref>

'''Playtime''' is a [[character]] who appears in the ''[[Baldi's Basics]]'' series. She is a little girl that roams the [[Hallway|halls]] of the [[Schoolhouse]] or [[Super Schoolhouse]], acting as a significant obstacle for the [[player]].

== Description ==
Playtime's in-game sprite is a poorly drawn, animated image of a small girl with a jump rope. She has light skin, and she has a smile (which turns into a frown if her minigame is interrupted), and a long, cartoony nose. She has quite poor eyesight, as described in her poster in the [[Principal's office]], but she is not entirely blind. Her black, scribble eyes possibly indicate retinal damage or were just drawn for stylistic purposes. Even though Playtime is young, two lines on both sides of her face under her eyes make her look older. However, it could possibly be bags under her eyes, or even, based on the position of the lines, scars.

Her short hair is mostly black, but there are some brown strands. Upon closer inspection, the brown strands appear to be scribbled in a circular spread-out style, while the black strands appear to be cross-hatched zigzag lines, implying that the brown strands are curly and/or more tangled than the possibly straight black strands. Additionally, only the black part of her hair moves compared to the brown strands. Her pose makes her appear as if she is balancing on her left foot, and her arms are outstretched as if she is trying to hold her balance.

Her hands are misshapen as well; her left hand is a side-viewed open palm as if she was leaning against a wall, and her right hand somewhat resembles a 4-digit hand with her thumb (or index finger, if she has 5 digits counting a hidden thumb) and pinky fingers sticking up.

She wears a red, long-sleeved dress with a pair of cyan pants and simplistic light brown shoes, which could possibly be boots since they go up to her pants' sleeves. Her jump rope and some parts of the black hair constantly move, the former in a manner that makes her appear to be jumping rope, hence her name, and the latter as if it is being blown by wind. She does not appear to jump over the rope when it comes down though, because, apart from her rope and her hair, the rest of her remains static. When her jump rope minigame is interrupted, however, her entire sprite becomes a static image.

The jump rope itself has two scribbly handles, and the rope is a single, curved line connecting the handles. Each handle is drawn on each of Playtime's hands, so they appear to be attached to her hands, and it does not actually look like Playtime is holding it. The entire jump rope is black, and when it is cut by using [[Safety Scissors]], each half resembles a short whip being held upside down.

Her voice is distorted by poor [[audio]] quality, and her speech is consistently monotone and somewhat masculine for a little girl because [[mystman12|Micah McGonigal]] provided her voice. Her voice is also constantly peaking in volume, making her one of the loudest characters speaking "naturally".

== Personality ==
Playtime's personality ranges from being inconsistent to inscrutable, as her voice, mannerisms, and behavior are illogical and uncanny. Whenever she is happy, cheerfully seeking a playmate, or congratulating the player for winning her game, she always maintains a flat and deadpan tone in her voice. Her few genuine responses are when she is upset, specifically her outcry when she has her rope cut by the Safety Scissors.

Despite this, she is apparently very social and is always searching for someone to play with in her game. She seems to be very optimistic about being friends with others, but is also unprepared for hostility, which will leave her upset and confused for a short time once it happens. However, after said short time passes, she will go back to being social and cheerful again, and she will regain the player's trust. Going off of the fact she does not get detention for "running" in the halls (as she is jumping), this suggests a clever, cheeky side of her character, implying she's improvising ways to break the rules without actually breaking them.

Her poor eyesight and short memory are possibly why she never turns corners after the player and immediately trusts them again after they bully her, respectively. The poor eyesight might make her unable to tell if the corridors are still straight during a chase, and the short memory presumably prevents her from remembering that the player is mean to her, or even recognizing them for that matter, also possibly explaining her being unprepared for conflict.

Her true intentions are most likely never to purposely cause harm or make others upset, as it is stated several times in the game that she is not aware of Baldi's enraged chasing or attempted violence towards the player. 

== Artificial intelligence ==
=== Main gameplay ===
Playtime will roam the hallways in unspecified directions, playing her [[A-Tisket, A-Tasket and Party Time|signature jingle]]. If she spots the player while not on cooldown, she will immediately chase them until she loses track of or catches the player. Playtime's speed when chasing the player is slightly faster than the player's running speed. Playtime stops chasing the player and wanders randomly if the player is walking around a corner, as she forgets the player's location as soon as they are out of her sight.

[[File:Get-away time.png|200px|right]]
When Playtime catches the player, she will slightly glide to the designated spot, force them to stop moving and jump over a skipping rope five times in a row. If the player misses the rope, they will have to restart from the first jump and Baldi and angry [[Dr. Reflex]] will attract to the player's location. The player must press the "Interact" key (Left Mouse Button by default) to jump. They have to time the jump rope's position correctly in order to successfully jump over. During the minigame, the player can slightly move while jumping the rope. After successfully completing the game, Playtime will leave, and the player will earn [[You Thought Points]] and be free to walk again. 15 seconds will pass before Playtime can catch the player again.

The amount of You Thought Points earned after completing her minigame ranges from 0 to 25 in increments of 5. When the player jumps the rope five times without messing up, they will get 25 YTPs, but every time they miss the rope, the reward will be reduced by 5 YTPs. As a result, the player cannot get YTPs if they miss the rope five or more times before completing the minigame.

If the player uses Safety Scissors on the jump rope, it will be cut, and Playtime will say "''Oh! That makes me sad!''", and she will frown before leaving. She will also get sad when her minigame is interrupted.

=== Other appearances ===
<gallery position="center">
PT Classic.png|Playtime playing with the player in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' on mobile.
Playtime jump rope classic.gif|Ditto, but animated.
SURPRISE.jpg|Playtime appearing with the original cast for a birthday surprise in ''Birthday Bash''.
Playtime in Hard Mode.png|Playtime in ''Classic Remastered'' with Hard Mode Fun Setting.
</gallery>
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'', Playtime spawns in the middle of the left hall from the school's west wing. Sometimes she may keep going in the same direction even if the player exits her sight.<ref>"''In the original game, Playtime will stop chasing you and wander randomly again if she loses sight of you. Problem was, sometimes she would still keep walking in the same direction! Now, she's guaranteed to turn around every time.''" - [https://www.kickstarter.com/projects/193364415/baldis-basics-in-education-and-learning-full-game/posts/2539061 Kickstarter UPDATE #20: Status Update #8 - Everyone is here!]</ref> There is no control guide for jumping the rope. However, on the mobile version, there is text saying "'''{{Font|Use "[[File:CursorSprite Classic.png|25px|link=]]" to jump!}}'''" as control guide. She only gets sad when her jumping rope is cut by Safety Scissors. During the minigame, the player cannot move and look back.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash]]'', Playtime has the same mechanics as in ''Baldi's Basics Classic''. However, she wears a party hat (which falls off when her rope is cut) for the celebration of ''Baldi's Basics Classic'''s first anniversary. As soon as the player reaches the last [[exit]] in the [[cafeteria]], Playtime will arrive with the original cast to yell a surprise for the player. In one of the rooms in the [[glitched school]], a total of eight Playtime sprites can be found along with a giant {{C|[[PlaceFace]]}} and some {{C|[[Baldloon]]}}s. One of them is frowning and out of the line while the others are different Playtimes, all representing frames of the animated Playtime sprite. The sprites are all in the same height as the player's. These Playtimes are not interactive, but instead used for decoration.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered]]'', the mechanics are same as in ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]''. However, with the [[Hard Mode]] [[Fun Setting]], the player has to jump over Playtime's rope 10 times in a row. Also, the minigame is much quicker but easier to mess up on, with the jump height being reduced.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo]]'', Playtime has a new spawn location. This is the first time, during her jump rope minigame, that there is no text. The jump physics were also slightly improved in such a way that the player would land on the ground quickly instead of seemingly floating. The text reappeared in [[V0.3.5 (Plus)|V0.3.5]] of ''Baldi's Basics Plus''.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Kickstarter Exclusive Demo]]'', due to the demo's development oversight, Playtime's mechanics are very buggy, and she rarely appears.

== Tips & Tricks ==
=== Advantages ===
* Playtime can give the player You Thought Points and there is no limit for how many times Playtime's minigame can be played. So, the player can earn as much You Thought Points as they want, as long as they keep all the other threats away.

=== Neutral ===
* If Playtime is squished by Dr. Reflex's hammer, she will not start the jump rope minigame, although she still can chase after the player.
** However, if Playtime is squished when the minigame has already started, the minigame will not get interrupted.
* If the player is out of Playtime's sight or teleported, her minigame will be interrupted immediately. Here is the list of ways to interrupt her minigame:
** Turning around a corner while playing jump rope minigame, so Playtime will lose the player.
** Being moved or teleported by whirlpool during the [[flood]] [[event]].
** Using the [[Grappling Hook]] or [[Dangerous Teleporter]].
** Entering a [[Locker#Blue lockers|blue locker]].
** Being teleported by [[Arts and Crafters]] after he gets angry at them.
** Being teleported by [[Principal of the Thing]] after breaking one of the [[school rule]]s.
** Being pushed away from her by [[1st Prize]] or [[Gotta Sweep]].
** Using a [[Dirty Chalk Eraser]] or [[Invisibility Elixir]].
** Being dragged by the [[student]]s during the [[Student Shuffle]] event.
** Using a [[BSODA]] to push her away.
** Entering a different room and letting the door close.
* Some characters can still affect the player while playing the jump rope minigame.
** [[Baldi]] can catch the player and send them to either the elevator or title screen, unless they have [[An Apple for Baldi]] or he is either oriented opposite or squished.
** [[Cloudy Copter]] can push characters, but it may hard to interrupt her minigame unlike 1st Prize and Gotta Sweep if Playtime is also pushed by his wind as well.
** Dr. Reflex can do his reflex test while the player plays the jump rope minigame. Squishing either the player or Playtime will not interrupt the minigame.
** [[The Test]] can slow down time while the player is looking at him, but it does not interrupt and slow down her minigame. The same goes when time goes faster after The Test explodes.

=== Disadvantages ===
* During the minigame, the player's movement is limited, so they become vulnerable to other characters.
** In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', the player cannot move at all, cannot look back, and must turn manually. This can lead to Baldi sneaking up on the player or not looking away from Arts and Crafters in time.
* If the player messes up any of their jumps, Baldi and angry Dr. Reflex will be alerted to their location.

== Development ==
=== Audios ===
* According to mystman12's stream, to make Playtime's voice, mystman12 records himself talking "like a clown" and then distorts it in a way he never bothered to clarify himself.<ref>"''Playtime's voice is the only voice I can't do. When I recorded Playtime's voice, "I talk like this, like a clown," (clown-like voice) and then I just do a whole lot of distortion to it.''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|18:15|Livestream archive 18:15-18:26}}</ref>
** Playtime's voice was created by Paulstretching, pitching up, amplifying and changing the recording rate to 8000 Hz.<ref>"''Paulstretch, a little bit pitching up, amplifying so that the audio clips a bit, and setting the recording rate to 8000 hz. Basically, all the things you shouldn't do to audio. :P''" - mystman12. June 20, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1009613894694965250 X/Twitter]</ref>
** A similar, if not the same voice tactic, is also used for Dr. Reflex's voice.
* Playtime's voice line "''Let's play!''" was originally a test for Arts and Crafters' voice, but then mystman12 realized that the line would fit Playtime more, so he ultimately decided to use the line for Playtime instead, to form the origin of her voice.<ref>"''Actually, when I recorded Playtime's line, "let's play," I was actually doing it as a test for Arts and Crafters, I went "let's play!" (clown-like voice) and then... I remembered I actually had a plan for Arts and Crafters which was a screeching noise he has, so I was like, 'Oh, I must just use it for Arts and Crafters,' then I realized this is perfect for Playtime, so that's how her voice came to be.''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|18:28|Livestream archive 18:28-18:56}}</ref>
** This voice line was initially used in [[March 28th Build 1 (Classic)|first sub-build of March 28th Build]] for Arts and Crafters, but it became unused until Playtime was implemented in [[March 30th Build 2 (Classic)|second sub-build of March 30th Build]].
* Playtime's quote, "''Oops! You messed up!''", is actually only part of the quote, with the rest being "''Let's try again - from the top! Ready? Go!''".
** In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', the rest of the quote is usually never heard because the jump rope minigame starts over before she can finish speaking. The only way to hear it in-game is if the player fails the first jump two times in a row, so she can say the full quote before resuming the minigame.
** The rest part was completely removed since ''Full Game Early Demo''.

=== Sprites ===
* Playtime's scribbly-looking appearance, especially the eyes and hair, seems to be based on PilotRedSun's art-style, yet a bit more comparable to his "{{YouTube|neEAtaBYulM||nesquik rabbit}}" video.
* Strangely, Playtime's rope is over her hands as if it was glued onto her hands rather than her simply holding it.
* Playtime is one of the smallest characters in the game, but she has the largest sprite in the game files, at 653x593 pixels.
* Despite Playtime presumably being a student of the Schoolhouse, she will still chase the player if they are in a [[faculty room]]. She doesn't get detention for being in faculty rooms, likely due to her poor eyesight preventing her from reading the sign on the door.
** Also, she will not be sent to detention by Principal of the Thing for running in the halls if he sees her, despite being faster than the player's running speed. This is likely because Playtime is jumping, not running, given her animation. This implies she is improvising a way to break the "no running" rule.
* Playtime's animation has more frames in ''Plus'' and ''Classic Remastered'' than in ''Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash''.

=== Mechanics ===
* Playtime is one of the few characters who can give the player You Thought Points, alongside [[Mrs. Pomp]], [[Dr. Reflex]], and [[Student|students]].
* If the player hides in a blue locker while being chased by Playtime, her position resets every time they exit the locker. This happens because she cannot see the player hiding inside it.
* Potentially, Playtime will get reworked so that she will no longer give the player You Thought Points when finishing the jumprope minigame (just like all other characters (except Students) that gives YTPs). A possible rework for her is that she'll start jumping in place for around thirty seconds to one minute once completing the jumprope minigame, and every character that passes by her will be forced to play jumprope with her.<ref>[https://www.patreon.com/posts/basically-games-131326944 Basically Games Podcast: Episode 4 29:53-30:20]</ref>
* For unknown reasons, Playtime was present with Baldi in the test and unfinished maps where they behaved normally. For example, in the unfinished campsite from [[V0.6 (Plus)|V0.6]] to [[V0.6.2 (Plus)|V0.6.2]] and in the [[light test room]] in V0.6.
* Playtime's speed was supposed to be faster during development.<ref name="nerftime">"''I had to nerf Playtime a whole lot after the game released. I've slow her down... I know I've mentioned before, but I almost have seen jump 10 times for Playtime right before the release of the game. Like yeah, 10 times... Too much... Just too much.''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|17:28|Livestream archive 17:28-17:48}}</ref>
* Playtime rarely uses the long hallway of the east side with the two [[classroom]]s and the faculty room with the [[Swinging Door Lock|Yellow Door Lock]].
* When decompiling the game (or using tools such as [https://github.com/sinai-dev/UnityExplorer UnityExplorer]) and entering the secret ending scene, Playtime is in the scene, albeit disabled. If enabled, she cannot move, as she is out of bounds, and there is no navigation mesh set in the scene, nor any points she could wander towards.
** This was done due to how her behavior (specifically the jumprope mini-game) was hardcoded into the player's script in Classic, which is ''not'' the case in Plus/Classic Remastered.
* According to the [[Cut character#Tutorial tape recorder|tape recording #3]], which was only seen in the first version of the game, Playtime was going to have a tripping mechanic, where she would trip and fall over while chasing the player (likely over the jump rope, as she constantly plays with it while moving), resulting in extra time to run from her. However, this never made it into the game, likely because a sprite of Playtime having fallen over would be too cumbersome to draw and/or animate.

=== Other information ===
* In the game files, there are voice lines where Playtime counts up to 10.
** This indicates that Playtime was originally going to make the player jump rope 10 times, but it was reduced to 5 when the final product was released. According to mystman12 during his livestream, this was because 10 jumps would take too much time to complete.<ref name="nerftime" />
** Playtime also has a voice line where she tells the player how to play her minigame and to jump rope 10 times, only used in the very first version. This was likely cut as it took too long.
** Playing jump rope 10 times in a row was later introduced in ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'', though it only works if the Hard Mode Fun Setting is enabled.

== Trivia ==
* Baldi called Playtime '"Get-away" time' in [[Baldi's Tips'n'Tricks'n'Lessons]].<ref>"''Playtime? More like "Get-away" time!''" - Baldi's Tips'n'Tricks'n'Lessons part in {{C|README.txt}} of ''Baldi's Basics Classic''</ref>
* Playtime is the only NPC to have her own theme.
** If Playtime finds her way inside a [[vent]], either because of pushing entities such as [[Gotta Sweep]], [[BSODA|regular]] or [[Diet BSODA|diet]] BSODA sprays, or [[Student Shuffle|student crowds]], her music will behave as if she was in the halls, rather than properly updating or sounding as it if was coming through the vent.
* Playtime could possibly be considered the exact opposite of [[It's a Bully]], evidence being:
** It's a Bully is stationary and lazy, while Playtime is active and energetic.
** It's a Bully, despite being tall, is typically not too big of a problem compared to Playtime.
** While It's a Bully is greedy and selfish and shows no remorse for the player regardless of whether Baldi is behind them or not, Playtime is generally good-natured and is completely unaware of Baldi's true intentions.
** It's a Bully possibly has straight, neatly combed hair, contrasting Playtime's messy, unkempt hair.
* Playtime and [[Johnny]] possess numerous similarities:
** Their voice actors' genders don't match their own.
** They were both drawn with MS Paint, and their color pallettes take inspiration from there too.
** They both are easily made upset in certain circumstances.
** They both have brown and dark hair.

'''It's a Bully''' is, hence his name, the school bully of the [[Schoolhouse]] and [[Super Schoolhouse]]. He is a student existing in these [[:Category:Buildings|buildings]] presumably because it is believed that "every good school needs a good bully!", according to his poster description in the [[Principal's office]].

== Description ==
It's a Bully appears to be a terribly modeled CGI human figure with a fat orange ellipse sphere for a torso, blue cylinders for limbs, and small, tan, finger/toeless balls for hands and feet. His arms and legs both use the same model. He is completely barefoot. It's a Bully's outfit is difficult to make out, but it is likely that it's an orange shirt with long blue sleeves, with blue skinny pants underneath.

He has an incredibly distorted and deformed face with smaller brown eyes (beady due to the overall dithering effect), a gaping mouth with red lips (orange due to dithering) that clip into his fat, asymmetrical nose (upper lip) and where his nonexistent neck is (lower lip). It is possible that It's a Bully's brown hair is neatly combed in an unevenly chopped bowl cut, as it appears smooth.

There is a Verdana font text floating next to his head that says "'''{{Font|Verdana|{{Color|#412006|THIS IS A BULLY}}}}'''" in all-caps. His pose appears to be in a run cycle, which ironically contrasts his lazy personality. He speaks in a clear voice, but the last word of every line he says has an echo effect.

== Personality ==
Being the school bully, It's a Bully has a desire to take the [[player]]'s [[item]]s, making him appear as selfish and greedy. It's a Bully likes to get what he wants. He thinks that if the player has no items, they should not be able to pass by him. Even if Baldi is right behind the player, this still applies, making him come across as fine with allowing presumed violence happen in front of him.

It is evident that he is willing to bully anyone, as his bullying somewhat even extends to school faculty, as he will not let anyone pass him aside from the player, if they have an item to give. Despite this, however, he will not make any effort to bully the player if they are wearing the [[Faculty Nametag]]. This may suggest that he is either unaware or foolish enough to not easily distinguish who is part of the school faculty and who isn't, consdering the only time he doesn't bully someone is if they have the Faculty Nametag on.

Aside from living up to his name, it is also implied his grades are poor, contributing to his bad reputation in the school.<ref>"''Nothing seen here is final, except for It's a Bully's grade, which isn't looking too good...''" - mystman12. June 18, 2019. [https://www.kickstarter.com/projects/193364415/baldis-basics-in-education-and-learning-full-game/posts/2539061 Kickstarter]</ref>

== Artificial intelligence ==
=== Main gameplay ===
[[File:It's a Bully in the hallway.png|200px|thumb|It's a Bully in the halls.]]
It's a Bully will stand still around the start or end of [[hallway]]s in the school. If 180 seconds pass while occupying the same spot, he will disappear, stating that he is bored. He cannot be moved by any means, such as being pushed by a [[BSODA]] spray or [[Cloudy Copter]]'s wind, nor can he be pushed by [[Gotta Sweep]]. Because of this, he can block characters getting pushed by a BSODA spray.

If the player approaches It's a Bully, he will take a random item from their [[inventory]]. Afterwards, he will vanish and let them pass. If the player approaches him without any items, he will push them away, forcing them to go another way, if there is one. However, if the player approaches him while wearing the [[Faculty Nametag]], or if It's a Bully is either squished or with an opposite gravity from [[Gravity Chaos]], he will ignore the player and let them pass without requiring an item. 

If [[Principal of the Thing]] spots It's a Bully asking for an item from the player, he will send him to detention and open up the path that he was blocking, with It's a Bully not appearing until 90 seconds of cooldown have passed. When the Principal sends It's a Bully to detention, he will be teleported to the center of his office.{{clear}}

=== [[Field trip]]s ===
[[File:IS THIS A BULLY? NO. THIS IS A TREE.png|200px|thumb|It's a Bully hiding behind a tree in the camping hub.]]
It's a Bully appears to be hidden on a tree in the current camping field trip. The text next to him says '''{{Font|Verdana|{{Color|#412006|#FFFFFF|THIS IS A TREE}}}}''', with the word {{Font|Verdana|{{Color|#412006|#FFFFFF|BULLY}}}} crossed out.  He is also mentioned as 'no-good bully' in the tutorial of [[Pick-Quick-Nic]] and is the one responsible for occasionally putting bombs on the plates.

[[File:Bully in camping.png|200px|thumb|It's a Bully hiding in a tree in the camping field trip, prior to V0.3 of ''Baldi's Basics Plus''.]]
It's a Bully appeared in the [[camping]] field trip until ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]'' [[V0.3 (Plus)|V0.3]]. He would hide behind a forest [[Sprite#Camping 2|tree]] and stalk the player while not being looked at. Once he caught up to the player, he would come out from behind the tree, take all of the player's [[log]]s and flee away with the tree hovering above his head. He would only do this once per visit. The player had to avoid him, and a good way to know if he was there was checking to see if a tree appeared to be moving by looking away and back quickly or checking to see It's a Bully's arm sticking out from behind a tree. Unlike the main appearance, the player could actually push back It's a Bully with a BSODA. It was the only instance where It's a Bully could be seen moving rather than being static.

=== [[Challenge]]s ===
It's a Bully only appears in the [[Grapple Challenge]] and he cannot take the Grappling Hook in the first slot from the player's inventory. However, if there is only the Grappling Hook in their inventory, he will not let them pass, although the player can pass through him by using a Grappling Hook from a long distance.

=== Other appearances === 
* [[File:Bully in Classic.png|200px|thumb|It's a Bully found in a hallway in ''Baldi's Basics Classic''.]]In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'', It's a Bully spawns in a few preset spots and takes longer to disappear before spawning in another hallway. It's a Bully first spawns in front of the [[exit]] that the player starts the game at. In this version, however, he cannot push the player if they have no items in their inventory.
* [[File:SURPRISE.jpg|200px|thumb|It's a Bully appearing with the original cast for a birthday surprise.]]In ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash]]'' and Party Style of ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered]]'', he has the same appearance in ''Classic''. Additionally, the "'''{{Font|Verdana|{{Color|#412006|#FFFFFF|THIS IS A BULLY}}}}'''" text on the top-left is changed to "'''{{Font|Verdana|{{Color|#412006|#FFFFFF|THIS IS A PARTY}}}}'''". Other than that, It's a Bully does not appear to wear any relevant birthday accessories. When the player reaches the last exit found in the [[cafeteria]] after collecting all seven notebooks, he will appear with the [[:Category:Schoolhouse characters|original cast]] to shout a surprise, but he will tell the player to give him a gift instead.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics - Field Trip demo: Camping]]'', It's a Bully can move even when the player does not have any logs or is looking at him.
{{clear}}

== Tips & Tricks ==
=== Advantages ===
* It's a Bully can block the vision of other characters, similar to the [[Dirty Chalk Eraser]], so NPCs such as Baldi will ignore the player if It's a Bully is in between them. However, this does not apply to The Test.
* NPCs try to find a different path as It's a Bully blocks other characters from passing by.
* If [[Principal of the Thing]] sees It's a Bully asking the player for an item, he will scold It's a Bully for breaking the [[School rule|"No bullying" rule]] and send him to detention with himself, letting the player pass without needing to give him an item.
** Even if the player went far away from It’s a Bully after he asked they for an item, the Principal will scold him.
* If It's a Bully's orientation is opposite to that of the player during Gravity Chaos, then they can pass through It's a Bully, similar to if he was squished. However, no NPC can safely pass through It's a Bully, even if he is oriented opposite or squished by [[Dr. Reflex]] or [[Balder Dash|Balders]] besides Dr. Reflex and Balders themselves.

=== Neutral ===
* If the player approaches It's a Bully, he will take one of their items and let them pass. He will not take an item if the player is wearing a Faculty Nametag, or if he is squished by Dr. Reflex's hammer or in a different orientation during Gravity Chaos.
* The Test can still see the player behind It's a Bully, so he can still slow down time and explode.
* If It's a Bully was squished by Dr. Reflex or Balders, the player, Dr. Reflex and Balders can pass over him as if he is not there. This can be good since no item is required, but Baldi and other detrimental NPCs can see the player.

=== Disadvantages ===
* The player cannot pass It's a Bully without an item, enough speed, or the [[Techno-Boots]]' effect when they have no items; therefore acting as a barrier.
* The BSODA and [[Diet BSODA]] sprays pass through It's a Bully, which can be a detriment if the player needs to buy time, like Baldi or Dr. Reflex closing in, especially when It's a Bully blocks a long hallway.

== Version differences ==
=== Adjustments ===
{| class="mw-collapsible mw-collapsed article-table" data-expandtext="show" data-collapsetext="hide" style="width: 100%;" border="1" cellpadding="1" cellspacing="1"

! style="width: 50%;" |Description
! style="width: 20%;" |Versions
! style="width: 30%;" |Content
|-
|It's a Bully would not be taken to detention directly upon being spotted by Principal of the Thing.
|''Plus'' -[[V0.1.2 (Plus)|V0.1.2]], ''Classic'' -[[V1.2.2 (Classic)|V1.2.2]]
| rowspan="2" |{{N/A}}

== Development ==
=== Origins === 
* There is a formerly unused image called "{{C|[[PlaceFace]]}}" in the ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash'' files which appears to be a non-distorted version of the Bully's head, seen in screenshots on [[mystman12]]'s X/Twitter. The {{C|PlaceFace}} can be found in the [[test room]].<ref>"''Today was pretty slow. I had to create some textures, which was pretty time consuming and the results were pretty meh... Despite that though, the day ended with this, sooooo I'll consider it a success! #MetaGameJam #Gamedev''" - mystman12. March 18, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/975594614534168577 X/Twitter]</ref><ref>"''Well, things are going pretty good so far! I've pretty much got player movement down, and now I'm testing out billboarding sprites (Thanks to the Unity Wiki for the billboarding script!) now. I'm going to look into AI next. #MetaGameJam''" - mystman12. March 17, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/975194768736169989 X/Twitter]</ref> The final model of It's a Bully's head does seem to use the distorted water effect. The unused image was later recycled as a stand-alone character, who first debuts in ''Field Trip demo: Camping'' behind a secret [[swinging door]] outside of the map. Only {{C|PlaceFace}}'s eyes could be seen through the swinging doors. In ''Birthday Bash''/''Classic Remastered'', {{C|PlaceFace}} is seen in a room in the [[glitched school]] with many non-functional [[Playtime]]s, one being sad, which are all of Playtime's frames scattered into one line.
* [[File:Bully's head origin.png|200px|thumb|It's a Bully's face origin.]]It's a Bully's deformed head is inspired from mystman12's now unlisted video "I'm a CGI Pro Now!" at timestamp 0:20 while "testing" {{C|PlaceFace}}'s model.<ref>I'm a CGI Pro Now! | {{YouTube|KPVRlPWcIXk||YouTube}}</ref>
* The line "''I'm gonna take your candy...''" was [[Inspiration and reference|inspired]] by the YouTube video "{{YouTube|hy32A2NoCVw||preschool bully}}" by PilotRedSun, a YouTuber that mystman12 is a huge fan of,<ref>"''Nope, just a PRS fan.''" - mystman12. June 16, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1008196382304407553 X/Twitter]</ref> according to mystman12 during his stream on playing his own game for the first time.<ref>"''He says 'I'm gonna take your candy...' Yeah, that's completely based off of the PilotRedSun video.''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|1:01:35|Livestream archive 1:01:35-1:01:43}}</ref> All of It's a Bully's voice lines have a similar echo effect applied to the end of his lines.
* [[File:Bul run.gif|200px|thumb|It's a Bully walking in one of mystman12's Vine posts. "I'm animating!"]]It's a Bully's model was first revealed by mystman12 three years before the development of ''Baldi's Basics Classic'', seeing that he made a short animation of the model walking on December 6, 2015.<ref>"''Animation is easy, guys. Stop acting like it's so hard!''" - mystman12. December 6, 2015. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/673685823825584129 X/Twitter]</ref>
** The [[Stamina Boost]] [[sticker]]'s icon is based on this animation.
* The font for "'''{{Font|Verdana|{{Color|#412006|#FFFFFF|THIS IS A BULLY}}}}'''" or "'''{{Font|Verdana|{{Color|#412006|#FFFFFF|THIS IS A PARTY}}}}'''" text is [[Wikipedia:Verdana|Verdana]] (GIMP's default [[Wikipedia:Verdana|Verdana]] font) with [[Wikipedia:Anti-aliasing|anti-aliasing]] turned off and a font size of 18px.
* It's a Bully's poster in the Principal's office actually has the letter "A" from the text "'''{{Font|Verdana|{{Color|#412006|#FFFFFF|THIS IS A BULLY}}}}'''" on his sprite in the top left corner.
* In the game's files, his sprite is named "{{C|bully_final}}", which indicates that he may have had different appearances planned.
* The "''Give me your gift...''" quote is likely inspired by one of the lines in ''Hungry Pumpkin'', "''Give me the (food name)''". ''Hungry Pumpkin'' was one of the mystman12's favorite memes and the voice in the game is very similar to the one from the original.
** Additionally, in mystman12's livestream, It's a Bully's line, "''No, I don't want that.''" originally comes from ''Hungry Pumpkin'' as well.
{{clear}}

=== Mechanics ===
==== General ====
* According to [[Baldi's Tips'n'Tricks'n'Lessons]], the reason why NPCs cannot see the player through It's a Bully is because he is so big that they cannot see through him.<ref>"''He's also so big, people (myself included) can't see past him!''" - Baldi's Tips'n'Tricks'n'Lessons part in {{C|README.txt}} of ''Baldi's Basics Classic''</ref>
** While this applies to most games, ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' includes an exception as [[The Test]] is able to view the player through It's a Bully.

==== ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' ====
* It's a Bully, [[Chalkles]], and The Test are the only characters unable to visit the [[party]] event. This is most likely because It's a Bully is stationary and teleports around the school, while Chalkles only appears in classrooms and faculty rooms, and The Test does not move by himself.
* [[File:It'sabullyfacultyroom.png|200px|thumb|It's a Bully in a faculty room after being sucked in a whirlpool.]]It's a Bully can be seen in a room during or after the flood event because he can be teleported by a whirlpool if it spawns on the same spot as him.

==== ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' ====
* It's a Bully and the Quarter have the same spawn points. This may seem good, but if the player has no items, the player cannot pass It's a Bully even if the Quarter is in the same spot because it cannot be picked up. If Baldi is right behind the player, they have no choice but to game over. The only saving grace would be if Principal of the Thing happened to pass by and send him to detention for bullying in the halls.
* It's a Bully can spawn in the center of the T shaped hallway where the game begins. If the player got an answer wrong in the first notebook and is at the left or right swinging doors, and It's a Bully spawns, they cannot escape since he cannot despawn and the player will "need to collect two notebooks before they can use these doors".

== Trivia ==
* Along with Baldi, It's a Bully was voiced by mystman12 in the "{{YouTube|pb5BkBMT6WA||Baldi gets bullied}}" video.<ref>"''New video out! A huge thank you to @mystman12 for voicing Baldi and the Bully, and @PajamaFrix for the 3d models and vhs effect! Super proud of this one :)''" - Xploshi. July 13, 2020. [https://x.com/Xploshi/status/1282785285391167496 X/Twitter]</ref>
* It's a Bully could possibly be considered the exact opposite of [[Playtime]], evidence being:
** Playtime is an active, energetic student, while It's a Bully is stationary and lazy.
** Playtime, despite being small, is usually more of a challenge to deal with than It's a Bully.
** Playtime is generally good-natured and does not understand the player's danger, while It's a Bully is greedy and selfish, and shows no remorse for the player regardless of whether Baldi is behind them or not.
** Playtime's hair is messy and unkempt, while It's a Bully possibly has straight, neatly combed hair.
** Playtime is female, and It's a Bully is male.
* It's a Bully's extremely large size may be due to the fact that he takes a lot of soda and candy bars from students and eats them. However, he puts it in his pocket to melt before eating it.<ref>Q: "''Does It's a Bully eat everything he steals?''" / A: "''Yes, but only after letting everything melt in his pockets.''" - [[:File:Gmail questions 20220927.png|Gmail (question)]], [[:File:Gmail response 20220927.png|Gmail (response)]]</ref>
* Along with Baldi and Principal of the Thing, It's a Bully is one of the only characters who has appeared in every ''Baldi's Basics'' game, both demos and public releases.
* According to his description in the Principal's office, It's a Bully's presence is considered necessary. Ironically, one of the rules on the school rules poster states "No bullying". His presence is likely needed so the player isn't the only person who can get detention, or they're making fun of him by saying that they need a school bully solely so he can be punished.
* When getting sent to detention by [[Principal of the Thing|Principal]], It's a Bully will actually be teleported there like the player, but he will immediately play his "I'm bored..." voiceline and disappear, which will cause him to go on cooldown.
* It's a Bully is the only character in the main cast who doesn't walk through the hallways.

'''Gotta Sweep''' is a large broom that resides in [[Gotta Sweep's closet|his closet]] of the [[Schoolhouse]].

== Description ==
Gotta Sweep is a giant green angle broom with choppy-edged, dark-colored bristles and a white cap. He has a brown strip (yellow due to the dithering effect) of tape that covers half of the broom, with two stickers (white and red) on the top.

While Gotta Sweep is based on a real-life broom with a cropped-out background, he appears photoshopped with his handle top shortened a little, brightened and contrasted for supposed clarity, and the bristle areas stretched in width resulting in a distorted edit.

== Personality ==
While Gotta Sweep obviously loves his work, Gotta Sweep tends to be a troublesome and chaotic neutral [[character]] who can help or hinder both the player, other students, and his co-workers. He sweeps, not because it's his job, but because he deeply wants to, and does not care if he helps or bothers others in the process.

As implied in his description in the Principal's Office, Gotta Sweep was hired as a last-minute solution, since the school was going to open in 7 hours and they didn't have any janitors for it.

Gotta Sweep's voice lines are all loud, hammy and distorted, largely due to the [[mystman12|voice actor]] yelling into the microphone.

== Artificial intelligence ==
=== Main gameplay ===
[[File:Closet.png|thumb|Gotta Sweep inside his closet.]]
At the start of the game after Baldi finishes counting down, Gotta Sweep will spawn in his closet and do nothing for a prolonged period of time. After approximately 2-3 minutes,<ref>{{C|<nowiki>this.waitTime = UnityEngine.Random.Range(120f, 180f);</nowiki>}} - [https://i.imgur.com/APqp0ol.png Coding scripts]</ref> Gotta Sweep will proclaim "'''''Looks like it's sweepin' time!'''''" and leave his closet, dashing around the halls and sweeping almost anything in his path.

Gotta Sweep will take about 30 seconds to sweep as much of the school as possible, mainly focusing on sweeping all of the [[hallway]]s, and occasionally [[classroom]]s and [[faculty room]]s. He cannot, however, enter the [[mystery room]]. Once the 30 seconds are up, he will go back to his supply closet and once inside, wait inside for another 2 to 3 minutes before repeating the process and sweeping the school again.

As he sweeps the [[Schoolhouse]] or [[Super Schoolhouse]], he will move at a moderately fast speed. Gotta Sweep will push characters or objects and even possibly take them along his path if they get in his way, excluding a few choice others such as [[It's a Bully]] and [[Chalkles]]. Anyone, player or NPC, can be pushed around by Gotta Sweep when he runs into them, taking them wherever Sweep goes until they either get snagged by an obstacle or they manage to get out of his weak grip.

=== [[Field trip]]s ===
[[File:REAPINGAME.png|thumb|Gotta Sweep in the old farm field trip.]]
In the [[farm]] field trip prior to ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' [[V0.3 (Plus)|V0.3]], if 150–350 seconds have passed, a colossal-sized version of Gotta Sweep, with a nozzle similar to a corn harvester, text attached to it, reading: "'''''{{Font|Arial|I LIKE CORN}}'''''" and a short handle, would spawn as he yelled "''GOTTA REAP, REAP, REAP!!!''". He began to cut down all the corn across his path and would cause a hostile Baldi to spawn after he passed by the player.

=== [[Challenge]]s ===
* Gotta Sweep exclusively appears in the [[Grapple Challenge]].

=== Other appearances === 
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'', Gotta Sweep can only wander in hallways, faculty rooms, and his closet.[[File:GS in his closet Classic.png|thumb|Gotta Sweep inside his closet in ''Classic''.]]
* [[File:SURPRISE.jpg|thumb|Gotta Sweep appearing with the original cast for a birthday surprise.]]In ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash]]'', Gotta Sweep has the same appearance in ''Classic''. Additionally, he has bristles covered in red, tan, green, and white confetti. After reaching the last [[exit]] door in the [[cafeteria]], he appears in the same place with the [[:Category:Schoolhouse characters|original cast]] for a birthday surprise.
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered'', Gotta Sweep's behavior is similar to ''Classic''/''Birthday Bash'', but it is easier to escape from him unless [[Hard Mode]] [[Fun Setting]] is enabled.
* [[File:FacultyRoom4-demo.jpg|thumb|Gotta Sweep in a faculty room in the ''Full Game Early Demo''.]]In ''[[Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo]]'', due to his closet being unimplemented, Gotta Sweep spawns in one of the faculty rooms.
{{clear}}

== Tips & Tricks ==
===Advantages===
*If the player gets snagged by an obstacle such as a [[locker]] while being swept, they will manage to get out of his grip easier than just trying to walk away.
=== Neutral ===
*Gotta Sweep sweeping the player can be either helpful or harmful for them, depending on the direction Gotta Sweep's moving.
**Likewise, if he pushes other characters, he can help the player, but can also put them in danger.
*The player can use the [[Techno-Boots|techno-boots]] to prevent being pushed by Gotta Sweep. They will still be slowed down when they walk into him, however.
=== Disadvantages ===
*If the player has no items, and there are no structures nearby, they may have more difficulty to escape Gotta Sweep's sweeping, especially in ''Baldi's Basics Classic/Birthday Bash''.

== Version differences ==
=== Adjustments ===
