== Development ==
=== Origins ===
* [[File:Original GS photo.jpg|200px|thumb|The original image used for Gotta Sweep.]]Gotta Sweep is actually based on mystman12's broom seen on one of his X/Twitter posts on March 30, 2018, a day before the release of ''Baldi's Basics Classic''.<ref name="Gotta Sweep's origin">"''When time is running out and you have no idea what art style to make the janitor. "I'll just make it a broom." #MetaGameJam''" - mystman12. March 30, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/979898684543504385 X/Twitter]</ref> The actual broom is a Libman "Precision Angle" broom.
* [[File:30-2 PlaceFace as Gotta Sweep.png|200px|thumb|Gotta Sweep's appearance in the second March 30th prototype.]]Gotta Sweep is the last character to be added to ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' prior to the game's release. Although he was added to the [[March 30th Build 2 (Classic)|second sub-build of March 30th Build]] along with Playtime and It's a Bully, Gotta Sweep uses {{C|[[PlaceFace]]}} in this build.
* At first, Gotta Sweep was supposed to be a human but was made a broom because of the time limit to enter the game into the Meta Game Jam. mystman12 used Colors! 3D to paint an actual janitor with a [[Inspiration and reference#PilotRedSun|PilotRedSun]]-like style as a concept of Gotta Sweep, however, he did not like the art because it looked different than what he expected,<ref>"''Gotta Sweep could have ended up being a very different character if I hadn't been so low on time! Before just going with the broom, I tried painting an actual janitor in Colors! 3D. I was trying to go for a PilotRedSun sort of style, but I ended up not liking what I came up with.''" - mystman12. March 30, 2019. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1112140210832400386 X/Twitter]</ref> leaving him no choice but to use the photo of his broom as Gotta Sweep's final design before the game's deadline.<ref name="Gotta Sweep's origin" /> Gotta Sweep's early art was later reused for {{C|[[0th Prize]]}} in ''Birthday Bash''.
* [[File:Gotta Sweep origin.png|200px|thumb|The Libman Precision Angle Broom, used for the original sprite for Gotta Sweep.]]Gotta Sweep's bristles were originally a divided color of light-grey and green in ''Baldi's Basics Classic''<nowiki>'</nowiki>s initial release. For the release of [[V1.4 (Classic)|V1.4]], mystman12 tweaked his sprite in order to avoid considering Gotta Sweep a carbon copy of the [https://libman.com/products/precision-angle-broom/?&utm_source=google&utm_medium=cpc&utm_term=libman%20precision%20angle%20broom&utm_content=305003347321&utm_campaign=Branded%20-%20Precision%20Angle%20Broom&pdv=c Libman Precision Angle Broom],<ref>"''I didn't want it to be a carbon copy of the Libman Precision Angle Broom, so I tweaked Gotta Sweep a bit.''" - mystman12. November 20, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1065075668629766145 X/Twitter]</ref> hence the colors do not match the real-life broom's bristles.
** In a QnA video on another YouTuber's channel made with mystman12, he said that the company messaged him and he was scared by it, hence why he changed the design.{{Citation}}
** Gotta Sweep representing as {{C|PlaceFace}} in the second sub-build of March 30th Build may be done because of the aforementioned copyright issue.<ref>"''Some builds have been modified to remove certain assets present in the original releases that may pose copyright concerns. This may result in minor visual differences or errors that were not present originally.''" - [https://basically-games.itch.io/baldis-basics-classic-archives ''Baldi's Basics Archives'' description]</ref>

=== Audio design ===
* Gotta Sweep may yell "''Gotta, sweep sweep sweep!''" multiple times in quick succession. mystman12 has said that he did not expect that to happen, but he thinks that it makes the game a lot of fun.<ref>"''Man, the first time I-I heard Gotta Sweep goin', cause I had no idea what it was gonna sound like that I-I-I-I lost it, eh-makin' this game was so much fun, just like, lots of stuff I wasn't expecting to happen, but I just... loved the moment it happened.''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|42:00|Livestream archive 42:00-42:16}}</ref>
* According to mystman12, during his livestream, he mentioned that the reason that Gotta Sweep sounds similar to [[Joe]] from his on-hold project ''[[Joe's Ultimate Bus Ride]]'' was because he felt he could not do any more original voices, so he ultimately decided to use his Joe voice from said game.<ref>"''When I was making Gotta Sweep, I was just like I can't do any more voices, so I'll just do the Joe voice.''" - {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|15:03|Livestream archive 15:03-15:10}}</ref>
* In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', Gotta Sweep's voice lines are louder compared to ''Baldi's Basics Plus''.

=== Other information ===
* An unrecorded playtesting session by [[Beta-tester#NintendoNaut|NintendoNaut]] exists, in which Gotta Sweep swept the majority of the characters, leading to NintendoNaut getting cornered by almost every character at once.<ref> "''My brother was playing it earlier, and, he... it was the craziest thing, like, I've ever seen Gotta Sweep do. I'm pretty sure he had, like, every single character at once. And like, sadly I wasn't able to record it, but... it was incredible.''" - mystman12, {{YouTube|2HP1NmoWMdw|8:48|Livestream archive 8:48-9:01}}</ref>
* Gotta Sweep's appearance in the farm field trip prior to V0.3 vaguely resembles a [[Wikipedia:Combine harvester|combine harvester]].
* If Gotta Sweep is pushed by a [[BSODA]], he can slightly clip through the wall in other locations, slowing down characters that pass by him.
* Gotta Sweep can immediately spawn in his closet even if the player is standing right on his spawning spot. The same goes for Dr. Reflex.
* [[File:Gotta Sweep ignores 'Nana Peel.gif|200px|thumb|Gotta Sweep sweeping through a 'Nana Peel.]]Gotta Sweep is the only grounded wandering character that is immune to the effect of the [['Nana Peel]], since he pushes it out of his hitbox with his sweeping.
{{clear}}

== Trivia == 
* Gotta Sweep and Baldi are mystman12's favorite characters.<ref>"''I think Baldi would have to be my favorite, since that’s a character I made up years ago, but I think besides that I would have to go with Gotta Sweep.''" - mystman12. August 3, 2018. [http://gamingtrend.com/feature/interviews/you-call-hamburgers-steamed-hams-baldis-basics-in-education-and-learning-creator-on-development-burgers INTERVIEWS You call hamburgers steamed hams — Baldi’s Basics in Education and Learning creator on development, burgers by Elisha Deogracias]</ref>
* During mystman12's livestream, Gotta Sweep's line, "''GOTTA GO FAST, FAST, FAST!''", is likely a reference to the theme song of the ''[[Wikipedia:Sonic X|Sonic X]]'' English dub called "Gotta Go Fast".<!---Move this trivia part to mystman12's Baldi's Basics livestream video page when it's published.--->
* mystman12 said that Gotta Sweep's greatest life achievement was cleaning the whole cafeteria floor until it was spotless in three minutes and eight seconds, one day after a food fight.<ref>"''One day after a food fight, Gotta Sweep cleaned entire the cafeteria floor in three minutes and eight seconds. It was spotless.''" - [[:File:Gmail response 20211007.png|Gmail]]</ref>
* The Libman company is aware that Gotta Sweep's design is one of their brooms.<ref>"''gotta sweep sweep sweep''" - Libman Company. June 19, 2018. [https://x.com/LibmanCompany/status/1009066275627257857 X/Twitter]</ref>
* Gotta Sweep's gender is stated to be male in a quote in the [[Cut character#Tutorial tape recorder|tape recorder]] in the earliest version.
** However, it is confirmed that Gotta Sweep has a gender according to the [[V0.1.2 (Plus)|V0.1.2]] update log of ''Baldi's Basics Plus''. Though, there is no in-game description about his gender like Chalkles.

'''1st Prize''' is a [[character]] in the ''[[Baldi's Basics]]'' series. He is the Science Fair's robot who roams the [[Hallway|halls]] of the [[Schoolhouse]] and the [[Super Schoolhouse]], acting as a significant obstacle for the [[player]].

== Description ==
1st Prize is a poorly 3D-modeled CGI robot with a teal "gym cage" as a body that has oval wheels, round hands each sporting five stubby fingers, and a large crimson/red heart connected to his head by wires inside his torso. His spherical head has a rectangular mouth and a cut out section revealing large googly eyes, which when viewed from anywhere from the front, are distorted and extremely long, with pupils jutting out further than the irises. 
According to his description, he was submitted to a [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Science%20fair science fair] and won, although it is not described who built him. It is possible his wires may be of very poor quality, as the store description of the [[Safety Scissors]] says that the scissors can only cut jump-ropes, bubble gum, and cheap wires.

== Personality ==
1st Prize is a robot who loves to hug people, showing that he is affectionate towards others. 1st Prize thinks that everyone is his bestie and is very loving considering he always asks to marry [[the player]]. He does not like it when he loses them. But, he turns very slowly compared to his fast speed, making it easy for [[the player]] to avoid him. He also seems to be unaware of potential hazards; an example is 1st Prize pushing the player straight into [[Baldi]], similar to [[Gotta Sweep]].

== Artificial intelligence ==
=== Main gameplay ===
[[File:1PR.png|thumb|1st Prize coming towards the player.]]
1st Prize's regularly wanders the hallways of the Super-Schoolhouse aimlessly, searching for the player. When 1st Prize notices the player, he'll turn around very slowly to face them and then accelerate forward, pushing the player down the [[hallway]] until they hit a wall or some other obstacle.

1st Prize has a similar function to Gotta Sweep, as they both push the player around the building. However, 1st Prize is always active and specifically targets the player, while Gotta Sweep is only active part of the time and follows his own path. Unlike Gotta Sweep, 1st Prize cannot push NPCs, so he cannot push them into the player and will instead go right through them.

[[File:1stPrizeBrokenWindow.png|thumb|A window broken by 1st Prize.]]
When 1st Prize collides with the surface while he is accelerating at fast speed, a ''*Bang!*'' sound will play, and it will attract Baldi and Dr. Reflex to the location where this sound is played, having a noise value of 64. If 1st Prize rushes toward any [[window]]s and collides with one, the window will break, and any character can pass through the smashed window. The sound of windows breaking has a very high noise value of 120, meaning that the only sounds that can overwrite it are [[Mrs. Pomp]]'s screaming, the [[Math Machine]]'s buzzing noise for when a question is answered incorrectly, and the alarm of [[Johnny's Store]] in Endless Mode. Because of this, Baldi and Dr. Reflex will almost always head in the direction of the broken window, unless they are attracted by other sounds that have higher [[Audio#Noise value|noise values]] or if they see the player.

If [[Techno-Boots]] are active, 1st Prize just passes through the player, instead of pushing them. If the player uses Safety Scissors on 1st Prize to cut his wires, he will slow down and spin around for 30 seconds (15 seconds in ''Classic''/''Birthday Bash'') before recovering. The time that 1st Prize spins out will be accumulated if the player uses another pair of Safety Scissors while 1st Prize is malfunctioning. It takes a total of 24 seconds for 1st Prize to make a full 360° turn, but while his wires are cut, he will spin faster than normal.

=== Field trips ===
[[File:Camping overview.png|thumb|1st Prize at the campsite.]]
[[File:Campfire Frenzy.png|thumb|1st Prize in the Campfire Frenzy minigame.]]
In the [[camping]] field trip, 1st Prize spawns in the campsite and the [[Campfire Frenzy]] minigame. In this minigame, the player is the one controlling 1st Prize and has to throw good fuel and avoid throwing the bad fuel into the fire. 1st Prize will also not push the player here.

{{clear}}
=== Other appearances ===
* [[File:1PR pushing in Classic.png|thumb|right|1st Prize pushing the player in ''Baldi's Basics Classic''.]]In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'', 1st Prize spawns in the dark hallway where the [[Mailbox Comic|mailbox comic]] is found. While 1st Prize pushes the player, their [[stamina]] will not refill, even if they do not press any buttons.
* [[File:SURPRISE.jpg|thumb|1st Prize appearing with the original cast for a birthday surprise.]]In ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash]]'', 1st Prize does not appear to wear any birthday-related accessories unlike most of the [[:Category:Schoolhouse characters|original characters]]. This is the first time that he makes a loud thud when colliding with a wall, but unlike in ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', this sound is also made when he collides with a wall at slow speed, and it does not attract Baldi. At the end of the game when the player reaches the last [[exit]] in the [[cafeteria]], 1st Prize will show up with the other characters to shout out a birthday surprise for them. Like in ''Classic'', the player's stamina will not be refilled while 1st Prize pushes them.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered]]'', 1st Prize has the same appearance in ''Baldi's Basics Classic/Birthday Bash'', but he can now break a window and properly make a ''*Bang!*'' sound when colliding with a wall at high speed, both of which alerting Baldi.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo]]'', 1st Prize can be found in the [[faculty room]] where the [[Grappling Hook]] is located. Although he can still be seen in multiple angles, he can never move as he remains in a deactivated state. He is also completely stationary, unable to be pushed by a BSODA spray.
{{clear}}
== Tips & Tricks ==
=== Advantages ===  
* 1st Prize is very useful for escaping from Baldi in long corridors and finding the exit at the end.
* 1st Prize can also disrupt [[Playtime]]'s minigame by pushing the player while they are playing jump rope.
* 1st Prize can break the windows of the Principal's office, which allows an endless means of escaping when caught by [[Principal of the Thing]].
* [[WD-NoSquee]] can be used on 1st Prize to prevent him from making a loud noise attracting Baldi to his location when he collides with a wall at a high speed.

=== Neutral === 
* While searching for [[Mrs. Pomp]]'s classroom to be there in time, 1st Prize is neutral: he can push the player to her classroom but can also push them away from it. Techno-Boots can prevent 1st Prize from pushing the player away from Mrs. Pomp's classroom, but it should not be used if 1st Prize is about to push the player into her classroom.
* 1st Prize can only push the player. Other characters cannot be pushed by him.
* 1st Prize turns around very slowly, which can either be good or bad:
** Good if 1st Prize is about to push the player into Baldi, as they can escape before 1st Prize finishes turning to push them.
** Bad if 1st Prize is about to push the player away from Baldi, especially when he becomes too fast to outrun, as Baldi can catch the player before 1st Prize finishes turning to push them.
* Using [[Safety Scissors]] on 1st Prize will make him unable to push the player. But it also means he can't help them against Baldi.
* 1st Prize will not go after the player if they are wearing the [[Faculty Nametag]].

=== Disadvantages ===
* 1st Prize can also be a hindrance, as he can smash the player up against a wall for a few moments, blocking their sight and ability to move.
* 1st Prize will make a loud noise when he smashes the player against the wall, even louder when he breaks a window, attracting Baldi and Dr. Reflex to that source of noise. The former can be prevented by using WD-NoSquee on 1st Prize.
* If Baldi is already behind the player while 1st Prize is facing towards them, 1st Prize will push the player into Baldi, resulting in the player losing a power tube or game over. However, this can be prevented if either the Safety Scissors, Techno-Boots, [[Dangerous Teleporter]], or [[An Apple for Baldi]] are used.
* During [[Dr. Reflex]]'s minigame, 1st Prize pushing the player will trigger Dr. Reflex's rage mode as it counts as skipping the minigame. Again, this can be prevented with Techno-Boots.

* In [[V0.3.6 (Plus)|V0.3.6]] and V0.3.7, teleporting the player to the outside happened much more commonly than previous versions. However, they were often teleported too far away from the building and this usually caused softlock because it took too long to get back to the map.

July 26, 2022. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1551939591959093250 X/Twitter]</ref>
* mystman12 has stated that he was considering adding this bug as an intentional feature.<ref>"''This is a bug, but I'm kind of tempted to make it a feature, haha. It would be cool if 1st Prize was hugging you so tightly that not even PotT could teleport you away!''" - [https://steamcommunity.com/app/1275890/discussions/0/5383446733853950692/?ctp=3#c5383446733864227339 ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' Steam Community Hub]</ref> However, this glitch was fixed eventually.

== Development ==
=== General ===
* 1st Prize is the only character that talks in text-to-speech. The text-to-speech voice for 1st Prize is from [[Wikipedia:Dr. Sbaitso|Dr. Sbaitso]] but slowed down.<ref>"''1st Prize's voice is from this old program:''" - mystman12. June 5, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1004178022893748225 X/Twitter]</ref>
** After accessing the voice parameters in Dr. Sbaitso with {{C|.param}} and pressing the Enter key, the parameters for 1st Prize's voice are:
*** General: {{C|0930}} and type {{C|say (quote)}}.
*** ''Birthday Bash'': {{C|0951}}. For the quote from the game itself, type {{C|say suuuerpreeize!}}.
** Dr. Sbaitso is also used for the countdown voice during [[test procedure]]. Because of this, the countdown voice and 1st Prize's voice sound very similar.
* 1st Prize pushing the player will make all NPCs' sounds nearby be heavily distorted due to the Doppler effect. In ''Classic Remastered'', it only applies to Arts and Crafters and Gotta Sweep.
* He is the only character from the main cast who was absent from most of the demos.
* While 1st Prize is in motion, his motoring sound will increase in pitch and it will stop until he hits a structure such as a wall or window, even if it does not play the banging-wall or window-crashing sounds.
* 1st Prize is actually hidden in the [[test room]] scene, but is disabled.
* 1st Prize is the first character whose name does not start with a letter.
** Instead, it starts with a number.
* 1st Prize was the first character to be implemented in an update.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Plus''/''Classic Remastered''/''Birthday Bash'' ===
* When sped up, 1st Prize's sound when colliding with a wall sounds like hitting a table.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Plus''/''Classic Remastered'' ===
* In the {{C|Subtitles_En.json}} file, there is an unused ''[SLAM!]'' (''*SLAM!'' in ''Classic Remastered'') caption, which is likely used when 1st Prize hits a wall at fast speed. The key value is {{C|Vfx_Prize_Bang}}.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' === 
* 1st Prize is one of the four characters to have a complete 360° view (meaning he can be seen in multiple angles). The second character is [[The Test]], but his head does not count. The third character is Dr. Reflex, although his reflex test sprites do not have multiple angles. The fourth character are the [[student]]s.
* If 1st Prize is moving towards the opposite direction of [[Cloudy Copter]]'s wind, he will just flicker rapidly, unless he is trying to push the player.
* If the player hides behind 1st Prize in the corner of a hallway, 1st Prize will say his voice lines while wandering.
* It is unknown how 1st Prize recovers from being cut by the Safety Scissors.
* Unlike in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', 1st Prize's rare voice lines are not that rare in ''Baldi's Basics Plus''.
* If 1st Prize is chasing the player and gets hit by Beans' gum, then he will be slowed down until he breaks free, going really fast and crashing into the player, with both going right into the [[Texture#Wall textures|wall]] right ahead of him, unless the player gets away from 1st Prize while he is stuck.
* In the early access trailer, 1st Prize is seen for the first time in ''Baldi's Basics Plus''. However, due to the new navigation system being non-physics based, getting his physics-based movement working correctly posed many challenges.<ref>"''Since this game's navigation system is completely non-physics based, getting First Prize's relatively physics based movement style working poses a lot of challenges.''" - [https://steamcommunity.com/app/1275890/discussions/0/2441461920662136883/#c2441461920662749269 ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' Steam Community Hub]</ref> 
** About a month later, [[mystman12]] stated that he got 1st Prize working again in a good way and planned to add him back in [[V0.2 (Plus)|V0.2]],<ref>"''I plan on adding him back in the game for 0.2, yes! I think I've finally come up with a good way to make him work well. As long as I don't hit any major snags, he should be in the game by then.''" - Micah McGonigal. July 17, 2020. [[:File:1PR's return.png|Gmail]]</ref> but there was a glitch where 1st Prize did not spawn in that version. This was fixed in [[V0.2.1 (Plus)|V0.2.1]].
* He is one of the five characters who can speak to not be voiced by mystman12, the others being [[Beans]], [[Chalkles]], [[Mrs. Pomp]], and [[Johnny]].
* [[File:1st Prize in the closed elevator.png|200px|thumb|1st Prize crashing into a closed elevator.]]1st Prize can crash into closed elevators.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash'' ===  
* From one end to the other, it takes 1st Prize exactly 8.12 seconds to go down the long hallway.
* The reason 1st Prize's wandering sounds are so rare is because of the fact 1st Prize only has a 1 in 9 chance (or an 11% chance) of saying an idle quote once he reaches his location without seeing the player, and the fact he moves very slowly while idle makes it even rarer.
* In 1st Prize's poster, the wall looks saturated.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' ===
* 1st Prize is the only Schoolhouse character to not debut in [[V1.0 (Classic)|V1.0]] of the game.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash'' ===
* 1st Prize's sprite seems to be strangely smaller than usual when appearing in the cafeteria for the birthday surprise. He keeps on facing the front as his sprite also rotates with the camera in said scene rather than moving in different angles.
* According to [[Basically Games]]'s reply via ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' [[V1.4.3 (Classic)|V1.4.3]] changelog post, 1st Prize's loud thud has not been added in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'', due to it being buggy in ''Birthday Bash'' and not working properly as it plays anytime when touching walls.<ref>"''Yeah, I considered adding it in, but I found it to be a bit too buggy in Birthday Bash and I'm not really sure how to get it to work right, so I decided not to add it to the original version.''" - Post by Basically Games in 1.4.3 Changelog comments{{Tt|*|This post was removed due to the comment amount. While is it possible to archive this post, due to the limit of itch.io post history and the link was not archived, this post currently cannot be provided.}}</ref>
** This sound was later reimplemented in ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' V0.3.5.
** 1st Prize does not attract Baldi when making his bang sound.

== Trivia ==
* Although 1st Prize is a robot and is called an "it" in his description, mystman12 refers to him as a male in some of his posts and livestreams.
* In Baldi's [[Valentine card]], 1st Prize's heart is seen.
* 1st Prize is the only schoolhouse character to debut in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' but not appear in the initial early-access release of ''Baldi's Basics Plus''.
* One of the Valentine's Day cards show 1st Prize having a large dent at the top of its heart.

{{Quote|Oh, hi! Welcome to my schoolhouse!|Baldi|sound=BAL_Hi.wav}}
The '''Schoolhouse''' is the general setting of ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'', ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash]]'' and ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered]]''. It is a smaller version of the [[Super Schoolhouse]] in ''[[Baldi's Basics Plus]]''.

== Description ==
The Schoolhouse is an elementary math school appearing as a simple rectangle-shaped building. Besides just [[Baldi]], only [[Playtime]], [[Arts and Crafters]], [[It's a Bully]], [[Principal of the Thing]], [[1st Prize]], [[Gotta Sweep]], and [[Null]] reside in the Schoolhouse.
{{clear}}

=== [[:Category:Common locations|Common locations]] ===

| Locations appearing as a direct way out of the Schoolhouse. Becomes available as a final objective before completing the game.

|Completely restricted rooms with no students allowed to enter. Principal of the Thing will scold [[the player]] when he sees them entering, exiting, or staying inside a faculty room.
|-
|[[Hallway]]
|[[File:Hallway ClassicRemastered.png|200px|center]]
|One of the most common locations in the Schoolhouse. Some halls include standard [[door]]s and [[swinging door]]s to specific rooms. The [[Quarter]]s can be randomly found in the halls.

== Development ==
=== General ===
* After collecting 7 notebooks in [[Story Mode]], when the player walks up to a fake exit, an image of a map<!--Not the same as the one in Baldi's Basics Plus.--> showing where the exit is will appear on the wall.
* [[File:Outside of the Schoolhouse.png|200px|thumb|The Schoolhouse's map from outside while cheating in V1.3.2.]]If the player were to view the Schoolhouse from the outdoor areas (except the one with [[Baldi's Bus]] in ''Classic Remastered''), the player can see through the [[Texture#Wall textures|walls]]. This is because the Schoolhouse is created out of walls that only have one side.
* According to It's a Bully's poster description in the Principal's office and [[Bladder]]'s second chalkboard, the Schoolhouse is named as "here school". 
** The first and second words do not start with capital letters. Though, in some online ''Baldi's Basics'' [[merchandise]] descriptions and [[mystman12]]'s [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Vbb0uZJcBlo&lc=UgwZVemPxvFTS5UvPn14AaABAg comment on one of SilvaGunner's rips], both words are capitalized.
* mystman12 tried to make the classic Schoolhouse map in ''[[Wikipedia:Minecraft|Minecraft]]'', but it is not finished yet.<ref>"''I actually tried recreating the original Baldi's Basics map in Minecraft once, but I didn't even get close to finishing it haha.''" - [[:File:Gmail response 20230808.webp|Gmail]]</ref>
** But for now, it is unknown what happened to the map.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic''/''Birthday Bash'' ===
* [[File:3D Schoolhouse.png|200px|thumb|A full map in the 3D model program showing the invisible Arts and Crafters triggers.]]When looking up the map's 3D model, there seems to be six hidden empty rooms in the map: two rooms between three hallways connected to the bottom right corner outside of the cafeteria, one around Gotta Sweep's closet, one from the hallway of the map's left-hand side where two connected faculty rooms are at, and two others from the hallway between where the [[pay phone]] is found. However, they are not actually rooms but instead the triggers for Arts and Crafters. What this means is that these triggers/boxes allow Arts and Crafters to show up in locations on the map.<ref>"''So those rooms aren't actually rooms at all, they're actually just pieces of the triggers used to control Arts and Crafters. There are actually eight triggers total and when you enter one Arts and Crafters will head to a predetermined location based on the trigger you entered.''" - {{YouTube|CSM2vX2uFGU|1:42|Baldi's Basics Classic LOST BITS (ft. The Developer!) <nowiki>| Unused Content [TetraBitGaming]</nowiki> 1:42~1:57}}</ref> 
** The reason why they show up is that they are actually collision boxes and are a part of the map, only that the renderer (the tool that allows boxes to be visible) is off, although the collision still exists.

=== ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' ===
* The brightness of the Schoolhouse changes throughout [[Baldi's Basics Classic/Versions|versions]].

'''Classrooms''' are rooms where [[the player]] can find [[notebook]]s in the ''[[Baldi's Basics]]'' series.

== Description ==
<gallery position="center">
ActivitySign Notebook.png|Activity-less notebooks
ActivitySign MathMachine.png|Math Machine
ActivitySign BalloonBuster.png|Balloon Buster
ActivitySign MatchMachine.png|Match Machine
ActivitySign BonusQuestion.png|Math Machines with a bonus question
</gallery>
Classrooms are rooms placed around the [[Schoolhouse]] and [[Super Schoolhouse]]. Every classroom has a teacher's desk, wooden seats, blue carpet flooring, a wall with molding, a [[chalkboard]] with something written on it, possible [[item]]s on some of the [[Model#Furniture|tables]], possible [[Sprite#Room materials|papers and uncollectible notebook]] objects, and a notebook on the big desk the player can collect. All classrooms have at least one [[Door#Class doors|class door]] with the number [[99]] on them, and in some cases, specific classrooms can have even more than just one door.

On [[Floor 2]] onwards, all classrooms will contain an [[educational activity]], consisting of a machine and its corresponding [[balloon]]s, instead of a stray notebook and a teacher's desk. The player must solve the activity correctly to get a notebook this way. The player can still get a notebook by failing to solve the activity, but it causes [[Baldi]] to get faster than usual. Each classroom has a sign outside of each door indicating what activities lie in each room.

In activity-less classrooms, there is always a [[water fountain]]. This does not apply to [[challenge]]s and the [[Tutorial]].

In challenges, classrooms have plain wall textures.

Every classroom (rarely in challenges and the Tutorial) has a sign outside of it to indicate what educational activity in there:
{| class="article-table" cellspacing="1" cellpadding="1" border="1"
!Activity
!Sign (Left)
!Sign (Right)
|-
|Activity-less notebooks
|[[File:ActivityExteriorSign NoActivity Left.png]]
|[[File:ActivityExteriorSign NoActivity Right.png]]
|-
|[[Math Machine]]
|[[File:ActivityExteriorSign MathMachine Left.png]]
|[[File:ActivityExteriorSign MathMachine Right.png]]
|-
|[[Balloon Buster]]
|[[File:ActivityExteriorSign BalloonBuster Left.png]]
|[[File:ActivityExteriorSign BalloonBuster Right.png]]
|-
|[[Match Machine]]
|[[File:ActivityExteriorSign MatchMachine Left.png]]
|[[File:ActivityExteriorSign MatchMachine Right.png]]
|}

== Overview ==
=== Main gameplay ===
A different amount of classrooms will generate depending on the [[floor]]: 
* [[Floor 1]]: 4 classrooms
* Floor 2 and [[Floor 4]]: 7 classrooms
* [[Floor 3]] and [[Floor 5]]: 9 classrooms
Occasionally, the player will be chased into a room by Baldi but may escape by mimicking the opposite of his movement path, effectively running around him to escape. This will set the player in close quarters with Baldi for a time, making them vulnerable until breaking out of his range of chase. Another way to escape this situation is answering the math problem correctly on Baldi's Math Machine if the classroom has one, which results in Baldi being delighted for five seconds, allowing the player to escape easily.

[[Principal of the Thing]] will occasionally survey classrooms on patrol for suspicious activity. However, he will not pose a threat to the player unless they are breaking the [[school rule]]s.

[[It's a Bully]] will sometimes block a classroom from being entered, depending on the [[tile]] of any [[hallway]] he is on.

[[Chalkles]] will spawn in some classrooms, slowly starting from the chalkboard. Once Chalkles becomes fully solid, he will fly out of the chalkboard, laughing to get Baldi's attention and locking the door(s) for 15 seconds.

[[Mrs. Pomp]] will invite the player to a random classroom once she spots them. The [[map]] shows her classroom until the player enters the room or the [[timer]] runs out.

Some classrooms can also contain two doors which can lead to hallways, another classroom, or other [[location]]s.

On Floor 2 onwards, there may be some unpowered classrooms, which do not have any power to them. Unpowered classrooms appear on the map in gray. The main difference between unpowered classrooms and regular classrooms is that they require a [[lever]] to be switched on to use the educational activity inside the classroom, since they are disabled when there is no power to them. When there is no power given to the room, the lights will either be slightly dimmed or completely dark, though the lights will be fully lit up once there is power given to the classroom.

* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'', the classrooms also contain pencils and notes. There are seven classrooms. The items' locations are listed as follows:
** '''"[[Math]]" classroom:''' None

** '''"Geography" classroom:''' None

** '''"Science" classroom:''' Safety Scissors
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash]]'', there are the same classrooms in ''Classic'' and two that are a part of the [[glitched school]].
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered]]'', there are the same classrooms in ''Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash'', but with different chalkboard writings, random-colored notebooks. The room materials in the "Math" classroom have been removed. In [[Demo Style]], the desks are replaced with Baldi's Math Machines and items are randomized.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Full Game Early Demo]]'', there are eight classrooms in total. The items' locations are listed as follows:

== Development ==
* Baldi, Principal of the Thing, students, Mrs. Pomp, [[Dr. Reflex]], and Arts and Crafters are the only characters that can enter classrooms while passive.
* This is the only location that uses more than one color on the map.
* The furniture in ''Baldi's Basics Plus'' and ''Classic Remastered'' is much smaller, and the distance between them is shorter than other games.
* In classic Schoolhouse, the "Geology" classroom has way more desks than the other classrooms.
* The signs above the classrooms may be inspired by ''Sonic's Schoolhouse'', with that game having wooden signs above the classrooms similar to the ones in ''Baldi's Basics Plus''.

'''Hallways''' are long rectangular halls that connect [[Room|rooms]] to each other in the ''[[Baldi's Basics]]'' series.

== Description ==
Each room is connected to a hallway by a [[door]]. Faculty doors lead to [[faculty room]]s, class doors lead to [[classroom]]s, a yellow door with the word "Principal" written on it leads to the [[Principal's office]], a orange door with the word "supplies" written on it leads to [[Gotta Sweep's closet]], a door with a red letter H on it leads to [[Dr. Reflex's clinic]], and all doors that lead to hallways use the class door texture, resulting in some doors having different textures on each side. [[Swinging door]]s can lead to most of [[:Category:Special locations|special locations]] or to each other hallways by, which can be locked with a [[Swinging Door Lock]], to prevent any entity from moving through it, including [[the player]].

Some hallways have [[locker]]s (coming in different colors) and various [[poster]]s on their walls. On [[Floor 1]], some hallways contain some [[Sprite#Room decorations|potted plants]].

Hallways are also generated with various [[texture]]s (except for [[challenge]]s and [[Endless Mode|Endless]] medium).

== Overview ==
=== Main gameplay ===
Entities can move freely in this space. The player will spend most of their time in the hallways, moving from room to room.

Sometimes, a hallway will be blocked by [[It's a Bully]], who will only move if the player gives him an [[item]] from their [[inventory]], [[Principal of the Thing]] catches him and puts him in detention, he gets sucked into a whirlpool, or if he remains in the same spot for more than 3 minutes.

[[Gotta Sweep]] will occasionally pass through the hallways, pushing along any character except for It's a Bully along his path. [[1st Prize]] will push the player down quickly if he contacts with them.

Some hallways can also be generated multiple [[tile]]s wide; these are known as areas. Tiles in areas not separately revealed, revealing full area.

=== Other appearances ===
* [[File:Dark hallway.png|thumb|The dark hallway in ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash''.]] In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic]]'' and ''[[Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash]]'', the jointed hallway by the Principal's office appears to be darker than the rest of the hallways. A [[Quarter]] can also be found randomly in some of the halls. One of the lights in the dark hallways is still working and is buzzing.<ref>"''The lights are out and the one that's still working is buzzing.''" - mystman12. November 30, 2018. [https://x.com/mystman12/status/1068544046040317952 Twitter/X]</ref> One of the halls also has some [[Sprite#Room decorations|plants]].
* In ''Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash'', a [[present]] is found randomly around the halls instead of Quarters.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics Classic Remastered]]'', there are the same hallways in ''Classic'' and ''Birthday Bash''. However, there is no broken lights in any hallways and a Quarter is randomly found in a hallway in all styles, even in Party Style.
* In ''[[Baldi's Basics - Field Trip demo: Camping]]'', the hallways are where the player starts.
* In the [[:Category:Games|games]] and [[:Category:Demos|demos]] prior to ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', the only available hallways have a ceiling in gray with black with celling lights and white dots tiles, a yellow tile floor, and a white brick wall.

== Types ==
There are several different types of hallways.
<tabber>Regular hallway=
'''Regular hallways''' are extremely common and can vary in length. They are always 1 tile thick.
<gallery position="center">
Hallway.png|Example of a regular hallway.
HallwayReg.png|Ditto, but seen on the [[map]].
</gallery>
|-|Corner=
'''Corners''' connect the ends of two perpendicular hallways. In ''Baldi's Basics Classic'' and ''Baldi's Basics Birthday Bash'', walking into a corner results in Baldi unable to catch the player.
<gallery position="center">
CornerPlus.png|Example of a corner.
HallTurn.png|Ditto, but seen on the map.
</gallery>
|-|Area=
An '''area''' is a special, rarer type of hallway that is more than 1 tile thick. Walking into an area will result in the whole area being highlighted instead of just the individual tiles. Since [[V0.4 (Plus)|V0.4]], there is a chance that pre-made areas can be generated, such as an area with a pillar in the middle or two long areas joined by a wall with an opening. This does not feature in the [[Schoolhouse|classic Schoolhouse]].
<br>
The following pre-made areas can be seen in both Floor 2 and 3.
<gallery position="center">
Area V0.4~.jpg|Example of an area.
Area V0.4~ Map.jpg|Ditto, but seen on the map.
Area Pillar Middle.jpg|An area with a pillar in the middle.
Pillar Area Map 2.png|Ditto, but seen on the map.
Double-area hallway.jpg|An area consisting of two long areas bisected by a wall with an opening.
Double-area hallway map.jpg|Ditto, but seen on the map.
Zigzag hallway.jpg|An area of a zigzag-shaped hallway.
Zigzag hallway map.jpg|Ditto, but seen on the map.
</gallery>
|-|Dead-end=
'''Dead-ends''' are the rarest type of hallway containing only one way in and out.
<gallery position="center">
DeadEndPlus.png|Example of a dead-end.
HallDeadEnd.png|Ditto, but seen on the map.
</gallery>
</tabber>

== Development ==
* The hallway, which was the starting point with three connected halls, was the first location ever to be implemented. 
* In ''Baldi's Basics Plus'', only hallways are affected by the alternate textures.

== Trivia ==
* All of Principal of the Thing's accusatory sentences involve him saying that the action is occurring "in the halls", even if the player is not in the hallways.
